# Bokito Platform – Product Knowledge Base

> **Levend document.** Elke keer dat nieuwe informatie wordt geleerd over hoe het Bokito platform werkt — qua features, workflows, SOPs, configuraties of business rules — wordt dit bestand bijgewerkt.
>
> **English company handbook:** structured docs for onboarding and ops live in [`docs/company/README.md`](docs/company/README.md).

### Eén generatie (2026-10-01)

Er is één product: `apps/api`, `apps/dashboard`, `apps/mobile`. De V2-herbouw van 30 september is op 1 oktober 2026 verwijderd (zie sectie 42). Richting: `docs/STRATEGY_2026-09.md` en `docs/POSITIONING.md`, toegepast op het bestaande platform.

### Attention path + Overview exception cockpit (2026-10)

- **For you is the default work queue:** Communication rail, last-inbox fallback, onboarding/tour finish, and home landing use `/communication/inbox/for_you` (not Open). Empty For you offers Connect a channel / Start a chat / Open inbox.
- **Bell mirrors For you:** badge is `max(forYouCount, unreadNotifications)`; dropdown always shows an **Open For you** row; empty copy points at For you + channels. System notices stay in the list under the bell.
- **`new-message` defaults on** (in-app + push) for conversations you or your team own (`notify_new_inbound`). Prefs schema `version` 2 upgrades legacy stored-off rows once; after version 2 an intentional off stays off.
- **Overview:** **Needs you** is full-width first (up to 8 threads) with empty CTAs; AI activity and the three context blocks (tickets / running / trajectory) sit below under “More workspace context”.
- **Overview outcomes (2026-10):** AI activity also shows **Time saved** (`time_saved_minutes_week`, credit matrix in `time_saved.py`) and **Resolved** (`resolved_conversations_week`: external, closed, no `handoff_requested` / `escalated` / `operator_takeover`, `updated_at` between now−7d−72h and now−72h). Open external threads with no message for 14 days appear as **Quiet for 14 days** / **Al 14 dagen stil** with **Close** (`quiet_threads` on `cockpit_summary`, close via bulk `close`).
- **Time saved credits (2026-10):** Matrix credits autonomous sends (12), draft send/approve unchanged (10), edited draft (6), draft then human continued via `human_replied` / `superseded_by_external_reply` / `handled_externally` (7), `no_reply_noted` triage (2), approved **No reply needed** close (5), `category_set` by `agent` or `triage` (5), completed flow runs (8). Skips dismissed / stale inbound supersedes. Open Assisted backlog is not an outcome credit.
- **Handoff in the list (2026-10):** `handoff_requested` and `escalated` sort first (`human_first`), show badge **Asked for a person** / **Vroeg om een medewerker** and a thread banner, and match **For you** even when the thread sits with All people.
- **File-as-tag card (2026-10):** a certain read (`certainty >= threshold * 10`) files only when the action tag has `ai_auto_tag`. Otherwise `interpretation._propose_file_tag` raises one in-thread decision **File as #name** / **Dien in als #name** (`action_type=file_ticket`). Deduped per signal and title for 7 days (awaiting, or rejected/deferred). Approving files the ticket; **Not now** defers.
- **Close settles the ticket (2026-10):** `settle_ticket_on_close` moves an open ticket to its done stage when the conversation closes (operator, bulk, send-and-close, handled externally, inbox rule, automated mail, agent tool). Missing required fields leave the ticket in place. A done stage with `auto_close_conversation` does not close a conversation that is already closed.
- **Email composer (2026-10):** email opens on Reply when the last message is inbound and no decision is open. Ask stays when a decision is open or the last message was outbound. Note stays for a non-receiving address.
- **Assisted draft cloud (2026-10):** open reply proposals render as **Suggested a reply** / **Antwoord voorgesteld** (expand to read + **Use draft** / **Overnemen**). The composer auto-fills only when the agent finishes while you stay on that thread; after another chat, take over from the pill. `split_suggestion` note-only output (`INTERNAL_NOTE:` with no customer body) never becomes a reply proposal — it is stored as an internal note (`reason: note_only`).
- **Overview action mix (2026-10):** AI activity shows a donut of weighted **autonomous / assisted / manual** actions (`cockpit.summary.action_mix`). Weights are minutes per action key in `platform_settings.time_saved_action_minutes`, edited on staff **Ops** (`GET/PUT /api/staff/ops/time-saved-weights`). Manual human outbound replies count in the pie only; they do not add to time saved.
- **Draft edits as examples (2026-10):** approving a changed draft writes `Feedback` `subject_type=draft_edit` (customer message, sent text, category; no thumbs-down sentiment). `build_workspace_context` injects the last 3 as **Recent corrections**.
- **Widget email (2026-10):** `pre_chat_form` defaults to true when the setting key is absent. The form asks once; Skip is remembered; submit requires a valid email. `link_visitor_email` links on identify when a conversation exists, and `livechat_stream` links the stored email when the Signal is created. The in-app personal assistant still forces the form off.
- **Assisted progress (2026-10):** channel `GET/PUT /api/ai-handling/channel/{id}` includes `promotion` (`drafts_resolved`, `unedited`, `target_drafts` 50, `target_rate` 0.8). The channel row shows “N of 50 drafts sent unchanged” while the channel is Assisted.
- **Operator loop surfacing (2026-10):** Usage Outcomes also show avg feedback, CSAT, and open decisions. Thread side panel shows `aiSummary`. Closed threads can **Use as example** (`POST /signals/{id}/example`, `is_example` on serialize). Pending/snoozed threads reopen from the header. Deferred sibling drafts link **Open active** via `superseded_by_signal_id`. List rows show triage category + certainty ≥80%. Overview shows a dismissible promo banner (illustration, title, short text, **Taak instellen**) while the workspace has no interval or cron trigger. The CTA opens Agenda `?new=interval&seed=watch`, which is the normal trigger dialog prefilled with a daily interval, name and prompt. Dismiss is `localStorage` key `bokito-recurring-wake-dismissed:{workspaceId}`. The seeded heartbeat stays a separate setup-guide path. Communication banners when `!sendReady` and when `noReplySuggestions > 0` (dismiss via existing dismiss endpoint). `@` agent mentions rank by `listThreadAgentCandidates`.

### Shell + Communication UX + agent marks (2026-10-01)

- **Shell:** denser tokens/recipes, shared `NavRow`/`NavFolder`, `SidebarWorkspaceSwitcher` + `SidebarUserMenu`, compact Card/Button/Badge. Page guides are a compact help icon next to the topbar title (`pageGuideForPath`: Communication, Overview, Agenda, Projects) rather than a duplicate in-page heading. Overview’s in-page title is the workspace name; Projects uses `{workspace} projects`; Agenda drops the second title/subtitle and puts Today, range, view switcher and New on one toolbar.
- **Communication:** simplified list rows (no `ThreadIndicatorMenu`); filters include **Your turn** / **Jij aan zet**; follow-up / meta live under **This conversation** / **Dit gesprek**; redundant composer **Ask assistant** removed. Thread timeline must be `h-full` under a sized flex parent so Virtuoso does not collapse to empty.
- **Agent marks:** `AiAvatar` always uses platform AI violet (`--color-ai` tokens). Operators pick **name** and **icon** (or initials) only — no per-agent color. API `avatar_payload` always returns `#7c3aed`; legacy stored colors are ignored. Role defaults: Front desk = headset, orchestrator/PO = briefcase, Bokito helper = sparkles.

### Repo-scope (bokitoAiV2)

Deze repository bevat `apps/dashboard` (portal), `apps/chat-widget`, `apps/api` (FastAPI backend), `apps/mobile` (Expo), en `packages/shared`. Lokale dev: `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up`, daarna `uvicorn` in `apps/api` en `npm run dev` in `apps/dashboard`. Zie `README.md` en `docs/architecture.md`.

**Stack:** FastAPI + SQLModel + Postgres/pgvector + Redis/arq. Dashboard in bokito mode (`VITE_API_MODE=bokito`) proxied `/api/*` naar de API. Geen externe BaaS-backend meer in deze repo.

**Mock AI / Autonoom gate (2026-09 Wave 2):** Without a live chat key (`resolve_model_call` → `live=false`), the workspace shows a shell banner and mock replies are stamped `is_mock` / `llm_mode=mock`. Timeline + message DTOs expose `is_mock` and `delivered_to_customer`; mock/placeholder bodies never get **Sent to the customer**. Auto-mode inbound replies skip external delivery when mock. Govern **Autonomous** requires `prerequisites.llm_live` and `prerequisites.send_ready` (API 400 `autonomous_prerequisites` otherwise). Reply language Auto still mirrors the customer; when unclear, prompts fall back to the workspace language (NL when UI/workspace default is Dutch). Mock LLM emits Dutch placeholders when instructions or the customer message look Dutch. Runtime probe: `GET /api/settings/models/runtime`.

**Deploy:** één Hostinger VPS (`31.97.45.44`) draait de hele stack via Docker Compose + Caddy (`docker-compose.prod.yml`: postgres/pgvector, redis, api, worker, web). Live op `bokito.ai` / `app.bokito.ai` / `api.bokito.ai` (Cloudflare proxied A-records → VPS). Details + cutover: sectie 17. Widget bundle: `/chat-widget/bokito-chat.js` (één bundle; `data-auth-mode` bepaalt visitor vs logged-in). Livechat API: `/api/livechat/*`.

**Tenant routing:** alle hosts (`bokito.ai`, `app.bokito.ai`, `api.bokito.ai`, `*.bokito.ai`) gaan rechtstreeks naar de VPS-Caddy → FastAPI; de oude Cloudflare Workers (`bokito-tenant-router`, `bokito-app-passthrough`) zijn verwijderd. Control-plane = `app.bokito.ai`; cross-host session-handoff via URL-fragment `__bokito_at__=<accessToken>` (`lib/host-routing.ts`, host-driven via `VITE_APP_CONTROL_PLANE_HOST`/`VITE_TENANT_ROOT_DOMAIN`).

**Releaseversie:** `VERSION` in de repo (start `1.2.01`). Overview en het accountmenu tonen `environment · tenant-slug · Live · versie`. Login toont `build: <versie>`. Productie en staging bakken dat bestand in `VITE_APP_VERSION`. Na een geslaagde productie-deploy schrijft de workflow de volgende patch (`+0.0.01`) terug naar `VERSION` en naar `/opt/bokito-dev/VERSION`, zodat de dev-host één patch voorloopt. Een grotere stap zet je zelf in `VERSION` (bijvoorbeeld `1.3.00`) vóór de deploy; daarna loopt dev weer één patch voor. `scripts/bump_release_version.py`.

---

## 1. Platform Overzicht

Bokito is een AI-platform waarmee bedrijven (voornamelijk SMBs) AI-agents kunnen inzetten voor dagelijkse operaties. Het platform bestaat uit vier applicaties:

| App | Type | Primaire gebruiker |
|---|---|---|
| **Dashboard (portal)** | React webapp | Admin / operations manager |
| **Mobiele app** | Expo (React Native) | Eindgebruiker / medewerker |
| **Chat widget** | TypeScript + Vite IIFE embed | Websitebezoeker / klant |
| **Website** | Statische marketing site | Potentiële klant |

Op de marketing website (`apps/website`) tonen de hoofdnavigatie (Navbar) tijdelijk geen links naar `/pricing` en `/kennisbank`; die pagina’s blijven bereikbaar via directe URL.

Tech stack: React + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind (`apps/dashboard`). Backend: FastAPI (legacy portal APIs) plus **Bokito AI OS** Python/FastAPI backend (`apps/api`) for V1 migration. The former `apps/messenger` PWA and shared `@bokito/messenger-ui` package are deleted (2026-07); the embeddable widget is `apps/chat-widget`.

### Bokito AI OS (V1, 2026-06)

**Product north star (why and how to align features):** [`docs/CORE_INTENT.md`](docs/CORE_INTENT.md). **Market positioning:** [`docs/POSITIONING.md`](docs/POSITIONING.md) — one-liner: *Drive the company from the conversation.*

### Operator vocabulary (Set C)

| Operator word (EN / NL) | Internal table | Role |
|---|---|---|
| Conversation / Gesprek | `signals` | The chat thread |
| Hashtag | `signal_tags` | Free label on a conversation; with a playbook it is a category |
| Action tag / Actietag | `signal_tags.workstream_id` | Hashtag that starts a ticket flow (accent `#`); filing it makes the conversation a ticket. Older docs may say Category |
| Flow | `workstreams` | Stage pipeline for an action tag; UI title is always `#hashtag` (no independent playbook name) |
| Project | `projects` | Shows its playbooks as stacked ticket boards |
| Contact / Agent / Decision | `contacts` / `agents` / `decision_requests` | Party and human gate |

Overview, Govern, Agents stay English loanwords in Dutch UI. Rail hub: Communication / Communicatie.

### Agent shape and selection

- Every Agent uses one operator shape: name, purpose (stored as the system prompt), model, tool allowlist, owner, and optional default channels or Signal types.
- **Agent.audience removed (2026-10):** `agents.audience` (`customers|partners|internal`) is gone (Alembic `070_drop_agent_audience`). Cards no longer show Klanten/Intern. Front desk is identified by slug `front-desk`.
- Bokito is a platform-owned Agent row with `acts_for_user=true`.
- Each workspace is seeded with Bokito and a **Front desk** agent (`slug=front-desk`).
- Website chat (`ChannelAccount` channel `widget`) defaults to Front desk (`tenant_bootstrap.ensure_widget_default_agent`); startup backfill repairs unset, inactive, or Bokito bindings. Widget Look settings write `ChannelAccount.default_agent_id` (not legacy `ChannelBinding`).
- **Inbound AI agent (2026-10):** automatic inbound interpretation and reply use `resolve_inbound_agent_for_signal` — thread pin (`Signal.agent_id`) or the channel's explicit `default_agent_id` only. No lead / Front desk fallback on the worker path (`skipped: no_channel_agent`). Manual "Bring in an agent" still uses `resolve_agent_for_channel` (default, then lead / any company agent). Interpretation is no longer a separate platform LLM: the channel agent calls `record_thread_read` in the same `AgentLoop` as the reply. Assisted inbound files the outcome with a tool (`suggest_thread_reply`, `note_no_reply`, or `create_decision_request`) instead of a worker-wrapped final line; autonomous still sends the final text and may use more tools. Timeline agent events sit next to that agent's avatar. `signal_created` renders first as "Gesprek gestart door {naam}". The generic `agent_processed` pill is hidden. **Manual** AI handling still runs a read-only agent turn (summary/priority/tags/ticket/contact; no draft). Side panel Sparkles triage scores are removed; the timeline `triaged` event shows the summary (BookOpen icon). `POST /api/signals/{id}/triage` re-queues inbound processing.
- A conversation's pinned `Signal.agent_id` selects its agent first for inbound. Otherwise the connected `ChannelAccount.default_agent_id` is required for auto AI. `ChannelBinding`, lead, parent, and role hierarchy fields are legacy compatibility data and do not drive inbound auto-run.
- Customer human-ask / operator takeover sets the conversation's AI handling to `manual` with a reason (`handoff_requested`, `operator_takeover`, `escalated`, `assigned`); handoff category notifications are NL/EN via workspace language (prefs row `handoff`). Agents cannot silently resume a held conversation; only a person hands it back.

### AI handling (AI-afhandeling)

- **One setting, three modes:** `autonomous` (AI replies and acts on its own; consequential tools still ask), `assisted` (AI drafts replies and proposes actions), `manual` (no customer draft/send; linked channel agent may still read via `record_thread_read` and file tags/tickets). Icons: Zap (autonomous) and PenLine (assisted) in purple, Hand (manual) in gray — `components/ai/AiHandlingIcon.tsx`, catalog `lib/ai-handling.ts`. Replaces channel-type `ai_mode` (suggest/auto/off), mailbox `ai_config.mode`, `Signal.ai_paused` and `inbox.autonomous_reply`; legacy values map suggest→assisted, auto→autonomous, off→manual.
- **Layers (most specific non-null wins):** workspace `Tenant.settings_json.ai_handling.mode` (default `assisted`) → channel `ChannelAccount.settings_json.ai_config.ai_handling.mode` → `Contact.ai_handling` → `Signal.ai_handling` (+ `ai_handling_reason`). Service: `app/services/ai_handling.py` (`resolve_ai_handling`, `resolve_for_signal`).
- **Ceiling (applied after resolution):** privacy "AI may use message bodies" off → manual; Govern `messaging` allowance deny/ask/allow → manual/assisted/autonomous; a tripped channel breaker → assisted. The payload reports `effective`, `requested`, `source`, `ceiling`, `clamped_by` (`privacy|govern|breaker`), `reason`, `until_close`, `inherited*` and `own`.
- **Conversation overrides are temporary:** closing clears them; assigning a person sets manual (reason `assigned`); unassign/hand back clears. A human reply only takes over (sets manual + assigns) when the conversation is autonomous.
- **Permissions:** lowering is free for any member; raising anything to autonomous requires owner/admin (403 otherwise). The UI previews followers (open conversations that inherit the change) and evidence (unedited-draft rate) before an autonomous raise.
- **API:** `GET/PUT /api/ai-handling/{scope}/{id}` (scope workspace|channel|contact|conversation; workspace id `current`; body `{mode|null, reason?, assign_to_me?}`), `GET /api/ai-handling/{scope}/{id}/preview`, `GET /api/ai-handling` (workspace overview: settings + exceptions), `PUT /api/ai-handling/settings` (safeguards, disclosure, breaker), `GET /api/ai-handling/metrics?days=30`, `POST /api/ai-handling/channel/{id}/reset-breaker`. Thread, contact and channel payloads carry an `ai_handling` block. The `/signals/{id}/takeover|release` endpoints and ai-modes endpoints are removed.
- **Per-run safeguards (autonomous only, `apply_safeguards`):** below the certainty threshold (default 7), pending contacts (not on website chat) when "Review replies to new contacts" is on, and categories with `send_mode=draft` turn that single reply into a draft. Each writes an `ai_handling_downgraded` event (`low_certainty|new_contact|category`).
- **Circuit breaker:** defaults 30 autonomous replies or 3 negative signals per channel per hour; tripping writes `ai_breaker_tripped`, caps the channel at assisted, and stays until someone resumes it (channel picker or Settings → AI handling Exceptions).
- **Disclosure:** on by default; autonomous replies carry a short "written by AI" line (custom text up to 200 chars). Stored on `SignalMessage.metadata_json.ai_disclosure`; the widget renders it under the bubble.
- **Events:** `ai_handling_changed` `{from,to,scope,reason,via}`, `ai_handling_downgraded`, `ai_breaker_tripped`, `agent_assigned`; the timeline renders them as pills with the mode icon.
- **Surfaces:** thread header picker (`thread-ai-handling`), list-row icon when a conversation/contact differs, contact panel + contact page row and list filter (`ai_handling` param, `custom` = any own value), channel row picker, Settings → AI handling (workspace cards, ceiling/pause via messaging ask/allow, exceptions with breaker resume, safeguards + breaker limits, action tags that always need review), Govern → Policy (workspace posture ceiling, tool allowances, scopes per action tag/flow — not a second reply-mode picker), Overview metrics block, composer verbs `/manual` `/assisted` `/autonomous` (NL `/handmatig` `/geassisteerd` `/autonoom`), agent tools `set_ai_handling` / `set_channel_ai_handling` (Govern-gated), mobile thread banner (PUT conversation endpoint).
- **Govern vs AI handling split (2026-10):** Govern owns the workspace autonomy ceiling + Ledger; AI handling owns conversation reply modes, pause/resume of autonomous replies, circuit breaker, and per-action-tag always-review. NL Govern posture label is **Geassisteerd** (not Ondersteund).
- **Default badge in pickers (2026-10):** settings dropdowns mark the inherited option with an inline `DefaultBadge` pill (`components/ui/DefaultBadge.tsx`) instead of a separate "Follow ..." item. AI handling menus show e.g. "Geassisteerd · Bedrijfsstandaard" (badge per source: Platform/Bedrijfs/Kanaal/Contact/Gespreksstandaard; EN Platform/Company/Channel/Contact/Conversation default); picking the badged mode sends `mode: null` and clears the override. The channel handling-agent select shows the default agent name with a "Standaard" badge.
- **Learning hook:** when at least 80% of 50+ assisted drafts in the window go out unedited, learning creates a Govern proposal to raise the channel to autonomous; a person accepts it. The channel AI-handling row shows the same counts (`promotion` on the channel payload) before that proposal exists.
- Autonomy has three layers: workspace posture ceiling, a stricter per-Signal-type or Playbook policy, and the Agent tool allowlist. The dashboard does not expose per-tool autonomy overrides.

The primary rail keeps page routes but uses Set C labels: **Overview** and **Communication** are pinned above groups (no Control/Besturing section). **Work** holds Projects, Playbooks and Agenda; **AI** holds Agents and Knowledge. Cases and Activity are not primary-rail items. Installed modules remain cards under Connections and never add rail items. From any thread, `Ctrl+K` / `Cmd+K` offers explicit actions to open an agent, playbook, project, or Knowledge.

### Context rail (Who / This conversation)

The thread right panel splits into **Who** (contact or agent card) and **This conversation** (signals on the thread). Manual filing uses one control: **Add what we missed** (type picker). Tracked signals use one lifecycle: `open | waiting | done`; label-only types are created as `done`, and operators can change the lifecycle inline.

Playbook steps retry failures or stalls up to the workspace limit (`workstream_step_retry_limit`, default 2). After the limit, the originating conversation gets a Decision card with **Retry**, **Skip step**, and **Stop playbook**, while its signal is `waiting`. Completed agent runs are written to the source thread as agent-authored `run` messages. Notification pings cover decision-needed, mention, assignment, and customer human-ask (`handoff`); delivery preferences remain per channel.

Composer slash verbs: `/assign`, `/ticket`, `/playbook`, `/schedule`, `/approve`, `/project`, `/workbench`.

PlatformChange tools include `create_project` and `set_posture` (posture always asks). Agenda wakes use `schedule_wake`; `upsert_trigger` is the same write (one Trigger row). `dispatch_work` hands coding jobs to a workbench Connection (GitHub Copilot, Cursor, Anthropic, OpenAI, MCP adapters).

Playbooks (`workstreams`) use an editable ordered list with exactly six step kinds: `send_message`, `agent_task`, `wait_for_reply`, `ask_decision`, `call_tool`, and `schedule`. Reply and decision steps can branch by target step ID, name, or position; other steps continue linearly. `send_message` and `call_tool` support `{field}` templates from run context/input. Filing a ticket starts at most one `WorkstreamRun` per conversation and playbook, and the ticket API exposes the active step name. Agents draft or replace complete step lists through governed `create_workstream` / `update_workstream` PlatformChanges. Playbooks and project canvases are the maps; the OS overlay graph is retired.

**Retired surfaces (conversation-first cutover):** `/cases` hub redirects to `/settings/action-tags`. Decisions are cards in the thread (no Decisions inbox leaf). `EmailRoutingRule` matching is retired — `InboxRule` only: the model, the `email_routing_rules` table, the `/api/email/routing-rules` CRUD, and the dashboard rules manager are all gone (Alembic `042_retire_legacy_routing`). Case `follow_up_task` is gone: filing a signal never opens an Agenda AgentTask, and human gates stay inline in the thread. The custom-table builder router is unmounted, so `/api/app/custom-*` and `/api/app/standard-tables` answer 404; `models/custom_db.py`, `services/custom_db.py`, and `routers/custom_db.py` are marked dead code and kept only so existing tenant rows survive until they migrate onto Contact, Project, and typed signal fields. `OperationalOutcome` is a trading-era side table with no router: rows enter only through the trading webhook, and reads are internal (learning signals, tenant introspection, thread reference cleanup). Notifications ping only for assign, mention, and decision. Custom DB models and `OperationalOutcome` are unloaded from the central model export; their tables and direct imports remain only for migration and external-reporting compatibility. `EvalCheckpoint` is deprecated and receives no new rows; evaluations use the `RunEvent` log. Bokito personal assistant is an Agent with `acts_for_user=True` and `audience=internal`; bootstrap seeds **Front desk** (`audience=customers`). Overview uses four fixed blocks; Cmd+K searches and runs verbs.

**Conversation-first contract stubs (2026-09):** A module contributes Signal types, Playbooks, tools, and knowledge to existing surfaces; it does not add a parallel rail screen. Learning proposals become governed drafts or examples and retain their module/source attribution. Knowledge defaults to `internal=true`, and usage is attributed through `UsageLedger` to agent, Signal type, and Playbook run where available. Financial apply tools are marked consequential and always cross the human gate. Widget visitors resolve into the same Contact and conversation records as other channels. Autonomy grows only within the workspace posture ceiling; role-based Govern access remains owner/admin for structural changes and member-readable for operational context. Govern presents **Ledger** and **Autonomy** as its two operator sections. Agenda is the single timeline for planned and past trigger occurrences; follow-up work remains in its conversation and the `AgentTask` ledger.

**One autonomy dialect (2026-10):** Signal types, playbooks, agents, and Govern scopes share `manual | assisted | autonomous`. Write still accepts legacy `approval`/`auto`. Learned inbox rule `auto_task` and the decision options `look_at` / `create_task` create a human `AgentTask` on the conversation (`scheduled_for`), not `Signal.follow_up_at`. Tool `create_task` / `schedule_task` on a conversation do the same; `delegate_to_agent` and playbook jobs still mint `AgentTask`. Overview **Needs you** renders the same conversation row as Communication. Project queue auto-accept follows the workspace posture. `Project.autonomous_mode` is dropped (Alembic `067`). Project agent and token budget are edited only on the **Project** tab; Settings points there. OS overlay HTTP `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/workforce/os/graph|nodes|edges` returns 410; `ensure_canvas_seeded` no longer inserts overlay rows; tools and Govern apply of `canvas_node`/`canvas_edge` return `retired`. Agent side panel look-at lives under **This conversation**, not a second Active-task block. Dashboard thread ids are UUID strings. Channel default agent is only `ChannelAccount.default_agent_id` (`AgentBindingPicker` on Settings → Channels and the widget handling agent). Inbox AI **Who answers** links there; `ChannelBindingsPanel` / `ChannelDefaultAgentsPanel` are gone. `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/channels/bindings` returns 410. The `ChannelBinding` table remains for disconnect cleanup. Scope copy `platform:graph:edit` is **Edit the project canvas**. Reserved Connections leaves `/connections/connected` and `/connections/tools` redirect to `/connections` so they never collide with a module slug. Dashboard clients no longer wrap `GET /orchestration/tasks` as a product “task” API.

**Agenda (2026-10, unified tasks):** `/agenda` is one calendar of everything that happens, by people and agents, next to connected Google or Outlook calendars. Views: Day, Week (default), Month, List; URL params `view`, `date` (omitted when today), `who`, `project`, `layers` (`tasks`,`activity`), `cals` (comma list of calendar connection ids; omitted = all connected). **Show** lists one checkbox per connected calendar plus **Tasks** and **Activity**. No separate Look-ats / Check-ups / Agent tasks / Routines layers — those are all Tasks (one-off or recurring). Stage follow-ups still use `Trigger.purpose=stage_checkup` under the hood but appear under Tasks. Conversation reminders create a human `AgentTask` (`kind=task`, `scheduled_for`) via `POST /orchestration/tasks` / tools `create_task`/`schedule_task` — `Signal.follow_up_at` is retired from Agenda. Snooze-as-park (`snoozed_until` / Uitgesteld queue) is retired; mark unread or plan a task instead (API still accepts legacy snooze patches as unread). Empty calendars show a banner to **Connections** (`/connections?kind=calendar`); OAuth connect for Google/Outlook Calendar runs through Connections/marketplace, not Agenda-only buttons. Sync and event CRUD stay on Agenda (`GET/POST /api/calendars/*`). A **Needs attention** strip counts due tasks and failed runs. **New** still offers once / event / cron triggers and Calendar block; recurring scans are managed under **Manage tasks**. Every surface reads `GET /api/agenda` (kinds `session|wake|checkup|task|calendar|activity`; max 92-day window). The project page shows a small agenda card. The shell widens to 1800px on `/agenda`.

**Stage owner and check-up (2026-10):** Each flow stage may set `owner {kind: user|agent|team, id}` (null keeps the current owner) and `checkup_minutes` (0 off, 60 to 43200; done stages are always 0). Entering a stage hands the conversation to its owner. While the ticket stays there, one interval `Trigger` with `purpose="stage_checkup"` fires on the rhythm to whoever owns the conversation then: an agent gets a run whose findings land as an internal note; a person gets the conversation marked unread and a notification; a team or nobody gets it marked unread. Leaving the stage or closing removes the trigger; saving the flow resyncs open tickets (`services/stage_checkups.py`). The ticket payload carries `checkup {trigger_id, every_minutes, next_at, last_at, last_status}`; board cards carry `assignee_kind`, `agent_id`, `checkup_at`. The ticket panel shows "Check-up {when}" linking to Agenda; flow cards show a check-up chip.

**One module package (2026-09):** The module catalog owns one package per module: hashtag templates, Playbook templates, and an optional Project template. Platform category seeds use the same catalog. Installing a module exposes package capabilities through existing Signal, Playbook, Project, and Connections surfaces; it does not create navigation.

Playbook steps use six kinds: `send_message`, `agent_task`, `wait_for_reply`, `ask_decision`, `call_tool`, `schedule`. Tracked signal operator lifecycle: Open / Waiting / Done.

- **Backend:** `apps/api` — FastAPI + SQLModel + Alembic + Postgres/pgvector + Redis/arq workers.
- **Auth:** signup (tenant+owner), invite/accept, staff-login + switch-tenant, roles `owner|admin|member`, JWT with `staff` claim, subdomain tenant routing (`*.bokito.ai`).
- **Platform support (2026-08):** Bokito operators are `User.is_staff`. They authenticate with the normal login form (JWT `staff: true`); staff-only accounts (no membership) land in the first workspace that allows support. Operators enter a workspace via `POST /auth/switch-tenant` and act as themselves with effective admin. Each workspace can turn this off in Settings → General (`security.allow_platform_support`, default **on** when missing). Opted-out workspaces stay visible in the staff picker as locked and cannot be entered. Every enter/leave is written to `StaffAccessLog`. There is no anonymous or secret-URL access.
- **Staff impersonation / log in as (2026-10):** From `/ops`, staff can open a customer user 1:1 via `POST /api/staff/ops/users/{id}/impersonate` (optional `tenant_id`). Issues a member JWT for the target with `impersonator_id` set and `staff: false` so permissions and UI match that user. Staff cannot impersonate other staff; the target needs an active membership in a workspace that allows platform support. Refresh cookies stay on the staff account (no target refresh session). Exit with `POST /api/auth/stop-impersonation` (dashboard banner “Stop viewing as”). Access logs use actions `impersonate` / `impersonate_end`. Ops directory users include `memberships[]` for the workspace picker.
- **Platform ops directory (2026-09):** Staff-only page at `/ops` (link from the platform support bar). `GET /api/staff/ops` returns this API environment (`environment`, `public_api_url`), all tenants (member count + support flag), all users (staff flag + membership count + memberships for impersonation), and recent `StaffAccessLog` rows. Optional `q` filters tenants/users. Enter uses the existing switch-tenant flow. Staff can hard-purge a tenant with `DELETE /api/staff/ops/tenants/{id}` (body `confirm_slug` must match). Owners delete from Settings → General; staff also see that danger zone while entered (effective role is admin, so `delete_workspace` alone would hide it). Subscriptions/billing are not listed yet.
- **Ownership guards (2026-09):** Only a real workspace owner (not admin, not staff-as-admin) can promote/demote/deactivate owners. Admins may still invite and manage admin/member roles. API: `update_member_role` / `remove_member` take `acting_is_workspace_owner`.
- **Deactivate people and agents (2026-10):** Operators hide a person or company agent from the working roster without deleting history. People: `Membership.is_active` + `deactivated_at` (workspace-scoped; `User` stays). `DELETE /api/app/workspaces/{id}/members/{memberId}` deactivates; `POST .../reactivate` restores. Inactive memberships cannot log into that workspace, do not appear in pickers/teams/assignment/chat-access, and are omitted from `/me` workspace lists. The membership row stays so conversation authorship remains. Inviting the same email is allowed and accepting reactivates the row. Agents: **Deactivate** is `DELETE /api/workforce/agents/{id}` (`kind=archived`, `is_active=false`); **Reactivate** is `POST /api/workforce/agents/{id}/restore`. `GET /api/workforce/agents` returns active company agents only; pass `include_inactive=true` for directory views. AccessPicker and `validate_entries` refuse deactivated principals (leftover grants clear on save). Agents library and Workforce both have a **Deactivated** filter; related links cross-link the pages. Workforce directory shows **Last active** (`User.last_seen_at` / `Agent.last_active_at`). Status copy is **Deactivated** / **Gedeactiveerd**. Alembic `060_membership_is_active`.
- **Workspace delete cycles (2026-09):** `delete_workspace` nulls `decision_requests` / `platform_changes` / `signal_messages` FKs before the tenant_id sweep — SQLAlchemy cannot sort the decision↔platform_change↔signal cycle. Staff purge helper: `scripts/ops/vps-purge-demo-test-tenants.py` (keeps bokito/autotrading/bourgondienadvies/kadeso/`demo` + `owner@demo.local`).
- **Workspace sessions (2026-07, cycle 1):** the JWT `tenant_id` is the single source of truth for the active workspace. `POST /api/auth/switch-workspace` re-scopes the token for any member with a membership (staff may enter any tenant, logged in `StaffAccessLog`); `POST /api/app/workspaces` returns a `session.access_token` scoped to the new tenant which the dashboard adopts with a full reload; `User.last_tenant_id` is persisted on signup/login/switch/create/invite-accept so login and refresh land in the last-used workspace (staff refresh honors it even without a membership). Frontend: `AuthContext.switchWorkspaceTenant`/`adoptWorkspaceSession` store the token in sessionStorage and `window.location.assign('/')`; `WorkspaceContext.switchWorkspace` only mutates local state when the target equals the JWT tenant. Fresh-tenant bootstrap is clean (assistant + personal agent, 4 workspace docs, runtime profiles, one assistant channel conversation, enabled hourly heartbeat — no demo project/orchestrator/workstream). Recurring triggers reuse one internal Signal thread via `Trigger.signal_id`. Heartbeat findings always land in the tenant `operations_signal_id` thread (created at bootstrap). Existing tenants get the ops thread and a check-in row on startup via `ensure_platform_watch`, but a disabled heartbeat is never flipped on. Onboarding: `GET /api/app/onboarding` computes a checklist (email, company, assistant, watching, first_decision, team) from live data; `watching` is the enabled platform check-in. The Communication empty state renders `OnboardingChecklist` until complete. Agent tools `get_platform_watch` / `set_platform_watch` (ungated) let the assistant turn watching on from chat. Integrity tooling: `apps/api/tests/test_tenant_isolation.py` + `scripts/ops/audit-tenant.py` (cross-tenant FK audit + per-tenant row counts).
- **Backend consolidation (2026-07, cycle 2):** canonical stacks only — orchestration lives fully under `/api/orchestration/*` (settings, workstreams CRUD, tasks, runs); the legacy `/api/orchestra` router, `AgentProfile`/`WorkstreamRun`/`WorkstreamStepRun` models and `orchestra_runner.py` are deleted (tables dropped by `schema_patch`). `/api/widget`, `/api/settings/llm-keys` and legacy `/api/email/threads*` are removed; `/api/livechat` is the only widget API and `/api/signals` the only inbox API. `settings_orchestra.py` became `inbox_settings.py` (inbox settings, feedback, notification prefs only). Notifications are wired end-to-end: `GET /api/notifications` + `POST /{id}/read` + `POST /read-all`, dashboard `NotificationContext` fetches from the API and subscribes to gateway `notification` events. **Reliability:** the in-process API scheduler (`trigger_scheduler.py`, 60s tick) is the canonical scheduler for triggers and runs the learning cycle hourly for `learning_enabled` tenants; the ARQ worker only handles queued jobs + mailbox polling cron. Redis enqueue failures and scheduler heartbeat are recorded in `services/runtime_health.py` and exposed via `GET /api/health/ready` (`runtime` field). **Security:** in-memory rate limiting (`middleware/rate_limit.py`) on login/signup/refresh/password-reset, `POST /api/hooks/{id}` and livechat session start; refresh tokens stored as SHA-256 digests (O(1) lookup, `Session.refresh_token_hash` indexed); prod validator also enforces `WORKER_INBOUND_SECRET` strength and complete OAuth client-id/secret pairs; mock OAuth and `POST /api/email/mock/inbound` are disabled in production. Smoketest: `scripts/dev/smoketest-core-flows.py` (13 checks: health, signup, onboarding, refresh, mailbox connect, mock inbound, trigger thread reuse, decisions, rate limit). **Open finding:** the mobile app still consumes `/api/notifications/decisions*` as its decisions list; those endpoints stay until mobile migrates to signal-based decisions.
- **AI-sectie + Projects als eersteklas feature (2026-08):** de middelste railgroep heet **AI** met drie tabs — Agents, **Projects** (`/projects`, Lucide `FolderKanban`), Knowledge (`tabGroups.ai` + `tabs.projects` in `nav.json` EN/NL). Projects is uit settings gepromoveerd: `/settings/projects` redirect naar `/projects`; de settings-navlink is weg. **Lijstpagina** `ProjectsPage.tsx`: kaartengrid (2-3 koloms) met per kaart PO-agent, repo + indexstatusbadge en een tokenbudget-voortgangsbalk (`ProjectBudgetBar`), voetlink **Open in communicatie**, kebab-snelacties (agentpagina, Communicatie-map, Delete) en een create-dialog (naam/slug/beschrijving). **Detailpagina** `ProjectDetail.tsx` (`/projects/:projectId`): header met slug-badge, View threads en Delete (naam-bevestiging); secties Orchestration (PO-koppeling + workstreams-lijst), Repository, Budget (remaining today/hour) en About (naam/beschrijving bewerken, project-ID kopiëren) plus Recent activity via `WorkLogsTable` (laatste 10 runs, `listWorkLogs({project_id})`). Herbruikbare bouwstenen onder `components/projects/`: `ProjectRepoSection`, `ProjectOrchestratorSection`, `ProjectBudgetBar`; `ProjectsSettings.tsx` is verwijderd. Geen backendwerk — alles draait op de bestaande `/api/projects`-endpoints.
- **IA + UX system (2026-07, cycle 3):** the rail has 7 items in 3 groups — Control (Cockpit, Communication, Contacts, Agenda), Agent (Agents, Knowledge; sinds 2026-08 heet deze groep AI en bevat ook Projects — zie hierboven), Settings. Cockpit merges Overview/Activity/Usage as inner tabs (`/cockpit`, `/cockpit/activity`, `/cockpit/usage`, component `CockpitTabs`); Knowledge = `WorkspaceDocs` at `/knowledge` (Skills page merged in); Personal settings merge Profile + Access & security into one "Profile & security" page; Members page no longer has the localStorage-only Teams UI. Legacy routes redirect (`/overview`, `/activity`, `/usage`, `/skills`, `/workspace[/:docId]`, `/home`, `/os/docs*`). Dead UI removed: mock data files, home widgets, `LlmKeysSettings`, `os-api.ts`, Billing/Support stub pages (uit portal-nav), "Coming soon" chips op New conversation, `href="#"`-links op Workspaces. Rail/topbar/command palette are i18n-driven via `nav.json` keys `tabs.*`, `tabGroups.*`, `topbar.*`, `palette.*` (EN base + NL). Inbox composer/timeline strings are English (EN base). Self-service signup lives at `/signup` (`authSignup` in `lib/api.ts`, `AuthContext.signup`); the onboarding checklist is dismissible per tenant (localStorage key `bokito-onboarding-dismissed:{tenantId}`). **Cycle 4 cleanup (2026-07):** the dashboard EN base is complete — `CompanyConfig`, `MemberManagement`, `InboxSettings`, `ResetPassword`, `ThreadDetail`, `form-validation`, `agentSteps` and integration/MCP descriptions were converted from hardcoded Dutch to English. The fake "brand scanner" banner on Company config (simulated scan with hardcoded colors) and the dead "Wijzigen" subdomain button were removed. The orphaned `/onboarding` 3-step wizard (route, `Onboarding.tsx`, `OnboardingStep1-3`, unused `FIELD_TYPE_META`) was deleted; the dismissible onboarding checklist in Communication is the only onboarding surface. **Known post-MVP items:** full NL translations exist only for the i18n namespaces (nav, communication, common); other pages are EN-only (consistent, no per-page mixing). Mobile still uses `/api/notifications/decisions*`; assistant-chat E2E with a live LLM is not covered by automated tests.
- **Communication UX + user management (2026-07, cycles 8-11):** Decision resolution writes no extra chat messages — the resolved state lives on the `DecisionRequest` (status + chosen option) plus a `decision_{action}` SignalEvent; `/api/chat` messages carry `kind` and an embedded server-driven `decision` payload (id/status/title/options/chosen_option_id) so `DirectChatPanel` renders one card that survives reload, and `system_event` messages render as subtle dividers. **User management:** transactional mail via `services/transactional_mail.py` (SMTP_* env vars; without SMTP_HOST mail is logged and non-production auth responses expose `dev_token`/`dev_link`); invites store `invited_by_user_id`, mail an accept link, and the Members page has Copy-invite-link + revoke (`DELETE /api/app/workspaces/{id}/invites/{inviteId}`); `/accept-invite?token=` is a public dashboard page (`GET /api/auth/invite-info` preview; existing accounts must confirm their current password — the invite token alone never grants a session; duplicate memberships are guarded). Member management: `PATCH/DELETE /api/app/workspaces/{id}/members/{memberId}` (UUID or numeric id) with a single-owner model — promoting to owner demotes the previous owner to admin, the last owner can't be demoted/removed, self-removal is blocked. Canonical roles are `owner|admin|member` in the frontend too (`USER_ROLES`; the old `editor|viewer` mapping that gave members zero permissions is gone). Email change via `PATCH /api/auth/profile` flips `email_verified` off and sends a verification link; `POST /api/auth/delete-account` (password-confirmed) anonymizes the user (FK-safe), deletes sole-member workspaces and blocks when the user is the last owner of a shared workspace; the 2FA stub row is removed and the duplicate `/api/auth/auth/*` routes are gone (frontend `authRoutes.session` now hits the canonical paths). **Mentions & inline agents:** messages use `@[Name](user:123)` / `@[Name](agent:uuid)` markup (`lib/mentions.ts`); ReplyComposer has an @-popover (members via shared `useMembers` hook + agents), chips render via `.mention-chip`; backend `notify_mentions` in `signal_threads.py` creates per-user `Notification` rows (kind `mention`, respects the `mentions` pref) and assignment via `patch_thread` notifies the new assignee (kind `assignment`, `assigned-to-me` pref, no self/duplicate notifications). `GET /api/notifications` is now a personal feed (own rows + tenant broadcasts). `POST /api/signals/{id}/invoke-agent` (`agent_id`, `instruction`, `output: note|reply_suggestion`) runs the agent over the thread transcript; notes land as internal `SignalMessage` (+ `agent_invoked` event), suggestions reuse `create_reply_suggestion`; the dashboard auto-invokes agents that are @-mentioned in a note. **Ask assistant grounding:** conversations created with `context_signal_id` (POST `/api/chat/conversations`) get the live source-thread transcript injected into the LLM history each turn (`context_thread_transcript`); Draft with AI has an optional guidance popover (instruction reaches `POST /api/signals/{id}/draft`). Verification: `tests/test_user_management.py`, `test_mentions_notifications.py`, `test_invoke_agent.py`, `test_ask_grounding.py`; live scripts `scripts/dev/e2e-usermgmt-c9.py` and `e2e-mentions-agents-c1011.py`.
- **Completeness sprint (2026-08, cycles 28-32):** Five implement-test-repeat cycles toward the first accountancy client. **Security + email correctness:** `GET /uploads/files/{tenant}/{file}` requires auth (Bearer or refresh cookie for browser subresources) with tenant match and path-traversal guards; server-side attachment reads bypass HTTP via `storage._local_file_for_url`. Ingest persists RFC `Message-ID`/`References` (Gmail headers, Graph `internetMessageId`); replies thread via those headers (Gmail) or the Graph `/messages/{id}/reply` endpoint (Outlook — Graph rejects manual `In-Reply-To`). `/api/email/send` delivers `cc`/`bcc`/`body_html`/attachments end-to-end; Outlook and Gmail attachments are downloaded at sync (`_hydrate_attachments`, 15MB cap) and served from uploads. **Microsoft SSO:** `GET /api/auth/microsoft/start` (public) + shared OAuth callback with `flow="login"` — `OAuthState.tenant_id` is nullable, `services/sso.provision_sso_user` finds-or-creates a passwordless user (`password_hash=""`) + workspace, mints refresh cookie; password endpoints guard passwordless accounts (initial password set allowed without current). **Google SSO (2026-09):** same path via `GET /api/auth/google/start` (`provider=gmail`, `GOOGLE_SSO_SCOPES` = openid/email/profile only — no Gmail mailbox scopes; authorize uses `prompt=select_account`). Frontend: `GoogleSignInButton` + `MicrosoftSignInButton` on Login/Signup, `sso=connected` return handling in AuthContext, provider-neutral `sso_error` reasons. One Google Cloud OAuth client serves SSO + Gmail mailbox (see `docs/PRODUCTION_READINESS.md`). One Entra app serves Microsoft SSO + Outlook mailbox. **Prod hardening:** boot fails when `LLM_MODE != live` or `BOKITO_MOCK_EXECUTION` set; mock MCP servers refused at install/test/call; GitHub mock repos/branches and mock OAuth disabled in prod; `BJORN_LUNDEN_MCP_URL` env feeds the BL catalog entry. Email sync polls each selected folder with per-folder cursors (`settings.sync_cursors`; Graph well-known folders, Gmail label ids). Rate limits on email sync/uploads/MCP install/invoke-agent; audit events for mailbox connect/disconnect, MCP install, role changes. **UI/UX:** notifications bell mounted in `ShellTopbar`; error-vs-empty states fixed (Activity, Members, Cockpit slices with partial-failure banner, onboarding checklist retry, ThreadDetail mention loads, AgentModelCard); remaining hardcoded Dutch strings converted to EN (mcp-remote-providers, theme-toggle, form-validation, staff-api, email-api); HelpCenters got skeletons/empty states/busy buttons. Onboarding checklist is email-first: new `email` step (real Outlook/Gmail account) ahead of company/assistant/channel/team; company step links `/settings/branding`. **Notifications + roles:** per-user prefs rows `assigned-to-me` / `mentions` / `decisions` each with desktop+email channels — email channel sends via `services/notification_mail.py` (SMTP through `transactional_mail`, link to thread); desktop-off+email-on skips the in-app row but still mails. `GET /api/push/vapid-public-key` returns 503 when VAPID unset (no mock key). Owner/admin required for workforce force-wake, `GET /api/govern/tokens`, and MCP install. Command palette searches live threads (`/api/signals?search=`) and contacts (`/api/channels/contacts?search=`) with debounce. **Verification:** `tests/test_email_fidelity.py`, `test_sso_login.py`, `test_prod_hardening.py`, `test_notification_email.py`, `test_role_tightening.py` (262+ tests green); end-to-end dry run `apps/api/scripts/smoke_cycle32.py` (SSO wiring, email-first onboarding, BL MCP connect, inbound → decision card → free-text resolve → reply with cc, palette search, prefs). Operator go-live steps: `docs/PRODUCTION_READINESS.md`.
- **SSO linked accounts in Profile (2026-09):** Google and Microsoft remain the only login IdPs. Identities persist in `user_identities` (`provider` google|microsoft, unique `subject`, `email_at_link`). Login resolves **subject first**, then email (`resolve_user_for_sso`), and upserts the row. Profile → Security → Connected accounts: `GET /api/auth/sso/identities`, `GET /api/auth/sso/{google|microsoft}/link/start` (`flow=link`), `DELETE /api/auth/sso/{provider}` (403 if last auth method and no password). `/me` and login payloads include `has_password`. Link requires IdP email to match the Bokito account; subject collision redirects `sso_error=subject_taken`.
- **Prod deploy ops (2026-08-25):** Push to `master` runs CI, then Deploy builds GHCR images and SSHs to the VPS (`appleboy/ssh-action`) for staging then production. Production still needs the GitHub Environment approval (auto-approve via `scripts/dev/watch-deploy.ps1`). Live cutover of `5793bc4` succeeded on 2026-08-25: staging smoke + prod SSH + `https://app.bokito.ai/api/health` all green; VPS `/opt/bokito` and `BOKITO_API_IMAGE`/`BOKITO_WEB_IMAGE` match that SHA. Earlier runner `dial tcp <vps>:22: i/o timeout` failures are no longer the default path. Manual fallback if Actions SSH fails: deploy key `~/.ssh/bokito_vps_deploy` as `root@31.97.45.44`, then `python scripts/vps-prod-deploy-remote.py <sha>` after GHCR images exist (script logs the VPS into GHCR with a `gh` token). Building images on the VPS still needs BuildKit (`docker buildx`; legacy builder breaks `RUN --mount`). Prod env facts: Microsoft OAuth pair set (client `f44d9848-…`, tenant `common`), `LLM_MODE=live` + Anthropic key set, `WORKER_INBOUND_SECRET` added 2026-08-13 (new boot validator requires it), VAPID web-push keys set on `/opt/bokito/.env.prod` (2026-09-04; rotate via `python scripts/ops/vps-set-vapid.py --apply --force`), SMTP/`BJORN_LUNDEN_MCP_URL` still unset. Staging runs `ENVIRONMENT=staging` with mock LLM (prod validators don't apply). Ruff is pinned to 0.15.17 in `apps/api` dev deps so CI (pip, fresh install) matches local lint.
- **Dev mock mailbox connect (2026-08, updated 2026-09):** When Microsoft/Google OAuth creds are absent in dev, the mock connect flow (`/api/email/oauth/start` and the integrations variant) seeds placeholder credentials `{"access_token": "mock-access-token", "mock": true}` via `ensure_email_account(seed_mock_credentials=True)` and stamps `last_sync_at` + `sync_window_days` so the mailbox lands as **Active** (not Connecting). `email_sync.sync_account` for mock creds stamps `last_sync_at` and returns `status=ok` (no provider poll); `send_via_provider` store-only "sends"; production never seeds mock creds (mock paths already 503 there). Live check: `apps/api/scripts/dev/smoke_outlook_connect.py`.
- **Mailbox install contract (2026-09):** Connecting a mailbox (SMTP/IMAP, Gmail, Outlook) asks for a backfill window during install (presets **7 / 30 recommended / 90 / 365**; default 30). Install succeeds only after credentials verify **and** the first Inbox `sync_account` completes (`last_sync_at`). SMTP/IMAP: `POST /api/channels/accounts` accepts `sync_window_days`; `POST .../verify` runs verify + first sync. OAuth: `sync_window_days` on `/api/email/oauth/start` is stored in `OAuthState.context_json`; callback runs first sync before `oauth_status=connected` (failure clears tokens). Day-to-day sync is automatic (worker cron). The channels list does **not** show Sync now for healthy mailboxes; **Retry sync** (`retry_sync` action) appears only when sync is pending/stale/errored. History in the expanded row is advanced (future backfills); first-install history is chosen in the connect dialog.
- **Timeline group-chat model (2026-08):** the thread timeline (`TimelineItem.tsx`) renders four author types with distinct bubbles — external contacts (neutral card, favicon avatar, left), teammates (elevated bubble + "Team" chip + member avatar, left), AI agents (accent-washed bubble + Sparkles avatar + "AI" chip + agent name, left) and the signed-in user (solid brand fill + `--color-accent-fg` text, no avatar, no name header, right-aligned — iMessage/WhatsApp-style; failed sends show "Not delivered"). Internal notes use the same chat-bubble shell (`ChatMessageBubble` variant `note`: dashed border) with the author's `UserAvatar` left/right by ownership, name always visible (You / colleague) and an **Internal note** subtitle — no avatar-less left-rule strip. **Mail cards (2026-10):** in email threads every non-internal mail (inbound, own, teammate, agent) renders as `MailMessageCard` ("coloured envelope, white letter"): body always on `bg-bg-surface`, author encoded by the envelope band on top (self = accent tint + `ring-accent/30`, agent = AI wash + **AI** chip, teammate = elevated + **Team** chip, external = elevated) and by side (own cards `flex-row-reverse` without avatar, like chat). Cards cap at `max-w-[min(100%,42.5rem)]`. The band reads `Sender · to you, +N` (`timeline.envelope.*`; "you" when the first recipient is one of `mailOwnAddresses`/the user) and expands on click to a `dl` grid with From/To/CC/BCC (`formatAddressListDisplay` normalizes JSON-array headers to comma lists); time + sent check sit right in the band. `ThreadTimeline.openMailRowIds` keeps the newest mail and the newest inbound mail open; older mail mounts folded to its band + first body line (`mailCollapsedByDefault`, initial state only, focused/unread rows never fold). Hover toolbar: Reply/Reply all/Forward on inbound external, Forward + Copy on own/team/agent mail. `data-testid="mail-card"` + `data-tone` for e2e/capture. `DirectChatPanel` follows the chat convention. **Thread visual unity (2026-09):** shared chrome lives in `ChatBubble.tsx` (`ChatMessageBubble`, `BubbleHeader`). Suggested replies / decision cards (`DecisionRequestMessage`) use the same left agent bubble instead of a full-width card. Active meta sessions (`AgentSessionCard`) drop the purple parent plane — operator right / agent left bubbles in the timeline; closed sessions collapse to one shared summary bubble (agent avatar left, operator avatar right). Agent outbound customer messages show a muted **Sent to the customer** / **Verstuurd naar de klant** subtitle under the agent name. **Bubble system v2 (2026-10):** conversation surfaces share a 720px reading column (`CHAT_COLUMN_CLASS` in `lib/chat-layout.ts`) for timeline rows and the composer; the composer has no top border. Bubbles are borderless fills with a hairline ring (external = surface, teammate = elevated, agent = AI violet wash, self = solid brand fill with accent-fg text, internal note = amber wash), 18px radius, 13.5px body text. Own messages sit right without an avatar. Consecutive same-author messages within 5 minutes (`CHAT_STACK_GAP_MS`) group WhatsApp-style: the lead bubble carries avatar, one-line header (name, chip, context) and the pointed 6px corner toward the avatar; follow-ups flatten their inner corners and sit 2px apart. The time sits inside the last bubble of a group (per-minute time pills are gone). Groups break on day pills, sessions, and decision cards. Consecutive system/AI activity pills that fall within the same 5 minutes (`CHAT_STACK_GAP_MS`, Slack/MUI-style) share one centered row with a clock time; a larger gap starts a new row so distinct operator bursts stay readable. Hover a pill for the exact timestamp. notes stay in a separate lane from customer-visible replies by the same person; `system_event` messages (contact link, escalate, handover) render as centered activity pills, not note bubbles, and do not stack. Copy, feedback (**Looks right** / **Not helpful** / **Correct this**) and note edit/delete live in a hover toolbar beside the bubble (`BubbleAction`) and take no layout space. Closed agent-session cards show only the agent avatar. **Widget parity (2026-10):** the grouping rules themselves live in `packages/shared/src/chat-layout.ts` (`assignBubbleStacks`, `chatRunLeads`/`chatRunCloses`, `CHAT_STACK_GAP_MS`, `CHAT_COLUMN_MAX_PX`); `apps/dashboard/src/lib/chat-layout.ts` re-exports them with the Tailwind column class, and `apps/chat-widget` imports the same functions, so dashboard and website widget stack identically. The widget renders three lanes (`#messageLane`): `ai` (AI violet wash, agent avatar, **AI** chip), `team` for `agent`/`human`/`operator` senders (surface bubble, **Team** chip) and `user` (solid `--bk-primary` fill + `--bk-on-primary` text, right, no avatar) — a human reply is no longer painted as an AI reply. All widget bubbles (rendered and streaming) come from one `#createMessageShell`; content writes target `.bk-msg-body` inside `.bk-msg-bubble`, with the time inside the bubble on the run-closing message. `#recomputeMessageStacks` replaced the old timestamp-cluster pass and treats day separators as run breaks. The stale, unreferenced `apps/chat-widget/public/css/chat-styles.css` is deleted. **Widget identity (2026-10):** the header avatar and message avatars have no initials fallback — a workspace brand image (`agent_avatar_image_url` / `widget_favicon_url`) or else the animated Bokito mark, same rule the launcher already followed. The launcher is 50px (`--bk-bubble-size`) with a 26/29px icon. The signed-in operator's own photo shows in the header account button: `livechat_compat.widget_session_payload` and `GET /api/livechat/me` now return `user.avatar_url` (base64 data URI from `User.avatar_url`), `#mergeSessionUser` never drops a photo the host already supplied, and `#fetchHostUserInfo` still calls the host `/me` when the session payload carried no avatar. **Member recognition (2026-08):** inbound mail from a workspace member login email never creates a CRM `Contact`, stamps `SignalMessage.author_user_id`, skips inbound agent processing, and the timeline resolves authorship by `author_user_id` or `from_address` vs members (never as external). Contact panel shows a teammate card (no Block/Approve). AgentLoop injects `## Current operator` (name, email, role) for non-external trust. `GET /api/signals/members` includes `role`.
- **Channel lifecycle: turn off, disconnect, delete (2026-10):** operator copy follows Front/Help Scout — **Receiving and sending** (switch; API `is_enabled`, state **Off** / **Uit**) for a temporary stop that keeps the sign-in, **Disconnect** / **Loskoppelen** (API still `POST .../archive`, state **Disconnected** / **Ontkoppeld**) to wipe credentials, **Bring back** / **Terugzetten** (`POST .../restore`) then reconnect or turn on, **Delete permanently** only after disconnect. A channel is never removed while its conversations stay behind. Detaching threads (`channel_account_id = NULL`) made them visible to every member, because the access check only restricts threads that have a channel, so the old `DELETE /api/email/connections/{id}` and the detach in `DELETE /api/channels/accounts/{id}` are gone. `services/channel_lifecycle.py` owns three steps. `POST /api/channels/accounts/{id}/archive` sets `ChannelAccount.archived_at` (Alembic `082`), `is_enabled=False`, drops primary, wipes credentials and the relay `inbound_secret`, and records `channel:archived`. Threads keep their `channel_account_id`, so `settings_json.access` keeps deciding who sees them and `PUT .../access` still works. `POST .../restore` clears `archived_at` and leaves the channel off (`channel:restored`). `DELETE .../accounts/{id}` deletes the channel together with every thread (`trash.delete_thread(permanent=True)`, not via the Bin), its inbox rules and bindings, and returns `conversations_deleted` (`channel:deleted`). It answers 409 when the channel still has conversations and is not archived. Archived rows have state `archived`, actions `["restore","delete"]`, no checks, and `conversation_count` on the row. Sync and resume answer 409, `/channels/status` and `/email/accounts` skip them, the widget count ignores them, and send on a thread of an archived mailbox answers 409 "archived". Reconnecting the same address (`ensure_email_account`) un-archives the row. Channels deleted before this change left orphaned threads with a null channel; those stay visible to everyone until re-linked.
- **Orphaned email threads (2026-08):** email threads without a channel are read-only for outbound mail: `POST /api/email/send` returns 409 ("mailbox disconnected", "archived" for an archived account, or "disabled" for `is_enabled=False` accounts) and never falls back to another mailbox implicitly — only an explicit `connection_id` in the send body rebinds the thread to that mailbox. Real provider send failures return 502 instead of storing a message marked `sent` (dev store-only sends still report `sent`). The AI worker skips email signals without an account (`mailbox_disconnected`). The dashboard detects `email_connection_id == null` on email threads, shows a "mailbox disconnected" notice with a reconnect link in place of the reply composer (notes keep working), and disables Draft with AI (`ReplyComposer.replyDisabledNotice`).
- **Channel send readiness gates inbound AI (2026-08):** the composer already used channel-registry `can_send` to block Send when a mailbox is `setup_required` / `action_required` / paused. The inbound worker and suggest/auto persist path now share that rule via `account_can_send()` (`services/channel_registry.py`): for email/slack/whatsapp with a bound account that cannot send, `process_inbound_signal` and `persist_inbound_agent_reply` call `acknowledge_channel_not_ready` — an **internal note** + `channel_not_ready` SignalEvent, **no** customer draft and **no** Send decision card. `deliver_outbound` rejects the same accounts with `failed:cannot_send` so approve/auto cannot leave undeliverable bubbles. Website chat is unchanged (always reachable). Tests: `tests/test_channel_send_ready.py`.
- **Session agent picker, cancel, and in-thread abilities (2026-08):** the composer action is "Bring in an agent" (`agentSession.launcher`), or "Bring in {name}" for one-click when a single agent is relevant. Candidates come from `GET /api/signals/{id}/agent-candidates` (`agent_sessions.thread_agent_candidates`), ranked with a `reason` that labels the picker row: `channel` (the agent `resolve_agent_for_signal` picks for this thread) → `project` (the linked project's `po_agent_id`) → `company` (chat-permitted company agents). Personal assistants are not candidates. `POST /sessions` resolves through `agent_sessions.resolve_session_agent`, not `resolve_chat_target`: any candidate is allowed (403 otherwise), so a channel agent that is closed for direct chat can still be brought in; omitting `agent_id` picks the top candidate. A session becomes history at its first turn: `list_sessions` reports a live `message_count` for active sessions, the card shows **Cancel** until then and **End session** after, and `DELETE /api/signals/{id}/sessions/{sessionId}` (`discard_session`) **permanently** deletes the conversation plus its `agent_session_started` event so nothing lands in the timeline or the Bin (it falls back to a normal checkout when turns did arrive). Session list queries exclude `deleted_at` rows so a cancelled empty meta conversation cannot linger. Acting on the host thread: `AgentLoop(tool_signal_id=...)` separates the thread that tool calls hit from the conversation that streaming publishes to; `chat.py` passes `signal.context_signal_id`, so an agent brought into a thread runs its messaging tools on the customer thread. Two tools make that concrete — `suggest_thread_reply` (ungated: proposes a draft through `inbound_agent.create_reply_suggestion`, so it lands as the normal approve/edit/decline card) and `take_over_conversation` (pins `Signal.agent_id`, clears `ai_paused` + assignee, emits `ai_resumed`; refused for personal assistants). `resolve_agent_for_signal` honours an active company `Signal.agent_id` as a thread-level pin before channel bindings, which is what makes a takeover stick for the next inbound message.
- **Inline agent sessions (2026-08):** an operator can bring an agent into any external thread ("Ask agent" in the reply composer). Switching the composer to Ask (mode chip or `@agent` mention) does **not** create a ThreadSession; `POST /api/signals/{id}/sessions` runs on the first Ask send (or when sending to a different mentioned agent than the leftover session). `source="agent_session"`, `context_signal_id` = host thread, `session_state="active"` so the existing chat endpoints handle the conversation with full thread grounding, tools, and MCP access. Nested sessions (`context_signal_id` set) stay off Alle communicatie and Chat met agents; they only appear as a card on the host thread. `POST /api/signals/{id}/sessions/{sessionId}/close` is the checkout: it freezes the session (`session_state="closed"`), extracts an outcome (`session_outcome_json`: summary = last agent message, actions = consequential tool calls from message trace steps — read-only research tools like `search_index` are excluded, MCP calls surface the inner tool name) and writes `agent_session_started`/`agent_session_closed` SignalEvents on the host thread. `GET /api/signals/{id}` returns `sessions[]`; the dashboard renders an active session as live left/right chat bubbles in the host timeline (`AgentSessionCard` + shared `ChatBubble`, per-answer "Use as reply" copies into the composer) and closed sessions as a shared summary bubble that expands to actions + read-only transcript. Sessions are team-visible; the old `AskAssistantPanel` side panel is removed. Sessions cannot be started on assistant threads (400). **One meta per thread:** `start_session` closes any other operator's active session on the same thread before returning. The caller's own active session is reused only when it is the same agent; a leftover session for a different agent is discarded (or checked out if it already has turns).
- **Session checkout proposal (2026-09):** an agent never closes its own session. The builtin tool `propose_session_checkout` (category messaging, `gated=False`) creates a `DecisionRequest` on the **host** thread with `action_type="session_checkout"` and per-option `payload {session_checkout: true, kind, session_id, thread_id}`. `kind` is `end_only` | `continue` | `apply_actions`; `end_only` and `continue` are always appended when the agent omits them. The proposal merges `checkout_decision_id` + `checkout_summary` into the session's `session_outcome_json` and leaves `session_state="active"`; a newer proposal defers the pending card (`chosen_option_id="superseded"`) instead of stacking. Resolution runs in `notifications.resolve_decision` → `agent_sessions.apply_checkout_choice`: `continue` clears the checkout keys and keeps the session active, `end_only` / `apply_actions` call `close_session` with the agent's `checkout_summary` as the session outcome (V1 applies nothing extra — actions already ran during the session). Session chats with an active `session_state` get a system-prompt hint (`assistant_threads.signal_chat_history`) to call the tool instead of asking in prose. Tests: `tests/test_session_checkout.py`.
- **Idle session nudge (2026-09):** `SESSION_IDLE_SECONDS` (config, default 300, `0` disables) drives the ARQ cron `nudge_idle_agent_sessions_job` (every minute at `:30`). `agent_sessions.nudge_idle_sessions` finds active `agent_session` Signals whose last `user_message` is older than the idle window and whose outcome has neither `idle_nudge_at` nor `checkout_decision_id`, then creates the same server-side checkout card (End / Keep going) with an idle summary and stamps `idle_nudge_at`. One nudge per session; choosing "Keep going" clears the marker so a later idle period can nudge again. Sessions without a single operator turn are never nudged (those are cancels, not checkouts).
- **Bokito AI model policy (2026-10):** Selection order is **item override → agent mode → workspace mode → catalog default**. Workspace mode lives in `Tenant.settings_json.models.workspace_chat_mode` (`automatic` | `bokito-maki` | `bokito-ai-3-1` | `bokito-kong`); `PATCH /api/settings/models/workspace-chat-mode` (admin). **New workspaces default to Automatic** (`default_tenant_settings` + empty-prefs fallback). Settings → Models shows **Bokito AI models** with clickable cards (Automatic + three tiers) for the workspace default. Agent `model` may be `inherit` (follow workspace), `automatic` (Bokito picks a tier from `task_hint`), a managed slug, or a BYOK slug; new agents default to `inherit`. `AgentLoop(model_override=, task_hint=)` feeds `services/model_policy.resolve_chat_model_slug`; callers that pin a flow stage or Agenda task pass `model_override` later. Automatic hints: light/triage/tag → Maki; heavy/complex/code → Kong; else Bokito. UI labels: Workspace default / Automatisch (NL).
- **Managed Bokito tiers Maki / Bokito AI / Kong (2026-10):** three virtual catalog rows (`provider="bokito"`, empty `model_id`): `bokito-maki` (Maki, lighter), `bokito-ai-3-1` (Bokito AI, default), `bokito-kong` (Kong, heavier). Silent backings are **backing chains** in `services/bokito_models.py`: each tier maps to an ordered list of `ModelCatalog` slugs; the provider of a step is that catalog row's provider, so adding a provider is a catalog row + platform key (no code). Built-in chains are Mistral only: Maki → ministral-3b-2512, mistral-small-latest; Bokito → mistral-medium-latest, mistral-large-4, mistral-small-latest; Kong → mistral-large-4, mistral-large-latest, mistral-medium-latest (Large 4 has no reasoning mode, so Kong turns show no thoughts unless a fallback with reasoning answers). (`mistral-large-4` is public preview since 2026-10-06, 1000 req/min on our plan vs 15 req/min for `mistral-large-latest`/Large 3; catalog cost uses its list price $1.36/$4.18.) Overrides, per tier, highest first: `platform_settings["bokito_backings"]` (staff, runtime, `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/staff/bokito-backings`, cached ~15 s per process; PUT validates every step is a non-Bokito chat row) → `BOKITO_BACKINGS` env JSON → built-in. `BOKITO_BACKING_FAMILY` is removed. Resolution takes the first step with a usable key: a tenant (BYOK) key on any step wins, then platform keys, skipping (key, model) pairs parked by `provider_health`. Operator UI never names backing providers; each card shows list price per 1M tokens and an **EU** region badge. Customer list prices are fixed env vars (`BOKITO_MAKI_*` / `BOKITO_AI_*` / `BOKITO_KONG_*` `_INPUT/OUTPUT_COST_PER_MTOK_CENTS`, defaults 10/10 · 50/150 · 150/750) — not derived from backing costs and not multiplied by `TOKEN_MARKUP_MULTIPLIER` (markup still applies to other platform-key usage). `GET /api/settings/models` returns `managed.models[]` + `selectable_chat`. Identity: `identity_for_bokito_slug` in style.py. Dashboard: `ModelIcon` / `ModelOptionLabel` always show a mark before model titles (managed silhouettes in `/models/{maki,bokito,kong}.png` as grey masks → provider brand → generic Cpu). Channel/connection **Access** matrix locks owner/admin toggles on (unrestricted server-side). Tests: `tests/test_model_catalog.py`, `model-icon.test.ts`.
- **LLM spend caps + alerts (2026-08):** Tenant caps live in `Tenant.settings_json.spend`: `daily_token_cap` (default 2M tokens/day) and `monthly_customer_micros_cap` (default $100/month, micro-USD); explicit `null`/0 means uncapped. Enforcement happens at the single choke point `resolve_model_call` (`services/spend_guard.check_tenant_budget`): only platform-key (billable, live) resolutions are checked, BYOK is never hard-blocked; over-cap raises `AppError` 402 `budget_exceeded`. Aggregates (platform tokens per UTC day, billable customer micros per month) are cached in-process for 60s. Alerts at 80%/100% fire from `record_usage` (fire-and-forget) through the ops-alert pipeline with pref category `billing-alerts`; dedupe via period-labeled titles + notification cooldown. API: `GET/PATCH /api/cockpit/budget` (PATCH owner/admin). UsagePage shows used-vs-cap bars, a blocked banner, and cap editing. The project `usage_budget` endpoint uses the tenant daily cap instead of the hardcoded 100k default when set.
- **Onboarding first-decision + demo thread + return nudge (2026-08):** The Getting Started checklist steps are `email`, `company`, `assistant`, `watching`, `first_decision`, `team` (the generic `channel` step was dropped). `watching` is done when the seeded heartbeat trigger is enabled. `first_decision` completes when any DecisionRequest on a signal has been resolved — the demo thread counts on purpose. The demo thread is opt-in: `POST /api/app/onboarding/demo-thread` (idempotent) seeds one email-style Signal with `source="demo"`, an inbound message and a pending decision card (`services/onboarding_demo.py`); `DELETE` removes it. Demo threads are deleted automatically when a real channel connects (channels router `create_account`, `ensure_email_account`) and never feed inbox-rule learning (`_record_no_reply_outcome` skips `source="demo"`). The checklist CTA seeds and navigates into the demo thread; a compact dismissible checklist card also renders on the Cockpit. Return nudge: an hourly scheduler pass (`send_channel_nudges`) mails owners of tenants that are 24h-14d old with no external channel, once — sent-at timestamp in `Tenant.settings_json.onboarding_channel_nudge_at`.
- **Platform check-in / watching (2026-08, herzien sep 2026 in IA fase 1; sep 2026 wizard):** New workspaces seed an hourly heartbeat **paused** (`enable_if_created=False`). The assistant tools `get_platform_watch` and `set_platform_watch` toggle only that seeded trigger (ungated). Setup hub counts enabled automations only and offers Turn on check-in. Cockpit shows a watch banner when the check-in is paused. Existing disabled heartbeats stay off until an operator or the assistant enables them. Findings land in the agent channel — see the agent-channel entry below.
- **First-run onboarding wizard (2026-09):** After workspace create, owners/admins with `settings_json.onboarding.wizard_required` are force-redirected to `/onboarding` until `wizard_completed_at` is set. Steps: intake (`source` / `org_size` / `use_case`), UI + workspace languages, notification prefs, Govern posture, rename/icon lead assistant, optional channel. Invited members only get language + notifications when tour unseen and `personal_wizard_completed` is false. API: `GET/PATCH /api/app/onboarding/wizard`. Checklist step `wizard` precedes email/assistant/decision/watching. Product tour (`TOUR_VERSION` 2) does not auto-start while the wizard is required; finishing the wizard sets a session handoff so the tour opens. Tour finish primary CTA = optional assistant setup chat; secondary = Communication. Setup hub Later list stays for branding/team/modules/projects.
- **Check-in landt in het agentkanaal (sep 2026, IA fase 1)**: `platform_watch.ensure_agent_channel()` (voorheen `ensure_operations_thread`) levert per agent één gedeeld kanaalgesprek: `Signal(channel="assistant", source="agent_channel", owner_user_id=None, agent_id=<agent>)`, subject = agentnaam. `owner_user_id=None` maakt het tenant-breed: de folder `assistant` filtert op `owner_user_id == user OR NULL`, dus elk lid ziet dezelfde historie op `/communication/agent/{agent_id}`. `triggers._surface_result` stuurt heartbeat-resultaten naar het kanaal van de agent die de trigger uitvoerde (`ensure_agent_channel(..., agent=agent)`); de `operations_signal_id`-fallback blijft alleen voor niet-heartbeat triggers (webhook-tenants zoals autotrading). De triggernaam is niet langer de hardcoded string "Platform check-in" maar `checkin_trigger_name(agent)` ("Check-in: {naam}"); de UI labelt de rij toch op `kind === 'heartbeat'`, en `_is_seeded_name()` ververst de naam alleen wanneer het platform hem zelf gaf (legacy namen of prefix "Check-in") zodat een eigen naam van een operator blijft staan. Agentresolutie staat op één plek: `triggers.resolve_trigger_agent()` (voorheen `_resolve_agent`, nu publiek) bepaalt wie de check-in uitvoert, en `platform_watch` bindt de thread aan diezelfde agent — anders vecht de binding met `_surface_result` en ontstaan dubbele kanalen. `lead_assistant()` spiegelt die regels (kind company, actief, role assistant, anders `get_lead_agent`), dus een gearchiveerde assistent laat de check-in bij de lead landen. **Migratie bestaande tenants** loopt via de bestaande startup-backfill `ensure_platform_watch` → `ensure_heartbeat_trigger`: een pre-migratie "Platform check-in"-thread (uit `Tenant.settings_json["operations_signal_id"]`) wordt in-place omgezet naar het kanaal van de lead assistant (zelfde rij, berichten blijven staan), de trigger wordt herbonden en de settings-key verdwijnt — alleen wanneer die key naar precies die thread wijst, zodat een tenant die de key voor een webhook-thread gebruikt hem houdt. Idempotent: een tweede run maakt geen tweede kanaal. `watch_status()` geeft nu `signal_id` in plaats van `operations_signal_id`. Agenda-items dragen `signal_id` van hun trigger, zodat `agenda-thread.triggerThreadPath()` direct naar het kanaal (heartbeat) of de run-thread linkt in plaats van op subject te zoeken. Onboardingstap `assistant` eist nu `owner_user_id IS NOT NULL`, anders zou het gezaaide kanaal die stap meteen afvinken. Tests: `tests/test_platform_watch.py` (seed, backfill-migratie, findings in kanaal, zichtbaar in `/api/signals/conversations`), `tests/test_tenant_isolation.py`, vitest `lib/agenda-thread.test.ts`.
- **WhatsApp connect UI (2026-08-30):** `WhatsAppConnectForm` is a three-step guide (Prepare in Meta → Paste in Bokito → Register webhook) with a step rail, per-field hints (Phone number ID ≠ phone number), and a link to Meta’s Cloud API get-started docs. After **Connect number**, step 3 shows copyable Webhook URL + Verify token and the messages-subscribe / test checklist. `AddChannelDialog` uses a WhatsApp-specific subtitle instead of the generic “create a Bokito address” copy. Product-help `inbox/channels` documents the same three steps.
- **WhatsApp inbox channel (2026-08):** WhatsApp Business Cloud API is a native inbox channel. Adapter `apps/api/app/channels/whatsapp.py` mirrors the Slack adapter: `normalize_inbound` (Cloud API webhook `value` → InboundMessages; media types become placeholders like `[Image received]` + caption with the media id in metadata; `statuses[]` are ignored), `send_message` (Graph `POST /{phone_number_id}/messages`, text; error 131047/470 → `failed:outside_service_window` for Meta's 24h customer-service window, 190/401 → `failed:auth`), `verify_signature` (`X-Hub-Signature-256` HMAC with `META_APP_SECRET`).
