One app-level webhook serves all tenants (`GET/POST /api/channels/whatsapp/webhook`): GET answers Meta's handshake against `WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN`, POST routes each payload to the `ChannelAccount` with `channel="whatsapp"` and `address == metadata.phone_number_id`. Threading is one continuous Signal per customer number (`thread_external_id` = wa_id); dedupe on `wamid` via the shared `ingest_inbound`. Accounts are created via the generic `POST /api/channels/accounts` (`provider="whatsapp_cloud"`, `credentials={access_token, waba_id}`); `GET /api/channels/whatsapp/setup` (owner/admin) returns webhook URL + verify token for the connect card. V1 is bring-your-own Meta app (System User token pasted in `WhatsAppConnectCard` on `/settings/channels`); Embedded Signup / Tech Provider, template messages, media send are V2. Default AI mode is `suggest` (tenant-tunable on `/settings/communication`); human takeover toggle covers whatsapp threads. UI surfaces: Messages hub rail entry, `whatsapp` composer surface (no signature, Enter-to-send chat style), channel binding routing, marketplace tile that links to the channel settings. Env: `META_APP_ID`, `META_APP_SECRET`, `WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN` (pair-validated at prod startup). Tests: `tests/test_whatsapp_channel.py`, `src/lib/message-composer.test.ts`.
- **Slack decision-notify + digest mail (2026-08):** Decisions reach operators outside the dashboard. `services/slack_notify.py` posts a Block Kit card (Approve/Deny/Open buttons, decision id in the action value) from the same convergence point as push (`publish_decision` → `schedule_notify_decision_slack`). Targeting: thread assignee DM via `users.lookupByEmail` (opt-in through the `slack` channel on the `decisions` notification pref row; requires `users:read.email` scope), fallback the workspace channel in `ChannelAccount.settings_json.notify_channel_id`. Interactivity: one global `POST /api/channels/slack/interactions` — the payload `team.id` selects the ChannelAccount whose signing secret verifies the raw body; clicks resolve through `resolve_message_decision` (actor mapped via Slack profile email, `via: "slack:U..."` in the SignalEvent payload) and the original message is replaced through `response_url` so buttons cannot double-fire. **Digest mail:** opt-in pref rows `digest-daily`/`digest-weekly` (email-only categories); arq cron `send_tenant_digests_job` at 06:00 UTC (weekly fires on Mondays) → `services/digest_mail.py` aggregates open/unassigned threads, pending decisions, inbound messages and agent-run stats per tenant; empty digests are skipped. NotificationSettings UI renders a Slack column and dash-cells for channels that do not apply to a row.
- **Alembic-managed schema (2026-08):** Postgres schema is Alembic-managed since revision `003_baseline` (replays `create_all` + frozen `schema_patch` column patches + idempotent data repairs, so `upgrade head` converges fresh and existing databases without a manual `stamp`). `init_db()` on Postgres runs `alembic upgrade head` in-process at startup under advisory lock 729001 (api + worker can boot concurrently); SQLite (tests/local) keeps `create_all` + patches. `schema_patch.py` is frozen — every new schema change must be a new Alembic revision with explicit DDL. Because the baseline replays live model metadata, later revisions that add columns (e.g. `004_user_totp`) guard with an inspector check so fresh databases (where `create_all` already made the columns) do not fail. Docker image ships `alembic/` + `alembic.ini`. Runbook: `docs/ops/MIGRATIONS.md`. Since aug 2026 `init_db()` on Postgres runs `_assert_no_schema_drift` after `upgrade head`: every model column must exist in the live schema or startup raises (a model change without a revision then fails the deploy smoke test and triggers auto-rollback instead of surfacing as 500s on every select — this happened with `agents.settings_json`, fixed by revision `013_agent_settings_json`).
- **Human audit coverage + unified activity (2026-08):** Consequential human mutations write `AuditEvent` rows (actor_type `user`): thread status/assignee changes (`signal:updated` — tag/priority tweaks intentionally excluded), bulk actions (`signal:bulk_close|reopen|spam|assign`, one event per bulk with before-states; read/unread excluded), delete (`signal:deleted`), AI handling changes (`ai_handling:changed`), agent CRUD (`agent:created|updated|archived|restored`), invites (`invite:created|resent|revoked|accepted`), member role/deactivation (`user:role_changed`, `user:member_deactivated` / `user:member_reactivated`), workspace settings (`workspace:settings_updated`), tool overrides (`govern:tool_override_update`), budget caps (`billing:budget_updated`), password change (`user:password_changed`), 2FA (`user:2fa_enabled|2fa_disabled`), mailbox disconnect (`email:mailbox_disconnected`), integrations (`integration:connected|revoked`). `GET /api/cockpit/activity` is the unified timeline: RunEvents (kind `agent_run`) merged with user AuditEvents (kind `audit`, `actor_name` resolved from users) sorted newest-first; the Cockpit ActivityPage has an All/Agents/People source filter and shows the actor per audit row. Tests: `tests/test_human_audit.py`.
- **TOTP 2FA (2026-08):** RFC 6238 implemented stdlib-only in `services/totp.py` (SHA-1, 6 digits, 30s period, ±1 step window); secrets stored Fernet-encrypted on `User.totp_secret`/`totp_pending_secret` + `totp_enabled` (Alembic `004_user_totp`). Flow: `POST /api/auth/2fa/setup` (returns secret + otpauth URI) → `POST /api/auth/2fa/enable` (verifies first code, promotes pending secret, audits) → login on enrolled accounts returns `{requires_2fa, challenge_token}` (5-min JWT, type `totp_challenge`, no API access) instead of a session → `POST /api/auth/2fa/verify` exchanges challenge + code for the normal LoginResponse. `POST /api/auth/2fa/disable` requires the password (SSO-only accounts confirm with a current code). `totp_enabled` is exposed in `_user_dict` and `/me`. Frontend: Login page has a 6-digit code step (AuthContext `login` throws `TwoFactorRequiredError`, `verifyTotp` completes), Profile & security has an enroll/disable section (manual key + copy + otpauth link; no QR lib), and `TwoFactorBanner` in the shell soft-enforces the existing workspace `require_2fa` flag for non-enrolled users. Tests: `tests/test_totp_2fa.py`.
- **Learning loop v2 (2026-08):** Learning is on by default — `run_tenant_learning_cycle` / `run_learning_for_enabled_tenants` treat missing `learning_enabled` as true (tenants opt out with `learning_enabled: false`). Guardrails are asymmetric for safety: high escalation (>40%) on `autonomous` still tightens to `assisted` immediately but now writes a `learning:posture_tightened` AuditEvent (actor_type `system`); low escalation (<10%) on `manual` no longer silently eases — it creates a system-proposed `PlatformChange` (resource_type `autonomy_posture`, pending_review, deduped on open proposals) that a human accepts in Govern; accepting applies the posture via `apply_autonomy_posture_change` in `platform_apply.py`. Persona proposals: 3+ negative feedback entries in the past week create a `persona_review` PlatformChange (change_kind `review`, evidence samples in `after_json`, deduped); accepting acknowledges the review — nothing is auto-applied. `PLATFORM_RESOURCE_TYPES` gained `autonomy_posture`/`persona_review` and `CHANGE_KINDS` gained `review`; Govern UI labels added in `govern-labels.ts`. Digest mails include a Learning line (active rules, rule suggestions to review, open system proposals) via new `rules_active`/`rules_suggested`/`learning_proposals` digest keys; learning stats alone do not un-skip an otherwise empty digest. Tests: `tests/test_learning_v2.py`.
- **Learning writes tool_allowances (2026-08):** CORE_INTENT policy tightening is closed. `apply_heuristic_allowance_tighten` (in the learning cycle after posture guardrails) maps 7-day escalated `tool_call:*` audits and rejected tool-gate `DecisionRequest`s to tool categories; when a category is effectively `allow` and hits ≥5 escalations or ≥3 rejects, it auto-writes `tool_allowances[category]=ask` with audit `learning:allowance_tightened` and appends `settings_json.learning_allowance_history` (max 5). Safety asymmetry: never auto-loosens and never pushes ask→deny. Govern `GET /allowances` returns `learning_history`; Policy tab shows recent notes. Digest adds `allowances_tightened`. Helper: `set_category_allowance` / `is_stricter_mode` in `tools/policy.py`.
- **Mobile: stacked inbox + PWA + web push (2026-08):** Below `md` the inbox stacks: `ThreadList` fills the viewport (`w-full md:w-72`, hidden when a thread is selected), `ThreadDetail`/`DirectChatPanel` show full-width with a back button (`onBack` prop, `md:hidden`) that navigates to the list path; the "Select a thread" placeholder and `DirectChatEmptyState` only render from `md` up and `AgentThreadPanel` only from `lg`. PWA: `public/manifest.webmanifest` (standalone, start_url `/communication`, SVG icon) + theme-color/apple-touch meta in `index.html`; `public/sw.js` is a minimal service worker (no offline caching by design) that renders `push` events (title/body from the payload, tag-collapsed per `kind:signal_id`, click focuses/opens `/communication/inbox/all/t/{signal_id}`); registered in `main.tsx` in prod builds. Web push: backend already had `POST /api/push/subscribe` + `GET /api/push/vapid-public-key` + `send_push_to_user` (pywebpush VAPID + `expo:` endpoints) wired from gateway publishes; added `POST /api/push/unsubscribe` (delete by endpoint for current user). Frontend `lib/web-push.ts` handles register/permission/subscribe/unsubscribe; Notification settings has a per-device "Push notifications on this device" toggle reflecting the real browser subscription. Requires `VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY`/`VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY`/`VAPID_CLAIMS_EMAIL` in prod env. Tests: `tests/test_push_notifications.py` (incl. subscribe/unsubscribe roundtrip).
- **Growing composer (2026-08):** `ComposerCard` auto-grows from content height (`scrollHeight`) instead of counting newlines. Chat starts at two lines (52px) and grows to 280px; email replies start at ~6 lines (132px) and grow to 440px; notes sit in between. Operators can drag the top edge (or use the expand control) for more room; the chosen floor persists in `localStorage` (`bokito.composer.floor.{chat|email|note}`). Double-click or Enter on the handle resets. Used in `ReplyComposer` and `/communication/new`. Composer chips sit in one left-to-right row: Reply, then Ask, then Note. On an AI chat without Reply, Ask comes first and Note follows it. `tone` drives the outline: Ask/`ai` is purple (`border-ai` + purple focus ring), Note is gray, Reply keeps the accent ring. Reloading a stored draft is keyed on the thread (`persistKey` + channel/recipient), not composer mode, so `@agent` inserts survive the switch into Ask.
- **Composer mode clamp and timeline follow (2026-10):** `effectiveComposerMode` (`lib/message-composer.ts`) clamps the composer to `surface.modes`; a stale `reply` on an agent chat falls back to the surface default (Ask). Before this, coming from an email thread could leave the agent chat in Reply, which stored the text as an outbound `user_message` (`reply_sent`) with no agent run. The thread-mode effect in `ThreadDetail` compares the loaded `detail.thread.id`, not the route id. `ThreadTimeline` follows growth (streamed answers, live trace replaced by the reply) until operator input (wheel, touch, or an upward scroll right after a pointer or key press) marks reading history; Virtuoso's "not at bottom" alone no longer stops following. The resize observer watches `[data-testid="virtuoso-item-list"]` (Virtuoso's first child is a fixed-height viewport). `ComposerCard` pads its action row when the in-app helper launcher (`.bk-launcher` in the `bokito-chat` shadow root, via `assistantLauncherRect`) overlaps it, so Send stays clickable.
- **New conversation draft (2026-09):** `/communication/new` is a draft surface in the hub (not a modal). Step 1 shows three intents — Contact, Agent, Teammate. The thread is created only on first send. Agent send creates the thread with a placeholder title, posts the typed text as the first chat bubble, then sets the list title once to a short intent label (not the full first message). `location.state.autoSend` is consumed by `ThreadDetail` so the bubble is actually streamed. Contact/Teammate use outbound email (`sendNewEmail`) with visible **From** mailbox picker; `default_outbound_connection_id` on `/me/preferences` (plus localStorage mirror) remembers the default, and the operator can switch Before send. Sidebar **+** on email channels and agents deep-links into the matching intent. `composeEmailPath` points at this draft. Forward from a thread still uses `ComposeEmailModal`.
- **Chat feel unification (2026-09):** Messages hub composer now matches the widget focus glow (`focus-within` accent border + 3px ring). Resize grip is thinner and quieter until hover/focus. Timeline bubbles use a soft `msg-bubble-enter` slide/fade (respects `prefers-reduced-motion`). Widget side: conversation-list overflow (`width:100%` + side margin) no longer creates an ugly horizontal scrollbar above the tab nav; footer is a hairline separator; user bubbles use a solid brand fill with on-brand text (same as the hub, matching mobile); brand apply lifts dark tenant colors for marks/launcher (`--bk-mark`, `--bk-launcher-icon`) so the monkey stays readable on dark chrome, remaps legacy neon to teal, and applies brand *after* `data-theme` so dark/light lifting is correct. **Lazy conversation create (2026-09):** "Start new conversation" only opens a local draft composer; `POST /livechat/conversation` runs on the first send (`#ensureConversation`). Widget history lists (`user/conversations`, `customer/conversations`) omit threads with no messages so abandoned drafts do not clutter the list.
- **Chat send buffer + smoother streaming (2026-09):** Website widget and dashboard agent chats share the same mid-stream Send model: abort the unfinished assistant reply, keep every user bubble, buffer new texts (~400ms settle), then start one turn with the joined buffer (`\n\n`). Typing alone never cancels a stream (widget removed `#cancelActiveResponse('typing')`); only Send or Stop does. Composer refocuses after Send. Widget SSE paints via rAF (one DOM write per frame), scrolls only when pinned to bottom, softens markdown finalize with temporary `min-height`, and ignores WS `streaming_delta` while SSE owns the turn. Dashboard `AgentChatView` / `useAgentSessionChat` queue mid-stream sends; live `AgentStreamBubble` shows plain smoothed text while active (markdown on finalize).
- **Inbox operator parity (2026-08):** Email replies support CC/BCC (`ReplyBody.cc/bcc` → `deliver_outbound` → stored in `SignalMessage.metadata_json`, exposed via `serialize_message` as `cc`/`bcc`; ReplyComposer has a Cc/Bcc toggle). New outbound email starts from `ComposeEmailModal` (compose button in the thread-list quick filters, `POST /api/email/send`, multi-To/CC/BCC supported end-to-end — `deliver_outbound` accepts a `to_address` override with comma-separated recipients). Forward = compose prefilled with quoted history (`Fwd:` subject), sent as a new thread. **Scheduled send + soft undo:** `ReplyBody.send_after_seconds` (server-capped at 600) stores the message with `send_status="scheduled"` + `SignalMessage.send_after` without delivering; the 60s scheduler tick (`deliver_due_outbound_messages`) delivers due messages; `POST /api/signals/messages/{id}/cancel` deletes a still-scheduled message and returns the body for draft restore. The dashboard gives email replies a 15s undo window (sonner toast with Undo; timeline shows "Sending..." while scheduled). **Full-text search:** on Postgres `list_threads` matches message bodies via `to_tsvector('simple', subject || body_text) @@ plainto_tsquery` backed by expression GIN index `ix_signal_messages_fts` (created in `schema_patch._ensure_search_indexes`); SQLite/tests fall back to ILIKE.
- **Unified inbox:** `GET /api/inbox` aggregates conversations (all channels), open decisions, email threads.
- **Chat:** multi-thread conversations, rename/delete, take over / hand back via AI handling, inline decision cards, message feedback into `FeedbackQueue`.
- **Chat folded into signals + one thread detail (2026-08, adhesive phase 4):** the `/api/chat` facade is gone; conversation endpoints live under the signals family (`apps/api/app/routers/signal_chat.py`, included at the top of `signals.py` so literal paths register before the `/{signal_id}` catch-all): `GET /api/signals/chat/targets`, `GET/POST /api/signals/conversations`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/signals/conversations/{id}`, `GET/POST .../messages`, `POST .../stream` (SSE + `_finalize_run` ledger settlement preserved). Take over / hand back uses `PUT /api/ai-handling/conversation/{id}` (see AI handling). Frontend chat helpers (`bokitoListConversations`, `bokitoStreamMessage`, …) moved from `bokito-api.ts` to `signals-api.ts` with paths in `app.routes.ts` under `appRoutes.signals`. `DirectChatPanel` is deleted: `ThreadDetail` is the single thread-detail component and renders `AgentChatView` (co-located `AgentChatView.tsx`, keeps `DirectChatEmptyState` + `directPathForAgent`) for `channel === 'assistant'` threads; `DirectCommunication` and `AgentSessionCard` embed `AgentChatView` directly.
- **Activity timeline (2026-09):** `/activity` (`ActivityTerminalPage`) is a standalone page (same AppShell chrome as Contacts — not nested in the Communication MessagesHub / thread list). It shows a modern vertical timeline (day pills, tone icons, soft rows) fed by `GET /api/cockpit/activity` plus the gateway `runs`/`decisions` stream, with per-agent chips (`?agent=`), search (`?q=`), and click-through to run threads. `/communication/activity`, `/cockpit/activity` redirect here (query preserved). Sidebar pin **Activity** / **Contacts** / **Settings** stays at the bottom of the Communication rail; Activity navigates out of the hub. Agent folder **Activity** sub-rows open `/activity?agent=:id`. The old Reports Activity tab is gone (`CockpitTabs` = Overview + Usage). Communication sidebar layout (v4, storage key `communication-sidebar-prefs-v4`): rail header = **New chat** + customize; no duplicate Communication heading (the page-guide icon sits next to the topbar title). Fixed top of the list = **All communication** (sub-views For you/Open/Unassigned/Closed/Snoozed/Spam); middle sections default order **Channels → Chat with agents → Teams** (each channel and agent is a folder with the same sub-views; agents also expose **Activity** → `/activity?agent=`); pinned bottom = **Contacts** and a single **Settings** link. Channel/agent chips above the list were removed. Any thread's More menu has **Add to project**: a project picker creates a queue Task (`POST /api/workforce/projects/{id}/queue` with `signal_id`) linked to the signal, with `origin="queue"` on the unified `AgentTask`.
- **Agent pause/wake removed (2026-09):** operators no longer Pause or Wake company agents. **Deactivate** (`DELETE /api/workforce/agents/{id}`, `kind=archived`, `is_active=false`) hides an agent from the library; **Reactivate** (`POST /workforce/agents/{id}/restore`) returns it. Chat access (`everyone` / `selected` / `nobody`) still controls who may open a direct chat. Alembic `025_drop_agent_pause` reactivates existing company agents that were sleeping/paused. `PATCH /agents/{id}/status` accepts only `standby|active|error` (runtime, not an operator pause). Agenda triggers report `agent_archived` when a bound agent is inactive/archived (was `agent_paused`). `POST /workforce/pause` is removed.
- **Managed resources (2026-09):** stack/module-owned agents carry provenance on `Agent` (`managed_origin`, `managed_ref`, `template_slug`; optional `origin_label` in settings). `ensure_managed_agent` in `app/services/managed_resources.py` creates/patches active managed agents and **never** silently un-archives. Archived managed agents get a `PlatformChange` restore proposal (`mode=ask`) → Decision in the agent channel; accept runs `apply_agent_change` restore. UI shows a **Managed** / **Beheerd** badge; archive confirm names the origin. First consumer: autotrading `seed_trading_stack` (Strategy Optimizer + MMXM Trader, `stack:trading`). Rejected restores cool down for 7 days before a new proposal.
- **Default agent (not “Lead”) UX (2026-09):** UI copy treats `Agent.is_lead` as the **default agent** (fallback for new channels and unassigned work). Setup still requires ≥1 active company agent. Changing default is a quiet control on agent detail (**Use as default agent**), not a primary CTA. Agent header menus keep only Duplicate/Archive; cross-links (Inbox AI, Knowledge, Govern, Channels) use `PageRelatedLinks` footers instead of header “More actions” / top cross-link boxes. Role labels no longer call orchestrator/PO “Lead”; project PO is “project agent”.
- **First-run guided tour (2026-08; handoff sep 2026):** after the first-run wizard, users get a welcome intro + spotlight tour + assistant setup handoff. Framework: `apps/dashboard/src/components/tour/` — `tour-steps.ts` (`TOUR_STEPS`, `TOUR_VERSION`, `WELCOME_PILLARS`), `TourContext.tsx` (welcome / steps / finish). Auto-start: wizard not required AND (session handoff after wizard OR tour unseen with incomplete checklist). State in `User.settings_json.tour` via `/me/preferences`. Finish CTA: setup chat (primary) or Communication (secondary). Replay: **Product tour** on the Help hub (`/settings/help`). Setup-chat deep-link uses `talkToAssistantPath` + `autosend=1`. Copy in i18n namespace `tour` (EN/NL).
- **Help hub + slim account menu (2026-09, labels 2026-10):** the avatar menu is **Profile**, **Settings**, **Workspaces**, **Support**, then **Sign out** (language stays on Profile; notifications / channels / inbox AI / tour / resume setup left the menu). Settings sidebar ends with a plain accent **Support** link to `/settings/help` (`HelpHubPage`): setup guide, product tour, `/docs`, `/docs/api`, plus contact options. Topbar **Help** / **Hulp** (next to **Setup** / **Aan de slag**) force-opens the in-app Bokito helper via `openAssistant()` even when Communication hides the FAB over an open thread. Command palette **Open help (Bokito)** opens the widget; **Open support** goes to `/settings/help`. In-app Learn stays on `/learn` for page-guide banners.
- **Agent-initiated human handoff (2026-08):** external conversations (widget/inbound, `trust="external"`) can always escalate to a human via the `handoff_to_human` tool (`tools/builtin.py`, category `messaging`, `gated=False` so escalation never waits on approval). The tool holds the conversation at AI handling `manual` (reason `handoff_requested`) + `has_unread`, writes an `ai_handling_changed` SignalEvent (`via: handoff_to_human`), publishes a thread update, and alerts owners/admins through `notify_tenant_admins` (pref category `handoff`, in `DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_ROWS`). Availability is guaranteed for external sessions: `AgentLoop` appends the tool when the passport allowlist omits it and `execute_tool` skips the passport check for `handoff_to_human` + external trust (operator sessions still enforce the passport). External sessions also get a `## Human handoff` system-prompt section instructing the agent to hand off instead of claiming it cannot reach a human. Operators take over via the existing thread-header toggle; the widget keeps polling so the takeover continues in the same conversation.
- **Policy (Phase 3, 2026-06):** unified tool layer in `apps/api/app/tools/` — `registry.py` (ToolSpec + categories), `policy.py` (allowance engine), `builtin.py` (all tools), `executor.py` (single execution path), `decision_copy.py` (human-readable gate titles/summaries). Each tool category (`messaging|workspace|projects|agents|delegation|channels|triggers|integrations|govern|tickets`) has an allowance slider `deny|ask|allow` stored in tenant settings (`tool_allowances`, `tool_overrides`). Resolution layers: posture preset → category slider → agent passport (`autonomy_level` manual caps at ask, auto lifts ask→allow) → per-tool override ("Always allow" writes `tool_overrides`; for MCP that is `mcp:{server}:{tool}` so one allow does not unlock all `call_mcp_tool`) → trust clamp (external/widget sessions never auto-mutate; agents/channels/triggers/integrations/govern denied entirely). **MCP discovery** (`list_tools`, `list_resources`, `list_prompts`, `ping`, …) auto-allows without a DecisionRequest. Gated tool cards use plain-language titles like `Approve: List tools on mock-tools` instead of raw JSON; the dashboard also rewrites legacy JSON summaries via `tool-decision-copy.ts`. Legacy `ActionPolicy`/`ActionWhitelistEntry`/apply-modes deleted (tables dropped by `schema_patch`).
- **Workspace (Phase 4, 2026-06):** markdown memory replaces the Blueprint stack. `WorkspaceDoc` (tenant-scoped, file-style `path` like `skills/triage.md`, kinds `doc|memory|persona|skill|daily_log|heartbeat`, frontmatter parsed into JSON) + `DocChunk` (per-section embedding chunks, JSON-stored vectors) power hybrid (vector + keyword) retrieval. API: `GET/POST /api/workspace/docs`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/workspace/docs/{id}`, `POST /api/workspace/search`. Agent tools: `list_docs`, `read_doc`, `write_doc` (governed via `workspace_doc` PlatformChange + `platform:doc:write` scope), `search_index` (hybrid search). Agent context = persona doc + long-term memory (`memory.md`) + compact skills list (SKILL.md pattern: only name/description injected, body read on demand) + RAG hits. **Thread compaction:** when a Signal thread exceeds 40 LLM-eligible messages, older turns are summarized into `Signal.compact_summary` (last 12 stay verbatim) and durable facts are flushed to `memory.md` first. Tenant bootstrap seeds `persona.md`, `memory.md`, `company.md`, `heartbeat.md`. Dashboard: markdown editor/viewer at `/workspace` (`WorkspaceDocs.tsx`, kind-grouped list, search, frontmatter display). Legacy `blueprint_*`/`index_chunks` tables migrate to `workspace_docs` and are dropped by `schema_patch`.
- **Tenant introspection (2026-07):** Agents receive a compact live `## Tenant snapshot` block in `build_workspace_context` (agents, projects, enabled triggers with schedule/last status, open decisions/tasks/internal threads, connected integrations/MCP — target ~800 chars). Read-only tools in `builtin.py` (gated=False, mutating=False): `get_tenant_overview`, `list_recent_activity`, `list_tasks`, `get_usage_summary`, `list_threads` — implemented in `services/tenant_introspection.py` and reusing `cockpit_summary` / `usage_breakdown`. Default assistant system prompt nudges the model to call these before claiming lack of info. **Roadmap gaps:** strategy/project docs under `strategy/` are not auto-injected (agents must `read_doc` / `search_index`); default-tenant `company.md` can still be a one-line stub; `platform:read` scope exists for future tighter gating of these reads.
- **Bulk thread cleanup via MCP (2026-10):** `list_threads` accepts `older_than_days` (activity = coalesce last_message/updated/created), returns `matched`/`returned`/`limit` (max 200; previously hard-capped at 50 which hid stale pages). `close_threads` closes a page by `older_than_days` and/or `signal_ids` (optional `dry_run`, `note`, channel/status filters); same messaging category gate as `close_thread`. Call again while `truncated` is true.
- **Contacts, workforce and decision tools (2026-09, MCP wave 2):** the registry exposes the remaining canonical objects to agents and MCP clients. CRM: `list_contacts` (query matches name/email/company, merged aliases excluded), `get_contact` (adds `thread_count`), `upsert_contact` (email-keyed; only the fields you pass are overwritten) — new module `apps/api/app/tools/contacts.py`, category `messaging`, reusing `services/signals.get_or_create_contact` + `services/companies.link_contact_company`. Workforce reads in `builtin.py` (`gated=False`): `list_agents` / `get_agent` (id or slug; detail adds purpose + tool passport) and `list_playbooks` / `get_playbook` (Workstream + ordered steps via `services/workstreams`) under category `agents`, plus `list_triggers` under `triggers`. Govern: `resolve_decision(decision_id, action=approve|reject|defer, chosen_option_id?)` wraps `services/notifications.resolve_decision`; it is `consequential=True`, so a call never resolves directly — it lands as its own decision card and only runs after a human approves. Approving requires `chosen_option_id` whenever the card offers more than one real option.
- **Orchestra foundation:** superseded in cycle 2 — Workstream(+steps) remain as definitions, execution is `RuntimeProfile` + `AgentTask`/`AgentRun` under `/api/orchestration/*` (see Backend consolidation above).
- **Triggers + routing (Phase 5, 2026-06):** one `Trigger` model (`triggers` table) replaces orchestra `Task` (orchestra_tasks), `AutomationTemplate`, and the Agenda stack (`agenda_calendars`/`agenda_events`, `/api/agenda/*`, dashboard `/agenda/:view` — all deleted; schema patch migrates enabled schedules into triggers and drops the tables). Kinds: `cron` (5-field expr) | `interval` (minutes) | `heartbeat` | `webhook` (public `POST /api/hooks/{id}`, per-trigger secret revealed once at creation). Firing: workstream-bound triggers start an `AgentTask`; otherwise the resolved agent (`agent_id` or `agent_role` fallback) runs one `AgentLoop` turn with the instructions. Heartbeat wakes embed all `heartbeat`-kind workspace docs as checklist; a reply of exactly `HEARTBEAT_OK` is suppressed, anything else posts to an internal Signal thread (Messages). In-process scheduler `TRIGGER_SCHEDULER_ENABLED` (default on, 60s tick) + ARQ `process_due_triggers_job`. API: `GET/POST /api/triggers`, `PATCH/DELETE /api/triggers/{id}`, `POST /api/triggers/{id}/run`. UI: Orchestra page Triggers tab. Tenant bootstrap seeds a disabled 30-min heartbeat trigger. **ChannelBinding** (`channel_bindings`) routes inbound signals deterministically: most specific enabled binding wins (contact > channel account > channel-wide, by priority), fallback = active assistant agent; used by the inbound worker, widget, livechat, and assistant chat (`services/routing.py`). API: `GET/POST /api/channels/bindings`, `DELETE /api/channels/bindings/{id}`.
- **Cockpit:** `GET /api/cockpit/summary` KPIs including time saved estimate and latest `EvalScore` autonomy metric. **Tijd bespaard** = actie-creditmatrix (`app/services/time_saved.py`, iets gunstig gekalibreerd): autonome reply 15, concept ongewijzigd 12, concept bewerkt 8, concept+mens door 9, no-reply triage 3, no-reply bevestigd 7, ticket door agent/triage 7, flow-run completed 10; response bevat `time_saved_breakdown`. Overview AI-activiteit: KPI-kaarten + tokengrafiek links (2/3), donut rechts (1/3).
- **Knowledge + cost attribution MVP (2026-09):** Knowledge pages and chunks carry an `internal` flag; agent retrieval excludes non-internal items. Operators can mark only closed conversations as approved few-shot examples through `POST /api/signals/{id}/example`; up to three recent examples enter agent context. `AgentRun` and `UsageLedger` can attribute work to `signal_type_id` and `workstream_run_id` alongside agent/run metadata.
- **Send-money/API MVP (2026-09):** Signal Types carry `send_mode=draft|ask|send` with safe default `draft`; `send_reply` resolves this type-level gate before sending. `ToolSpec.consequential` is an absolute ask gate that posture, passport, and tool overrides cannot bypass. Public integration vocabulary is the seven objects Conversation, Signal, Playbook, Project, Contact, Agent and Decision; stable REST remains intentionally small around Conversation/Signal, while webhooks and governed MCP cover richer actions. Metered LLM usage exists per model/agent/user; workspace membership is the seat boundary.
- **Learning modules MVP (2026-09):** Feedback accepts structured `correction_key` and metadata; draft edits can be recorded as feedback. Three matching `type:<id>:<label>` corrections create a pending Govern `case_type` description edit. `POST /api/learning/modules/save` stores a versionable internal module manifest and `/modules/{slug}/diff` returns key-level changes.
- **Contacts + widget MVP (2026-09):** Contacts are people and Companies are organizations. Exact normalized addresses auto-link new cross-channel identity aliases; owner/admin merge relinks threads and split creates a new person for selected threads. The website widget lists only the visitor's own conversations and no longer shows a shared Available/Away status pill; availability is communicated through message behavior. Anonymous widget visitors (`cust_*` / `visitor@…` / generic “Website visitor”) are conversation identities, not address-book contacts, until a real email or phone is linked; they stay **pending** if a stub row already exists, have no Profile, and are omitted from the Contacts list. The contact panel shows one header name and lists “Also seen as website visitor” as a secondary channel line rather than a second identity. Closing a conversation with open typed Signals prompts resolve-or-leave-open (F-49). Agenda defaults to **My look-ats** (follow-ups + calendar); agent cron/heartbeat scans collapse under All items (F-80). Empty Snoozed/Spam/Closed folders use queue empty copy, not the setup checklist (F-63).
- **Contact identity (2026-10):** One contact is one person. Every channel address (widget visitor key, email, WhatsApp number, phone) is an identity: a `Contact` row whose `merged_into_id` points at the person; `services/contact_identity.py` owns the model (`normalize_email`, `normalize_phone` with the workspace-language country code for a leading 0, `canonical`, `find_person`, `resolve_link`, `apply_link`, `unlink`). Linking adds identities and never overwrites one. `Signal.contact_basis` records how a thread was linked: `verified` (magic link), `claimed` (visitor typed it), `manual` (teammate); empty means matched on the inbound address. A claimed link never changes `assurance_level`. Paths: `POST/DELETE /api/signals/{id}/contact-link` (manual; merging two known persons needs owner/admin), the agent tool `link_conversation_contact` (always injected for external sessions, uniform `{"status": "noted"}` answer), decision action types `contact_link` / `contact_create`, and `consume_verify_token` (links as verified unless that would merge two known persons). AI handling matrix: Manual does nothing; Assisted links verified and asks for claimed, ambiguous or new; Autonomous links verified and claimed and creates on no match; ambiguous or merge always asks. Inbound `_resolve_contact` returns the canonical person, and a new email address or WhatsApp number that matches exactly one person becomes an identity of that person. `PATCH /api/channels/contacts/{id}` with `address` refuses visitor and non-email rows (400) and checks duplicates tenant-wide. `GET /contacts/{id}` returns `identities`; `/threads` includes threads of every identity. Unlink restores the previous contact, removes created rows that have no threads, and records `Feedback(correction_key="contact_link")` when an agent made the link. `apply_link` / unlink write a `system_event` message plus a `contact_linked` / `contact_unlinked` SignalEvent; the message says **Contact created** / **Contact aangemaakt** when a new person was made, otherwise **Linked to** / **Gekoppeld aan**. The timeline shows that body as a centered activity pill (same chrome as "AI gepauzeerd") and hides the duplicate SignalEvent. The contact panel uses **+ Contact** until linked (one path: in-panel link/create; no second add-contact, Mail, or Open contacts on the unmatched empty state). Unmatched senders also get **Mark as spam** / **Not spam** there (`PATCH` thread status), same as the thread ⋯ menu. Block lives under the thread ⋯ menu; the contact page lists **Also reachable at**.
- **Autonomy growth MVP (2026-09):** Completing owner onboarding idempotently installs starter Signal Types. Strong autonomy evaluation (at least 80% over 10 actions) proposes per-Signal-Type and per-Playbook `approval -> auto` changes in Govern; it never applies them silently. `GET /api/cockpit/trajectory` returns stored autonomy-rate points for the Overview trajectory line.
- **Custom metrics / Jouw cijfers (removed 2026-09):** tenant-defined Cockpit KPIs (`CustomMetric` / `CustomMetricPoint`, `/api/metrics`, `record_metric` / `list_metrics` tools, Cockpit `CustomMetricsSection`) were hard-removed. Overview keeps built-in cards only; leftover `custom_metrics` / `custom_metric_points` tables are dropped by `schema_patch`.
- **Correction loop (2026-08):** operators can correct an agent's interpretation from the inbox. Agent messages keep thumbs (existing `POST /api/messages/{id}/feedback`) plus a **Correct** action; decision cards got thumbs (generic `POST /api/learning/feedback`, subject_type `decision`) and a "Correct {agent}'s interpretation" action. Both use `lib/correction-chat.ts` (`useCorrectionChat`): it records a down-sentiment feedback row, creates a chat conversation with the responsible company agent (`agent_id` from the message payload; if none, the operator must pick a company agent on New chat) grounded in the source thread via `context_signal_id` (the agent loop injects the full transcript), and auto-sends a correction prompt instructing the agent to ask clarifying questions and persist the learning (via `write_doc` and `suggest_inbox_rule`).
- **Truth fixes (gap-pass phase 1, 2026-08):** notification prefs gate every emission: `notification_channels` returns a `slack` key (gates decision Slack DMs), decision push respects the `decisions` desktop pref, and in-app decision Notification rows for a specific user are created with status from `decision_bell_status` (pref off = born `read`, so no bell noise/realtime ping while keeping the DB anchor). The unused `mobile` channel was removed from prefs. Cockpit/Usage format costs in USD (ledger currency); paused agents no longer fire triggers (`_resolve_agent` checks `is_active`, `last_status="agent_paused"` surfaced in Agenda); WhatsApp UI was hidden until the adapter shipped (2026-08, see WhatsApp inbox channel entry); messenger module toggles (home/messages/help/tools — changelog removed) actually drive widget tabs through the theme payload; marketplace honesty (shopify_mcp `coming_soon`, Outlook no calendar claim, mock OAuth success paths removed); /os leftovers retired (aios locales, stale hrefs).
- **Learning loop closes (gap-pass phase 2, 2026-08):** corrections and thumbs now feed real automation. (1) Agent tool `suggest_inbox_rule` (ungated, in `builtin.py`) lets agents propose an InboxRule (sender/domain/list_id x auto_close/auto_task/mute_ai) via `inbox_rules.suggest_rule` — the rule lands as `suggested` (source `agent`, audit `inbox_rule:suggest`) and a human activates it in Inbox settings; active/paused rules are never overwritten. (2) `learning.suggest_rules_from_feedback` (in the learning cycle) clusters 7-day thumbs-down on messages per email sender; 3+ distinct threads (PROMOTION_THRESHOLD) suggest a `mute_ai` rule for that sender. (3) Thread-read scores still land on `serialize_thread` (`category`/`urgency`/`certainty`/`ai_summary`/`triaged_at`); the operator-facing summary is the timeline `triaged` pill (not a side-panel Sparkles block). (4) `certainty_threshold` (1-10) still clamps priority and auto-tag certainty in `apply_thread_read`. Dead InboxSettings fields `rules_text`/`labeling_enabled` dropped (Alembic `009_inbox_settings_slim`).
- **Persona single-store (gap-pass phase 2, 2026-08):** the `assistant_personas` table is gone (Alembic `008_persona_doc` folds non-empty rows into the doc and drops the table). Persona lives only in the `persona.md` workspace doc: `services/persona.py` parses/renders structured `## Tone` / `## Do` / `## Don't` sections (unmanaged sections like appended feedback reviews are preserved on save); the Messenger-settings Tone/Do/Don't form round-trips through `GET/PUT /api/persona` backed by that doc; agents already read it via `build_workspace_context` (kind `persona`), so edits take effect on the next agent run. Govern `persona_review` approvals now apply something concrete: `apply_persona_review_change` appends the proposal's `proposed_addition` (built from negative-feedback comments in `learning.propose_persona_review`) as a dated `## Feedback review YYYY-MM-DD` section to persona.md.
- **Intelligence Stack (2026-06 restructure):** Product backbone mapped to Salim Ismail layers — visible in OS Graph pipeline UX (hybrid IA), not as separate rail items per layer.
  - **SENSING:** unified `signals` table + inbox-parity `GET/PATCH/POST /api/signals/*` (assign, pin, reply, queues including `awaiting_decision` and `outbound`, folder filters). Email mailbox drill-down uses `email_connection_id` (numeric id from `EmailAccount` UUID) and `Signal.email_account_id`. Sync status in bokito mode: `GET /api/signals/sync-status`. Dashboard Messages hub uses `signals-api.ts` when `VITE_API_MODE=bokito`. Seed creates Signal-only demo threads (no duplicate legacy `inbox_threads`). `create_decision_request` ingests into internal Signal threads.
  - **INTERPRETATION:** channel agent tool `record_thread_read` → `interpretation.apply_thread_read` (summary/priority/certainty/tags/ticket/backlog); certainty threshold from inbox policy; no separate triage LLM in the worker.
  - **GOVERN & ASSURE:** Govern has two operator sections: **Ledger** (pending `PlatformChange` reviews, accepted version history, audit events) and **Autonomy** (workspace posture, per-category/tool allowances, per-Signal-type and per-playbook levels, agent access). Active `CaseType` and `Workstream` rows expose `manual|approval|auto` controls through `GET/PATCH /api/govern/autonomy-scopes`. Accepted changes can be reverted for 30 days; revert applies a new compensating `PlatformChange` and preserves the original ledger entry. `AuditEvent` log (`GET /api/govern/audit`), agent passports (`GET /api/govern/passports` with `permission_scopes_json` + role defaults), `PlatformChange` draft queue (`GET /api/govern/changes`, accept/reject, `POST .../rollback`), **allowance sliders** (`GET/PUT /api/govern/allowances`: `deny|ask|allow` per tool category, plus per-tool `PUT /api/govern/tool-overrides`), **Autonomy Posture** presets (`GET/PUT /api/govern/posture`: `manual|assisted|autonomous` — sets the category defaults; default `assisted`; autonomous keeps `integrations` at `ask`), and **API tokens** (`GET/POST/DELETE /api/govern/tokens`, SHA-256 stored, optional category scopes) for the MCP server. Workspace roles are canonically `owner|admin|member`; legacy labels are soft-mapped at authorization and API presentation boundaries. Trust & privacy exposes one workspace retention period for messages, calendar events, and audit data plus personal-data JSON export.
  - **Platform self-maintenance:** orchestrator/workstream agents propose structural edits via registry tools (`create_agent`, `update_agent`, `create_workstream`, `update_workstream`, `register_mcp_server`, `connect_integration`, `propose_integration`, `add_graph_node`, `connect_graph_nodes`, `write_doc`). The allowance engine resolves `deny|ask|allow` up front; `propose_platform_change(mode="apply"|"ask")` then either applies immediately (status `applied_yolo`, audit + rollback record) or creates a pending `PlatformChange` + inline `DecisionRequest`. Scopes enforced via `platform_access.py`; update tools capture a `before` snapshot; `propose_integration` always escalates to a human decision; PO agents may only mutate workstreams in their own project.
  - **MCP server (Phase 3 + OAuth maturity 2026-09):** Streamable HTTP at `/api/mcp` — `POST` JSON-RPC (`initialize`, `ping`, `tools/*`, `resources/*`, `prompts/*`), `GET` idle SSE (`Accept: text/event-stream`), `DELETE` session teardown. Responses set `Mcp-Session-Id`. `serverInfo` includes icons/title/websiteUrl; tools advertise MCP annotations (`readOnlyHint`, `destructiveHint`, …); `resources/read` serves product-help markdown. **Primary auth:** MCP OAuth 2.1 — paste only `{origin}/api/mcp` in Cursor; `401` carries `WWW-Authenticate` + `resource_metadata` at **`/api/oauth/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource`** (under `/api` so Caddy proxies it; root `/.well-known/*` alone used to return SPA HTML). Issuer `{origin}/api/oauth`; OIDC at `/api/oauth/.well-known/openid-configuration`; also `/api/mcp/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` (RFC 9728 path insert) and root `/.well-known/oauth-*`. AS: DCR `/register`, PKCE S256 `/authorize`, consent `/oauth/mcp` (tenant picker + scope chips), `/token` (auth code + refresh), `/revoke`. Opaque `bok_oa_…` / `bok_or_…` bind user+tenant+scopes+**resource** (RFC 8707); refresh rotation with **family revoke on reuse**. Operator revoke UI: `GET/DELETE /api/oauth/grants…` under **Settings → Developers → Connected MCP clients**. Open CORS (`McpOAuthCorsMiddleware`) for `/api/mcp`, `/api/oauth`, and well-known paths. Cursor redirects: `cursor://…`, `https://cursor.com/api/mcp/oauth/callback`, `https://www.cursor.com/agents/mcp/oauth/callback`, `http://localhost:8787/callback`. **Fallback:** `bok_…` API tokens. Logo in Cursor MCP list needs `plugins/bokito-mcp`; bare `mcp.json` shows the generic pin.
  - **LEARNING:** `Feedback` + `EvalScore` models; `POST /api/learning/feedback`, `POST /api/learning/process`, `POST /api/learning/eval/compute`, `GET /api/learning/eval`. Heuristic eval (autonomy rate, escalation rate, CSAT proxy) — no ML fine-tuning.
  - **Orchestration (2026-06):** Background agent tasks via `/api/orchestration/*`. Canonical entities: `RuntimeProfile` (model/tools preset per planner|executor|judge), `AgentTask` (durable work unit linked to internal `Signal` thread), `EvalCheckpoint` (rubric|tool_assert|llm_judge), `TaskArtifact` (step outputs). `AgentRun` extended with `task_id`, `step_id`, `parent_run_id`, `segment_index`, `runtime_snapshot_json`, `checkpoint_json`. Workstream steps bind `agent_id` + optional `runtime_profile_id`; runner enqueues ARQ segment jobs (`run_agent_task_segment`) or runs inline when Redis unavailable / `BOKITO_MOCK_EXECUTION=true`. `POST /api/workforce/trigger-agent` and `POST /api/triggers/{id}/run` invoke the dispatcher (no empty runs). Multi-agent handoff: each step runs a different agent/model; outputs pass via `handoff_template` + `context_json.step_outputs`. SSE: `GET /api/orchestration/runs/{id}/events/stream` (EventSource cannot set headers, so `get_current_auth` also accepts an `?access_token=` query token). Cost/context governance: each segment emits a `context_usage` `RunEvent` (context window %, tokens, cents) and the runner accumulates `context_json.cost_cents`; when a profile's `max_cost_cents` is exceeded the task is paused with `pause_reason="budget_exceeded"` (resume via `POST /tasks/{id}/resume`). Automation templates install per tenant: `POST /api/orchestration/automation-templates/{id}/install` creates an `orchestra_tasks` row (interval schedule) from the template; `GET /api/orchestration/automations` lists installed ones and `POST /api/orchestration/automations/{id}/run` triggers immediately. Messages hub: `ThreadDetail` shows an inline `OrchestrationPanel` (status, resume/cancel, context+cost meters, artifacts) when a thread's signal has a linked `AgentTask` (`GET /tasks?signal_id=`). Orchestra admin page exposes Templates, Automations, and Runtime profiles tabs. Cancel walks the `parent_run_id` tree. Bootstrap seeds planner/executor/judge profiles + demo **Ops Triage Pipeline** workstream per tenant.
  - **Decisions:** unified on `DecisionRequest`; `/api/workforce/messages` is a compat layer; approve executes tools or accepts linked `PlatformChange`.
  - **Real OAuth (2026-06):** authorization-code flow for GitHub, Google (Gmail) and Microsoft (Outlook), gated on env credentials with mock fallback. `app/services/oauth_providers.py` builds authorize URLs, exchanges codes, refreshes tokens and fetches identity; `app/services/oauth_flow.py` persists a one-shot `OAuthState` row (`oauth_states`) and on callback stores tokens into `ChannelAccount.credentials_json` (email) or `IntegrationConnection.credentials_json` (github/integration). Single redirect URI for all providers: `GET /api/integrations/oauth/callback` (302s back to the dashboard return URL with the same success/error params the frontend already parses). Start endpoints (`/api/email/*/oauth/start`, `/api/integrations/oauth/start`, `/api/github/oauth/start`) try `start_real_oauth` first and fall back to `mock_authorize_url` when a provider's client id/secret are empty. Env: `PUBLIC_API_URL`, `PUBLIC_APP_URL`, `{GITHUB,GOOGLE,MICROSOFT}_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET`, `MICROSOFT_OAUTH_TENANT`. GitHub repos/branches return live data via the stored token (fallback to mocks). Google project `bokito-ai` already has an OAuth client "Bokito Email Integration" to reuse — just add `{PUBLIC_API_URL}/api/integrations/oauth/callback` to its redirect URIs and set `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET`. **Provisioned creds (2026-06, in `apps/api/.env`, untracked):** Google client `582551847741-42p5…` (reused "Bokito Email Integration"; a new client secret was added — the original Xano secret + redirect `…/api:integrations/oauth/google/callback` are preserved, and `…/api/integrations/oauth/callback` for both `https://api.bokito.ai` and `http://localhost:8000` were added). Microsoft Entra app "Bokito AI OS" `9743ee3e-…` created (multitenant + personal accounts, `tenant=common`, Web redirect = localhost callback; Graph `Mail.ReadWrite`/`Mail.Send`/`User.Read`/`offline_access` are consented dynamically at sign-in via the v2.0 endpoint, no pre-registered API permissions needed). GitHub OAuth App "Bokito AI OS" `Ov23liGBw1CHjPSIWWEU` created with the localhost callback, **but its client secret is still pending** — GitHub gates secret generation behind a sudo "Verify via email" step that requires manual inbox confirmation, so `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` must be generated by hand and pasted into `.env`.
  - **Inbound email sync (2026-07 audit hardening):** `app/services/email_sync.py` uses `ChannelAccount.sync_cursor` for incremental sync — Gmail `historyId` via `users.history.list`, Outlook Graph inbox `delta`/`deltaLink` — with full-fetch fallback when the cursor is empty or stale. Dedup remains on provider message id. `last_sync_at` stays in `settings_json`. Send path (`channels/email.send_via_provider`) refreshes on 401 like sync. Mailbox polling runs on the **ARQ worker cron** (`sync_email_mailboxes_job`, every minute, gated by `EMAIL_SYNC_ENABLED`); the in-process API `trigger_scheduler` only fires due triggers (no duplicate email poll). Settings list (`GET /api/email/accounts`) hides `mock` providers and derives status from credentials (`connected` / `needs_auth` / `paused` / `error`) instead of coercing mock→gmail or treating `is_enabled` as connected. Seed no longer creates a phantom `support@bokito.ai` mock mailbox.
  - **Mailbox sync vs duplicate CRM companies (2026-10):** ingest links contacts by email domain via `get_or_create_company`. Concurrent syncs can create duplicate `(tenant_id, domain)` rows; `scalar_one_or_none()` then crashes with `MultipleResultsFound`, leaving channel health as a vague `Sync failed:` while login/folders still look OK. Fix: pick the oldest live company (ignore soft-deleted), record ingest failures in `last_error` with a real detail, and dedupe existing rows. Seen on `bourgondienadvies` / `harold@bourgondienadvies.nl` for `bjornlunden.com`, `mail.notion.so`, `resend.dev`.
  - **Email received-time fidelity + backfill window (2026-08):** inbound sync stores the provider's actual delivery time — Graph `receivedDateTime` (added to `$select`) and Gmail `internalDate` (epoch ms) — parsed to naive UTC and carried on `InboundMessage.received_at`; `ingest_inbound` stamps `SignalMessage.received_at`/`created_at` and only moves `Signal.last_message_at` forward (backfill never reorders threads). API timestamps from `signal_threads._iso` get a `Z` suffix so browsers stop parsing naive UTC as local time (this caused the "2h ago" offset on fresh mail). Each mailbox has a backfill window `settings_json.sync_window_days` (default 30, 0 = unlimited, max 3650) applied only on the initial fetch (no cursor): Graph adds `$filter=receivedDateTime ge ...` to delta + list, Gmail adds `q=after:{epoch}`; a client-side guard drops out-of-window items on paths without a server filter. Exposed as `sync_window_days` on `GET /api/email/accounts` and updatable via `PUT /api/email/connections/{id}/mailbox-settings` (which now only touches keys present in the body); the Email settings table has a "History" column (7/30/90 days, 1 year, Everything).
  - **Full email text for agents (2026-08):** HTML-only email (most support desks) has no `text/plain` MIME part; the Gmail parser previously fell back to the provider `snippet` (~200 chars) for `body_text`, so agents/triage saw mail "cut off mid-sentence" while the UI rendered the full `body_html`. The parser now derives text via `html_to_text` before falling back to the snippet (Graph already did this), and `services/signals.message_plain_text()` repairs already-synced rows at read time (prefers HTML-derived text when it is >120 chars fuller). Used by the inbound agent prompt (`process_inbound_signal`), triage, `serialize_message`, and the assistant transcript/history builders.
  - **Email AI suggestions MVP (2026-07):** Suggest-only inbound email flow. Worker `process_inbound_signal` skips when the resolved AI handling is `manual` (superseded the old `Signal.ai_paused` / `suggestions_enabled` checks). For email, the AgentLoop runs with an **empty toolset** (no `create_decision_request` / `send_email`) and `persist_inbound_agent_reply` creates a single inline `DecisionRequest` ("Suggested reply" with Send / Edit / Escalate). Approve Send runs builtin `send_email` → `deliver_outbound`. Escalate holds the conversation at AI handling `manual` (reason `escalated`) and writes an escalated system event. Resolve API accepts `option_id` + edited `body`/`subject`. Dashboard `DecisionRequestMessage` + mobile `MessageBubble` send `option_id`; Edit prefills the reply composer. Settings: `/settings/channels` (mailboxes) and `/settings/communication` (AI toggles; dirty-state save). Prod ops: `scripts/ops/vps-set-microsoft-oauth.py`; Entra redirect `https://app.bokito.ai/api/integrations/oauth/callback`. Local live-data bridge: `scripts/dev/prod-tunnel.ps1` + `scripts/dev/api-live.ps1` + `apps/api/.env.prodbridge`.
  - **Session refresh + 401 recovery (2026-08):** access tokens live 60 min (`access_token_expire_minutes`); the dashboard refreshes proactively 5 min before expiry. Sessions restored from `sessionStorage` schedule that refresh from the JWT `exp` claim (`jwtRemainingSeconds` in `lib/auth-retry.ts`) instead of silently never refreshing. A global fetch interceptor (`configureAuthRetry`, installed once by `AuthContext`) catches 401s on `/api/*` calls that carried a Bearer token (excluding `/api/auth/*`), performs one single-flight cookie refresh, and retries the request with the new token (marker header `x-bokito-auth-retry` prevents loops); when refresh fails the session is cleared so route guards redirect to login. The gateway WebSocket already backs off on `unauthorized` and reconnects with whatever token the auth layer holds.
  - **Integration connect suggestions with brand identity (2026-08):** `suggest_integration` decisions carry the provider slug in the connect option payload; titles use the catalog display name (`PROVIDER_BY_SLUG`, e.g. "Connect GitHub?"). Both decision surfaces render the provider brand logo via `IntegrationHostLogo` + `resolveProviderBrand` (inbox `DecisionRequestMessage` and assistant-chat `ChatDecisionCard`; the chat serializer `serialize_decision_for_chat` exposes `provider` per option). Approving "Connect now" resolves the decision and deep-links to `/settings/marketplace?connect={provider}`, which opens the connect hub for that integration.
  - **Automated / no-reply mail handling (2026-08):** the AI never drafts a reply to automated mail. Detection has two layers in `services/automated_mail.py`: (1) deterministic heuristics — sender local-part patterns (`noreply`, `donotreply`, `notifications@`, `alerts@`, `mailer-daemon`, `postmaster`, `bounce`, `newsletter`, …) plus RFC auto-mail headers captured at sync time (`auto_headers` in message metadata: `Auto-Submitted`, `Precedence: bulk/list`, `X-Auto-Response-Suppress`, `List-Id`/`List-Unsubscribe`, null `Return-Path`); Gmail supplies these from `format=full` headers, Graph only when `internetMessageHeaders` happens to be present; (2) a prompt guardrail — suggest and auto prompts instruct the model to return `NO_REPLY_NEEDED: <summary>` for automated notifications it recognizes (parsed by `extract_no_reply_summary`). **Heuristic path (2026-08 UX):** `acknowledge_automated_mail` writes a quiet `no_reply_noted` timeline event and leaves the thread open — it does **not** create an awaiting decision (busy personal mailboxes were flooding Agents / Cockpit with 100+ fake blockers). **Agent-judged path:** still calls `create_action_suggestion` ("No reply needed" card with Close / Create task / Keep open). Those tip cards are **excluded** from `agents_attention`, Cockpit `open_decisions`, `has_open_decision`, and the Decisions (`awaiting_decision`) queue; badge payload adds `no_reply_suggestions`. Operators clear a backlog via `POST /api/signals/dismiss-no-reply-suggestions`. The Agents library no longer shows workspace-wide decision/tip banners; each card shows per-agent `open_conversations` / `awaiting_decision` and links to `/agents/{id}#conversations`. Active inbox rules still short-circuit before classification. Dashboard tip cards remain calm (BellOff). Preview excerpts use `clip_with_ellipsis`.
  - **Live chat human takeover (2026-06):** widget threads support staff↔visitor chat end-to-end. `POST /api/signals/{id}/takeover` and `/release` toggle `Signal.ai_paused` (serialized as `ai_paused` on threads). When paused, the widget SSE stream (`livechat_stream.widget_stream_events`) stores the visitor message and returns a human-handoff `done` event instead of running the AI. Staff replies via `/api/signals/{id}/reply` publish on the `signal:{id}` gateway topic; the widget (`gatewayFrameToWidgetEvent`) now renders outbound `role=user` messages as a human agent bubble (`sender_type=agent`). Dashboard `ThreadDetail` shows a Take over / Hand back to AI toggle for `widget`/`chat`/`assistant`/`email` channels (`useThreadDetail.toggleTakeover`).
  - **Still pending:** Gmail push / Graph webhook subscriptions (polling covers MVP); production SMTP for auth mail. **Done:** encrypt OAuth tokens at rest (`CREDENTIALS_FERNET_KEY`, see §37); autonomous auto-reply mode exists via Govern posture.
  - **Wiring pass (2026-06):** dashboard features that previously 404'd are now wired to existing models. Path fixes: integrations router de-duplicated to `/api/integrations/*`; GitHub router moved to `/api/github/*`; workspaces/invites/members use app-scoped helpers (`appScopedGet/Post/Patch/Delete` → `/api/app/*`). Email send accepts the full compose DTO (reply when `thread_id`, else new outbound thread). Mailbox settings, signature, AI-config persist into `ChannelAccount.settings_json`; disconnect hard-deletes the account; all email handlers + OAuth start live on the `/email` router. **Routing rules (retired legacy):** inbound routing uses `InboxRule` only; `EmailRoutingRule`, `/api/email/routing-rules`, and the separate per-mailbox rule editor are removed. **Knowledge base:** `/api/kb/*` (collections + documents + keyword search) backed by `WorkspaceDoc` (`kind` `kb_collection`/`kb_doc`, metadata in `frontmatter_json`). **Auth mail:** `AuthToken` table (`auth_tokens`, kinds `password_reset`/`email_verify`, single-use, prior unused tokens invalidated) + `User.email_verified`; endpoints `/auth/password-reset-request`, `/auth/password-reset`, `/auth/verify-email`, `/auth/resend-verification`. Non-production responses include `dev_token`/`dev_link` (logged) so flows work without SMTP. **Workforce:** `force-rescan` fires due triggers for the tenant (`process_due_triggers(tenant_id)`); `maintenance-run` calls `clear_stale_runtime` (resets stale active agents to standby, fails long-running runs). **Settings:** workspace save/delete persist via `Tenant.settings_json` (incl. `require_2fa`); `delete_workspace` cascade-deletes tenant-scoped rows; messenger module toggles persist inside `MessengerAppearance` branding JSON. **Legacy `/email/messages*`:** removed from dashboard — orphaned `components/communication/*` deleted; inbox uses Signals API + `/email/threads`. **Auth UI:** `/verify-email?token=…` page + profile resend when `email_verified` is false; `/auth/me` exposes `email_verified`. **Nav badges:** `GET /api/signals/badge-counts` replaces four thread-list fetches.
- **Widget:** the only widget API is `/api/livechat/*` (`session/start`, `stream-chat`, `stream-chat-continue`, `attachment`, `me`, `user/*`, `customer/conversations`, `conversation` POST/GET + `conversation/{id}/messages`); the embed is `apps/chat-widget/dist/bokito-chat.js`, served at `/chat-widget/bokito-chat.js`. `packages/messenger-ui` is fully deleted (2026-07, cycle 6); its API types were inlined into `apps/dashboard/src/lib/bokito-api.ts`.
- **Widget branding + taal (2026-08):** de widgetnaam is dynamisch — keten `appearance.chatbot_name` (expliciete override) → companyagent-naam (mits aangepast; het generieke bootstrap-"Assistant"/"Assistent" telt niet) → `tenant.name`. "Bokito AI" verschijnt nooit meer op tenant-widgets. De theme-payload (`livechat_theme_from_tenant`, meegegeven `assistant_name` door `session/start`) bevat nu ook `locale` (uit `resolve_workspace_language`) en gelokaliseerde welcome-defaults wanneer titel/ondertitel leeg zijn (nl: "Welkom"/"Hoe kunnen we je helpen?", en: "Welcome"/"How can we help?"; overige talen → en). Tenant-bootstrap zaait geen Engelse welcome-teksten of `chatbot_name` meer: leeg = default tot de tenant het aanpast. Widget-chrome volgt `theme.locale` (fallback embed-attribuut/paginataal); `WIDGET_CHROME` (en/nl) dekt nu ook composer-placeholder, pre-chatformulier, CSAT, offline-/handoff-banners, lijst-states, chat-acties, sign-in-panel en "Powered by". De statische widget-HTML bevat geen hardcoded naam/welkomsttekst meer (geen taalflits); de Tools-tab is verborgen tot de sessie echt tools levert. `MessengerSettings`-placeholders tonen de resolved naam (`resolveWidgetName` in `messenger-appearance.ts`) en dezelfde gelokaliseerde welcome-defaults (teamtaal via AI-communication settings).
- **Widget unread badges (2026-08):** de livechat-lijstendpoints (`user/conversations`, `customer/conversations`) leveren `unread_count`: agent-berichten nieuwer dan het laatst-gezien-moment of de laatste eigen reply. "Gezien" wordt per viewer bijgehouden als geüpdatet `SignalEvent` `event_type="widget_seen"` (actor `user:{id}` of `customer:{id}`); het ophalen van `conversation/{id}/messages` geldt als leesmoment en reset de badge. De launcher-badge hangt buiten de cirkel (`overflow:visible` op `.bk-launcher`); `overflow:hidden` knipte hem eerder af.
- **Widget image vision (2026-08 fix):** the widget sends full attachment metadata (`{id, url, mime, name, size}`) with `stream-chat`; the backend (`AgentLoop._apply_attachments_to_messages`) inlines images as base64 vision blocks. Because customer sites may cache old widget bundles that sent bare `{id, url}`, the loop is defensive: missing mime falls back to an extension guess (`_attachment_mime`) and, failing that, the bytes are fetched and magic-byte sniffed (`_sniff_image_mime`, jpeg/png/gif/webp) before deciding image vs "[Attached files: ...]" note. The inbox renderer (`MessageAttachments`) treats missing mime the same way (extension on name/url) so a stored `{id, url}` attachment no longer crashes the thread view with `mime.startsWith`. Widget uploads are image-only (10MB cap) via `/api/livechat/.../attachment`.
- **Gateway reconnect (2026-07, cycle 7):** the dashboard gateway client only resets its reconnect backoff after the server's `connected` frame (not on socket open) and backs off to the max delay on an `unauthorized` error frame; an expired JWT no longer causes a 1-second reconnect storm against `/api/ws`.
- **Mobile:** native Expo app at `apps/mobile` on the gateway protocol (see Fase 7 section).
- **Dashboard bokito mode:** `VITE_API_MODE=bokito`; Vite proxies `/api/*` to FastAPI. Frontend API bases: `APP_API_BASE=/api` (signals, etc.), `APP_SCOPED_API_BASE=/api/app` (custom-db). Rail: Cockpit, **Messages** (`/messages`, unified thread-lijst), **AI OS** (`/os`), Integrations, **Govern** (`/govern`), Settings. Orchestra lives under AI OS sidebar (`/orchestra`), not as its own rail item.
- **Agent setup (2026-06):** Admins/owners can create company worker agents from the UI (`AiAgents` page "New agent" dialog: name, role `assistant|communication|builder|orchestra`, model picker from the workspace catalog, optional system prompt) via `POST /api/workforce/agents`, and edit a company agent's system prompt on the detail page (`AgentInstructionsCard` shows a faded preview; **Edit** opens the full prompt in a modal) via `PATCH /api/workforce/agents/{id}`. Both are admin-gated; `create_agent`/`update_agent` validate the chosen model against the catalog + tenant allowlist. The workforce serializer now exposes `system_prompt`, `chat_access`, and `kind`. Per-agent model rebind stays at `PATCH /api/workforce/agents/{id}/model`.
- **AI OS section:** Single rail item replaces separate Projects and Workforce. **Unified workspace graph canvas** at `/os` (React Flow): draggable nodes for orchestrators, workstreams, repos/sources, and tools with many-to-many edges (`routed_by`, `uses_repo`, `uses_tool`). Overlay tables `os_canvas_nodes` / `os_canvas_edges` store positions and links; domain entities stay in existing tables. Auto-seed on first load from projects/workstreams/repos/integrations. API: `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/workforce/os/nodes`, `POST/DELETE /api/workforce/os/edges`, `GET /api/workforce/os/graph`. Legacy `/os/project/:id` redirects to `/os`. Drill-in keeps `/project/:id/*` detail pages. Canonical agent URLs: `/os/agents`; legacy `/projects`, `/workforce`, `/ai/agents` redirect to `/os` paths. Single `bokito-chat` embed in `main.tsx` (no messenger-ui FAB). Open workspace → `/home`; one membership skips picker; unified inbox tabs (Conversations | Decisions). Branding save: `appearance_json` + `widget_favicon` on `POST /api/auth/workspaces/{id}/branding`; persona `GET/PUT /api/persona`; notification prefs `GET/PATCH /api/user/notification-preferences`. Primary seed `admin@bokito.ai`. Inbox, Blueprint, integrations, projects, workforce, and settings APIs as below. Stale SQLite: `apps/api/DEV_DATABASE.md`; `init_db()` runs `apply_column_patches` (auto-adds missing columns from SQLModel metadata + manual overrides) so `scripts/seed.py` works without deleting `dev.db` when the API is stopped.
- **Local dev:** `docker compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up`; seed `admin@bokito.ai` / `bokito-test-password`. Dashboard: `npm run dev -w bokito-dashboard` on `http://127.0.0.1:5174` with Vite proxy to FastAPI.
- **Tests:** pytest (59+ tests, mock LLM); covers govern, platform changes, signals, learning, decisions, notification preferences, branding `appearance_json`, livechat SSE. CI: `api` (ruff + pytest), `dashboard` build, `mobile` typecheck, `e2e` (Playwright: dashboard login/cockpit/inbox/integrations). Local: `npm run verify:bokito` (api tests + dashboard build) and `npm run verify:bokito:e2e`.
- **Legacy removed (production track):** `apps/runtime`, `packages/docker`, `platform-patches`, `apps/messenger` (PWA, vervangen door de native Expo-app in `apps/mobile`) — niet meer actief in de V1 FastAPI-lijn.

**Chat widget (`apps/chat-widget`):** TypeScript/Vite IIFE (`bokito-chat`, `src/widget-main.ts`) for portal preview, team embed, and external sites. Build: `npm run build` in `apps/chat-widget` → `dist/bokito-chat.js`. Dashboard Vite (and production Caddy) serve the flat path `/chat-widget/bokito-chat.js` from `dist/`. Livechat: Vite proxies `/api/livechat/*` to FastAPI (`session/start`, theme from the website-chat `ChannelAccount.settings_json` appearance, with tenant `livechat_settings.appearance` as legacy fallback). The embed snippet includes `data-tenant` and `data-channel-id`. `POST stream-chat` and `stream-chat-continue` return SSE (`data: {"t":"..."}` chunks and `{"type":"done","content":"..."}`). The widget is the **only** external embed path; there is no parallel messenger-ui embed.

**Broncode (GitHub):** monorepo onder [github.com/BokitoAI/Bokito-AI](https://github.com/BokitoAI/Bokito-AI) (`origin`).

### Competitive landscape (2026-06)

| Competitor | Shape | Bokito contrast |
|------------|-------|-----------------|
| OrchStack, Beam | AI OS + runtime + govern | Bokito leads with unified Messages + inline decisions |
| Relevance AI, Lindy | Visual multi-agent / flows | No unified external+internal inbox |
| Agentforce, Copilot Studio | Enterprise ecosystem agents | Not SMB standalone ops core |
| Front, Intercom | Inbox / chat support | Customer-only; no agent OS or structural govern |
| n8n | Dev workflow canvas | No Govern posture or Messages hub |

Full table: [`docs/POSITIONING.md`](docs/POSITIONING.md).

### Autonomy posture (2026-06)

Tenant preset on Govern **Policy** tab: `manual` | `assisted` (default) | `autonomous`. Sets the per-category allowance defaults via `GET/PUT /api/govern/posture` (changing posture resets explicit category overrides). **Autonomous** keeps `integrations` at `ask` so external connects always need human approval. Per-category fine-tuning via the allowance sliders; per-tool pins via tool overrides.

**Autonoom prerequisites (2026-09):** `PUT /api/govern/posture` with `autonomous` requires a live chat model (managed AI or tenant catalog ready) and at least one send-ready channel (`can_send` via channel registry). Otherwise the API returns 400 and Govern disables the Autonoom card with the same reason. Mock/placeholder AI cannot unlock Autonoom; mock replies are never labeled as delivered to the customer.

---

## 2. Dashboard – Pagina's & Features

### Frontend API-laag (portal)
- Build-time omgeving en FastAPI API-groep-bases staan in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/api.config.ts` (`VITE_BOKITO_API_URL`, `VITE_API_GROUP_*`, same-origin paden `/api/{canonical}/...` via de Vite-proxy of productie-workers).
- Relatieve paden per groep (bijv. `/workspaces`, `/inbox/threads`, `/triggers`, `/auth/refresh` op de auth-base) staan centraal in `apps/dashboard/src/api/routes/` (`auth.routes.ts`, `app.routes.ts`, `integrations.routes.ts`, `workforce.routes.ts`) en in `apps/dashboard/src/api/url.ts` voor query-strings. Featurecode en `lib/*-api.ts` importeren daaruit; `lib/bokito-api.ts` blijft transport (fetch, headers, cookies).
- Deze `src/api/**` bestanden horen **gecommit** te zijn: stonden ze alleen lokaal (niet op `origin/master`), dan herstelt `git fetch` ze niet na een revert; paden zijn dan te reconstrueren uit dezelfde strings die voorheen in `lib/bokito-api.ts`, `lib/custom-db-api.ts`, `lib/email-api.ts`, `lib/inbox-api.ts`, `lib/workforce-api.ts` en workspace/members pages stonden.
- Richtlijn voor agents: `.cursor/rules/frontend-api-env-pattern.mdc` en `apps/dashboard/docs/API.md`.
- **Autosave (2026-10):** Inline editors use `useAutosave` + `AutosaveStatus` instead of an explicit Save button. Debounced save (~700–900ms); status shows **Saving…** / **Last modified {{time}}** (NL: **Laatst gewijzigd**). Applied on flow ticket stages, category editor, project about, agent signature, website widget (Look + availability), and AI communication settings. Dialogs that create or confirm a discrete action (new trigger, MCP connect, modal instructions) still use Save.
- **Open in Communication (2026-10):** Project (and other folder) shortcuts land on the matching Communication sidebar folder (`/communication/project/:id/open?reveal=1`). If that section was hidden or collapsed, it is shown again; the folder row scrolls into view and flashes for two seconds (`useNavReveal`). Same reveal is used from agent chat links, flow action tags, Overview category rows, and the command palette.

### 2.1 Login (`/login`) en Signup (`/signup`)
- **Self-service signup (juli 2026; first/last 2026-10):** `/signup` (pagina `Signup.tsx`) maakt een nieuw account + workspace via `POST /api/auth/signup` (`email`, `password`, `tenant_slug`, `tenant_name`, `first_name`, `last_name`; legacy `display_name` still accepted and split). De workspace-URL (slug) wordt automatisch afgeleid van de bedrijfsnaam en is handmatig aanpasbaar. Na signup is de gebruiker direct ingelogd (zelfde `LoginResponse`-contract als login, refresh-cookie gezet) en landt in een lege workspace met onboarding-checklist. Loginpagina linkt naar `/signup` via "Create account".
- **User first/last name (2026-10):** `User.first_name` / `User.last_name` are the source of truth; `display_name` is the composed full name (`services/user_names.apply_user_names`). Alembic `083_user_first_last_name` backfills from existing `display_name`. Profile, signup and accept-invite collect first/last; `/auth/me` and `_user_dict` expose both plus composed `name`/`display_name`. Email signatures support `{{first_name}}` / `{{last_name}}` (and aliases `voornaam`/`achternaam`) alongside `{{name}}`. SSO provisioning splits the IdP display name the same way.
- E-mail + wachtwoord login via FastAPI `POST /auth/login`
- Dashboard auth gebruikt een same-origin auth-contract op `/api/auth/*` (`login`, `refresh`, `me`, `logout`) met cookie-gebaseerde sessieflow
- Dashboard auth-client gebruikt een fallbackpad naar directe FastAPI auth-endpoints (`//api/auth/*`) wanneer de same-origin auth-proxy niet beschikbaar is (bijv. 404/502 of netwerkfout), zodat login niet blokkeert op proxy-availability.
- Refresh token hoort in een `HttpOnly` cookie; access token blijft alleen in runtime memory (niet in `localStorage`)
- Bij laden probeert de portal eerst `POST /api/auth/refresh` en daarna `GET /api/auth/me` om de sessie te herstellen
- `GET /auth/me` levert tenantcontext in een stabiel object: `tenant = { id, slug, name }` en kan optioneel een logo-URL bevatten (bijv. `logo`, `logo_url` of gelijkwaardig op `tenant`, `account` of `organisation`)
- Frontend normaliseert auth-velden naar 1 intern model (`user.tenant`) zodat tenantdata herbruikbaar is in meerdere modules; `user.tenant.logo` is de eerste beschikbare logo-URL uit die objecten, anders `null`
- De tenantkaart linksboven in de dashboard-sidebar gebruikt de ingelogde tenant uit `user.tenant` (logo, naam + slug); zonder logo-URL valt de UI terug op `/bokito-logo.png`
- `ProtectedRoute` bewaakt alle routes en stuurt ongeauthenticeerde gebruikers naar `/login?return_to=...`; na login gaat de gebruiker terug naar dezelfde interne URL (met open-redirect validatie)
- Cross-host login returns zijn alleen geldig voor bekende control-plane of tenant-hosts; bare `localhost` is geen tenant-host en wordt genegeerd als `return_to` om app.localhost/login-lussen te voorkomen
- **Microsoft browser-login (OAuth) buiten deze repo:** de portal-login in `apps/dashboard` is alleen e-mail/wachtwoord. Zie je een Microsoft-fout `invalid_request` … `redirect_uri` is not valid, dan komt de `redirect_uri` in de authorize-URL **letterlijk** overeen met wat je in Entra onder **Web redirect URIs** zet (geen varianten). Voorbeeld: staat alleen `https://api.bokito.ai/api/auth/callback/microsoft` geregistreerd, maar de client stuurt `.../callback/azure-ad`, dan faalt de flow; voeg die tweede URI toe **of** pas de client aan naar de geregistreerde URI. Dit staat los van mailbox/Graph-OAuth op FastAPI (`MICROSOFT_REDIRECT_URI` → `/api/integrations` OAuth-callback); elke app registration heeft een eigen client-id en redirect-lijst.
- **Outlook mailbox connect toont account-picker (2026-08-25):** `start_real_oauth` zet `prompt=select_account` op de Microsoft authorize-URL wanneer `flow="email"` (Connect Outlook via `/api/email/oauth/start`, `/api/email/outlook/oauth/start`, of `/api/integrations/oauth/start?provider=outlook`). Zonder die prompt hergebruikt Microsoft stil de browser-sessie, zodat de verkeerde mailbox wordt gekoppeld. Platform SSO (`flow="login"`, `/api/auth/microsoft/start`) krijgt géén prompt en mag de huidige Microsoft-sessie hergebruiken.
- **Prod redirect_uri geverifieerd (2026-08):** alle FastAPI OAuth-flows (Outlook mailbox, Microsoft SSO, Gmail, GitHub) delen exact één callback: `{PUBLIC_API_URL}/api/integrations/oauth/callback` (`config.Settings.oauth_redirect_uri`). Prod `.env.prod` heeft `PUBLIC_API_URL=https://app.bokito.ai`, dus prod stuurt letterlijk `https://app.bokito.ai/api/integrations/oauth/callback` (live geverifieerd via `/api/auth/microsoft/start` en `/api/email/outlook/oauth/start`). Krijg je `invalid_request … redirect_uri is not valid` op login.live.com, dan ontbreekt die URI in de Entra app registration (client `f44d9848-89f3-4e7d-8636-a0756ddc87d2`) onder Authentication → Web → Redirect URIs; de registration moet ook personal Microsoft accounts toestaan (tenant `common` → login.live.com valideert strikt).
- **Entra app registration "Bokito" gefixt (2026-08-17):** de registration (client `f44d9848-…`, tenant `4659e272-e64c-4522-ad10-31d66462df82`, eigenaar `lorenzo@bokito.ai`, `signInAudience=AzureADandPersonalMicrosoftAccount`) bevatte alleen twee legacy Xano-callbacks (`https://api.bokito.ai/api:app/email/outlook/oauth/callback` en `https://api.bokito.ai/api/auth/callback/azure-ad`). `https://app.bokito.ai/api/integrations/oauth/callback` is toegevoegd via de portal; daarna accepteren zowel login.live.com (MSA) als login.microsoftonline.com de authorize-URL. Diagnose-tip: met de prod client-credentials (`MICROSOFT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET` uit `.env.prod`) kan een app-only Graph-token de registration **lezen** (`GET /v1.0/applications(appId='…')?$select=web`), maar niet wijzigen (PATCH → 403 zonder Application.ReadWrite-permissie).
- **Gmail OAuth prod geconfigureerd (2026-08-17):** Google Cloud-project "Bokito ai" (`lorenzo@bokito.ai`) heeft OAuth-client "Bokito Production" (web application, redirect `https://app.bokito.ai/api/integrations/oauth/callback`); Gmail API enabled. `GOOGLE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID/SECRET` staan in `/opt/bokito/.env.prod` (client-id `582551847741-…apps.googleusercontent.com`). Let op: de OAuth consent screen staat op **Testing** (External) — alleen testgebruikers kunnen een Gmail-mailbox koppelen; `lorenzo@bokito.ai` is toegevoegd als test user. Voor klanten moet de consent screen naar "In production" gepubliceerd worden (Gmail-scopes zijn restricted en vereisen dan Google-verificatie). "Internal" user type is niet beschikbaar: het project hangt niet onder een Google Workspace-organisatie. UI-valkuil: in het "Add users"-dialoog van de Google Auth Platform-console moet je na het typen van een e-mailadres **Enter** drukken zodat het een chip wordt; anders sluit "Save" zonder fout maar wordt er niets opgeslagen.

### 2.2 Dashboard Home (`/`)
- Welkomstbericht voor de ingelogde gebruiker
- **4 KPI-kaarten**: Gesprekken, Actieve gebruikers, Gem. responstijd, Tokens gebruikt (elk met trend %)
