- **ActivityFeed**: scrollbare lijst van recente platform-events (agent tool calls, nieuwe gesprekken, gebruikersacties, systeemmeldingen) met avatar, actie, doel en relatieve timestamp
- **QuickActions**: 4 snelkoppelingen — Nieuw gesprek, Agent beheren, Analytics bekijken, Organisatie instellen

---

### 2.3 Cloud Agents (`/cloud-agent`)
Beheer van FastAPI-hosted agents die op de achtergrond draaien.

- **Toggle-view**: kaartweergave of lijstweergave
- **AgentCard / AgentRow**: naam, model, status (actief/gepauzeerd/deploying), regio, laatste deploy, 24h request count, P50 latency, tools, systeemprompt preview
- **AgentDetailModal**: volledig detail modal — alle metrics, embed URL, tools, volledige systeemprompt
- **"Nieuwe cloud agent" knop** (in ontwikkeling)

Bekende mock agents: Bokito Support (claude-sonnet-4), Sales Assistant (claude-sonnet-4), Internal Ops (claude-3-5-haiku), Website v3 Draft (claude-opus-4)

---

### 2.4 Agent Canvas (`/agent-canvas`)
Visuele workflow builder voor het ontwerpen van agent-pipelines.

- **Toolbar**: node-types toevoegen (Agent, Schedule, Webhook, Kennisbank, Workflow), zoom in/uit, reset
- **Canvas**: token-gedreven dot-grid achtergrond die meeschakelt met dark/light mode, pan (drag) en zoom (scroll, 0.3x–2.5x), nodes zijn versleepbaar
- **Node types**: `agent`, `cron`, `webhook`, `knowledge`, `repo`, `slack`, `crm`, `email` — elk met icoon, accentkleur en statusdot
- **Edges**: SVG bezier-verbindingen met subtielere dash-flow en lagere glow/contrast voor een rustiger beeld; kleurgecodeerd per verbindingstype
- **Node hover tooltip**: compactere, token-gedreven tooltip + actieknoppen (Run, Config, Logs) voor betere leesbaarheid in light en dark
- **EventLog** (rechts, 224px): live events feed met token-gedreven achtergronden en tekstkleuren (geen hardcoded dark-only kleuren)
- **ScheduleTimeline** (onderbalk): 24-uurs tijdlijn met geplande agent-runs als gekleurde dots, "NU"-indicator en theme-aware styling

---

### 2.5 Webchat Configuratie (`/webchat`)
Volledig configureerbare chat widget, live preview inbegrepen.

**Configuratiepanelen (links):**
- **Uiterlijk**: botnaam, tagline, avatar (initialen + kleurpicker), accent/bubbel/achtergrondkleur, font, widgetbreedte (300-480px slider)
- **Begroeting**: welkomstbericht, inputplaceholder, typing-indicator toggle, geschiedenis-toggle, startvragen (max 5, toevoegen/verwijderen)
- **Launcher**: positie (bottom-right/left met visuele preview), label-toggle, auto-open toggle, labeltekst
- **Systeem**: model selector (gpt-4o, gpt-4o-mini, gpt-4-turbo, claude-3-5-sonnet, claude-3-haiku), temperatuur slider (0-1, labels: Precies tot Creatief), taal (NL/EN/Auto), systeemprompt textarea
- **Embed code**: script-tag + iFrame snippet, beide met kopieerknop. Attributen: `data-agent-slug`, `data-bot-name`, `data-primary-color`, `data-position`

**Live preview (rechts):** Volledig interactief widget-preview dat real-time meebeweegt met alle instellingen.

---

### 2.5.1 Instellingen (`/settings`)
- Hoofdingang via **Instellingen** onderaan de primaire rail; `/settings` redirect naar `/settings/profile`.
- Subnav-blokken: **Communicatie** (e-mail, inbox), **Workspace**, **Mijn organisatie** (bedrijfsconfiguratie; team/kennisbank nog disabled), **Account** (profiel).
- Integratiebeheer (marketplace, verbindingen, MCP, API-sleutels) staat niet meer onder Instellingen; zie §2.6.
- Legacy redirects: `/settings/integrations` → `/integrations/marketplace`, `/settings/mcp` → `/integrations/mcp`; `/company-config` → `/settings/company-config`.

---

### 2.6 Integraties (`/integrations/*`)
Eigen hoofdmenu-item **Integraties** in de primaire rail. Subnavigatie via context-sidebar (Connected-first):

| Route | Doel |
|-------|------|
| `/integrations/connected` | **Standaardlanding**: actieve koppelingen per type (Communication, Repository, MCP); beheren en ontkoppelen |
| `/integrations/marketplace` | **Marketplace**: providers ontdekken en nieuwe verbinding starten (niet primair beheer) |
| `/integrations/mcp` | MCP: tab **Externe servers** (install-tabel + modal) en tab **Bokito-client** (preview) |
| `/integrations/api` | Developer API-sleutels (verborgen in nav tot live) |

`/integrations` en legacy `/integrations/connections` redirecten naar `/integrations/connected`.

Gedeelde **type-segmentatie** (`IntegrationKindNav`): All | Communication | Repository | MCP op Connected en Marketplace, gesynchroniseerd via `?kind=inbox|repository|mcp`.

- **Marketplace UX**: kaartraster per **applicatie** (`integration_hosts`, bijv. één kaart **GitHub** met host-logo). **Connect** opent een hub-modal met **koppelingstypen** = catalogusproducten (`integration_providers`, bijv. repository-indexering + GitHub MCP). Per type: detailstap en setup (OAuth/API-key/remote MCP). URL `?connect=` accepteert host-slug (`github`) of offer-static-id (`github-mcp`). Frontend: `integration-applications.ts`, `ApplicationCard`, `ApplicationHubDialog`. Setup via `integration-setup.ts` + **`apps/dashboard/src/lib/integrations/registry.ts`**; OAuth via `integration-oauth-flow.ts`.
- **Integratiedata-model (schaalbaar)**: `integration_hosts` (merk, logo) → `integration_providers` (product/slug, auth, capabilities, `host_id`) → `integration_connections` (tenant-config, credentials) → `integration_bindings` (gebruik, bijv. `mcp_server`, `project_repo`). E-mail blijft deels parallel op `email_oauth_connection`.
- **Documentatie in de portal**: `/integrations/docs` (sidebar onder Integraties) — uitleg remote MCP OAuth en catalogus voor admins.
- **Developer docs**: `apps/dashboard/docs/INTEGRATIONS.md` — checklists voor nieuwe MCP-, repo-OAuth- en inbox-OAuth-providers; FastAPI-deploy en smoke tests. FastAPI-deploylijst: `docs/archived/v1/INTEGRATIONS-PLATFORM.md`.
- **Connected UX**: secties per type, summary-cards bij filter All, CTA **Integratie toevoegen** naar Marketplace (behoudt actief `?kind=`). E-mail: **Mailboxen beheren** naar `/settings/inbox` (label **Mailboxen** in settings-nav).
- **Catalogus**: `GET /integrations/providers` uit `integration_providers` (seed: github, outlook, gmail, king_accountancy, bjorn_lunden_mcp, custom_mcp, plus remote MCP OAuth slugs in `docs/archived/v1/integration-providers-seed.md`). Marketplace groepeert providers per host; fallback-metadata in `integrations-data.ts` en `mcp-remote-providers.ts`. Platform-tabellen (`integration_hosts`, `integration_providers`, `integration_connections`, `integration_bindings`, `github_connections`) en list-endpoints (API ids 307–312) zijn live op FastAPI workspace 1. Core hosts + providers seeden via `node scripts/seed-integration-providers.mjs` (hosts eerst, daarna providers met `host_id`).
- **Host-logo's**: tabel `integration_hosts` bevat merklogo's (FastAPI image-velden `logo` / `logo_dark`). Providers verwijzen via `host_id`. De portal gebruikt `IntegrationHostLogo`: FastAPI-URL indien geupload, anders officiële merk-SVG's in `apps/dashboard/public/brands/` (vendor sites, Wikimedia, Simple Icons met merkkleur). Zie `public/brands/README.md`. Host **shopify** toegevoegd (`shopify_mcp`, coming_soon, per-store OAuth).
- **Merklogos op connect-kaarten (aug 2026)**: koppelbare systemen tonen hun echte merklogo in plaats van generieke Lucide-iconen. `components/integrations/BrandMark.tsx` levert `BrandTile` (logo-tegel voor kaartheaders, via `IntegrationHostLogo`) en `BrandMark` (klein inline logo voor knoppen/accountrijen); beide resolven via `useIntegrationBrand` met statische fallback uit `brand-assets.ts`. **Elke integratie toont een mark**: vendor-logo als die er is, anders host-initialen — `BrandMark` rendert nooit leeg. Google Calendar deelt het Google-hostlogo (`logo-gmail.svg` / Simple Icons google). `SettingsSection` heeft een optionele `icon`-prop voor de leading tegel. Toegepast op de WhatsApp- en Slack-connectkaarten in `/settings/channels` (mailboxrijen hadden al `ProviderLogo`). WhatsApp-logo toegevoegd: `public/brands/logo-whatsapp.svg` (Simple Icons, `#25D366`) + slug `whatsapp` in `BRAND_ASSET_PATHS` en `HOST_STATIC_BRAND_META`.
- **Verbindingen**: `integration_connections` per tenant, meerdere rijen per provider (bijv. meerdere GitHub-accounts). Lijst: `GET /integrations/connections?provider=`. OAuth: `GET /integrations/oauth/start?provider=` + callback `GET /integrations/oauth/callback`. API-key: `POST /integrations/connections`. Intrekken: `DELETE /integrations/connections/{id}`.
- **GitHub (legacy + nieuw)**: `GET /github/connections` (lijst, API 310), `GET /github/connection` (legacy enkelvoud, API 311), `DELETE /github/connection` (API 312), `GET /github/repos?connection_id=`, repo koppelen via `PATCH /projects/{id}/repo` met `connection_id` + `integration_bindings` type `project_repo`. Worker: `POST /integrations/worker/credentials` (fallback `POST /github/worker/token`). Frontend `listGithubConnections` gebruikt primair `/github/connections` (geen extra fallback naar `integrations/connections?provider=github`).
- **E-mail**: blijft op `email_oauth_connection`; marketplace start OAuth vanuit de setup-modal (`startOAuthConnection` met marketplace return URL). Mailboxbeheer (mappen, handtekening) blijft op `/settings/inbox`.
- **MCP**: `POST /integrations/mcp/install`, `GET /integrations/mcp/bindings`, `GET /integrations/mcp/servers` (includes `tools`, `provider`, `tools_synced_at`). Providers: `king_accountancy` (Bokito-hosted partner MCP at `{PUBLIC_API_URL}/api/mcp/partners/king`; legacy `native://king-accountancy` still resolves), `bjorn_lunden_mcp` (platform MCP server id 8 / `native://bjorn-lunden`), `alpaca_mcp` (native Trading API at `native://alpaca`; Trading API keys today, Alpaca Connect OAuth wired via `ALPACA_OAUTH_CLIENT_*` — tool names align with [alpacahq/alpaca-mcp-server](https://github.com/alpacahq/alpaca-mcp-server)), `custom_mcp` (URL + auth in connection metadata), plus remote OAuth MCP presets. Marketplace setup-modal uses shared `McpConnectionForm`; connection lands on the same `McpServer` + binding node as other MCP integrations.
- **Partner MCP host**: `POST /api/mcp/partners/{partner_slug}` (streamable HTTP JSON-RPC: `tools/list`, `tools/call`). Auth is a per-connection `partner_access_token` (also stored as `bearer_token`). KING is the first partner; new partners = adapter + catalog `mcp_remote_url` + same install path. Operators see exact MCP tool names on the integration detail panel and under modules (`attached_mcp_tools`); agents keep calling module tools (`accounting_*`).
- **Remote MCP catalog** (DB + seed JSON): platform tables `integration_catalog_hosts` / `integration_catalog_providers` are the runtime source for remote MCP marketplace presets. `mcp_remote_catalog.json` seeds missing rows on boot (never overwrites staff edits). Staff CRUD: `/api/staff/integrations/catalog/*` and dashboard `/settings/mcp-catalog`. Tenants see changes via `GET /integrations/providers` without a deploy. Native cores (Alpaca, Bjorn, KING, GitHub, …) stay in code and win on slug collision. Logos: local `public/brands`, Simple Icons, or favicon via `logo_domain`. OAuth presets with a public MCP URL are `available`; without a URL they are forced `coming_soon`.
- **Index**: unified `index_chunks` met `source_type` o.a. `repo_file`, `tenant_doc_section`; runtime `POST /index/tenant-docs` voor doc-secties.
- **Setup Hub (2026-09)**: `/settings/setup` volgt dezelfde 4 onboarding-stappen als de API (`email`, assistant/company-kennis, `first_decision`, `watching`) plus een rustige **Later**-lijst (branding, team, modules, projecten, KPI’s, Govern) zonder pijler-done-matrix. Inbox-links gaan naar `/settings/channels`, niet naar de module-marketplace. Header-CTA opent de bedrijfs-Assistant.
- **Setup Hub (2026-08, historisch)**: zes pijlers met live voortgangsdetectie. Vervangen door de 4-stappenloop hierboven.
- **Eén setup-pad (2026-08 UX-pass):** first open → producttour (orientatie) → assistant setup-chat (tour-finish, primair) of **Setup guide** (`/settings/setup`, secundair). De Setup guide is de enige volledige checklist. Communication-empty en Cockpit `OnboardingCompactCard` zijn **Continue setup** (één volgende stap + Open setup guide + Talk to your assistant), geen tweede 5-stappenlijst. Landing blijft Communication (`HomeRoute`), niet Cockpit.
- **SMTP/IMAP mailbox channel (2026-09):** provider slug `smtp_imap` on `ChannelAccount` (`channel=email`). Operators connect via **Add channel → Email → SMTP / IMAP** (`SmtpImapConnectForm`: mailbox login → provider presets → Incoming/Outgoing → **How far back should we sync?** → **Connect and sync**; username optional when it matches email; domain may suggest Gmail/Outlook/Yahoo/iCloud/Zoho). Create: `POST /api/channels/accounts` with encrypted IMAP/SMTP fields + optional `sync_window_days`; connect complete: `POST /api/channels/accounts/{id}/verify` live-checks IMAP+SMTP, stamps `verified_at`, then runs the first Inbox `sync_account` inline — success only when sync status is `ok` (sets `last_sync_at`). On sync failure, `verified_at` is cleared and the endpoint returns 400 so the UI deletes the half-created row (no lingering **Connecting** / **Verbinden** after a green toast). Connected = username + password + hosts + `verified_at` (registry `has_token`). Ongoing sync: INBOX-only UID cursor in `settings_json.sync_cursors.inbox` via `email_sync`; minute cron `sync_email_mailboxes_job` only **dispatches** one ARQ `sync_email_account_job` per mailbox (skips if `last_sync_at` within ~45s). Canonical per-mailbox sync: `POST /api/channels/accounts/{id}/sync` (recovery only in UI as **Retry sync**; `/api/email/sync` is a tenant-wide shim). Send: `send_via_provider` → stdlib SMTP (To/Cc/Bcc, In-Reply-To/References, HTML, attachments). Defaults IMAP 993 SSL, SMTP 587 STARTTLS (optional 465 SSL). Network failures surface as Action needed. Hostinger must allow egress 993/587/465 (see `docs/DEPLOY.md`). Platform transactional `SMTP_HOST`/Resend is unchanged.

- **Platform efficiency / rules (2026-09 audit):** Email sync batch-dedupes `external_id IN (...)` with composite index `ix_signal_messages_tenant_external`; Gmail lists ids then hydrates bodies only for new messages. Storage store/fetch/delete runs in `asyncio.to_thread`. Fernet is a module-level cached singleton; list UIs prefer `settings_json.connected` readiness flags. Shared ARQ Redis pool for enqueue helpers. Notifications/triggers/webhooks/workspace docs list APIs default `limit=50` (max 100). Email routing reads `InboxRule` only (`action=route`, `source=routing`); the dual-write and the legacy `EmailRoutingRule` fallback are removed. Prod validation rejects localhost Redis/public URLs, incomplete S3, and `debug=True`. Product noun **Messages**; `govern.routes.ts` + shared `ConnectStepRail`.
- **Connected tools naamgeving (2026-08)**: gebruikersgerichte "MCP"-labels zijn hernoemd naar **Connected tools** (NL: **Gekoppelde tools**) in settings-nav, integratie-filters, pageMeta en de servers-tab; "MCP" blijft alleen als technische toelichting tussen haakjes. Route blijft `/settings/mcp`.
- **Koppelingen vs modules (2026-09)**: een `IntegrationConnection` is een zelfstandige partnerregistratie (meerdere per provider). Een module gebruikt die alleen na een expliciete attach (`IntegrationBinding` type `module`, `config_json.module_slug`). Attach is alleen toegestaan als `connection.provider` in `ModuleSpec.provider_slugs` staat. Connect vanuit `/modules/{slug}` auto-attach (`bokito_module` of path). Connect vanuit Koppelingen/Ontdekken blijft los; UI toont “Gebruik in …” als eligible. Module-verbs en `list_module_connections` lezen alleen attached rijen. Backfill in Alembic `026_module_connection_attach`. Hub-tabs: Modules / Koppelingen / Ontdekken. `/modules/tools` redirect naar Koppelingen. `GET /integrations/connected-summary` levert alle actieve registraties inclusief Moneybird (`kind=app`) plus `attached_modules` / `eligible_module`. Native accounting (Moneybird) is kind `app`, geen MCP. Koppelingen is een lijst per type en programma (Agenda i.p.v. Kalender; Code/GitHub onderaan), met eerste-stappen alleen als iets ontbreekt, en een filterbare catalogus **Toevoegen** (`Koppelen` / `Nieuwe koppeling` + aantal). Ontdekken blijft de kaartweergave.
- **Connections-hub twee zones (2026-09, IA fase 1)**: `/connections` is puur **installed** — dezelfde `MarketplaceModuleCard` als in de catalogus (statusbadge, partnerlogo's, **Beheren**, **Deïnstalleren** / Installeren), daarna dezelfde `ApplicationCard` per gekoppelde **integratie** (één kaart per product, niet per vendor: Microsoft 365, Outlook Calendar en Microsoft Graph staan los), daarna Custom MCP-servers, en als afsluiter een verwijzing naar de marketplace. De catalogus-sectie **Toevoegen** en de misplaatste tekst `integrations.connected.catalogHint` ("hetzelfde programma twee keer koppelen") zijn weg; `ConnectionsCatalogList` is verwijderd. `/connections/marketplace` is de volledige catalogus (geen statusfilter Verbonden/Beschikbaar): `MarketplaceModuleCard`-strook bovenaan en daaronder één platte integratiegrid met één `ApplicationCard` per offer (`flattenApplicationOffers`), niet gegroepeerd onder Google/Microsoft. Kind-filter en zoeken blijven: modules alleen bij **Alle integraties** of chip **Modules** (`?kind=modules`); bij andere kinds verdwijnt de modulestrook. Die chip staat alleen op Marketplace (`includeModules`), niet op de Connections-hub. Marketplace-kind komt uit provider-capabilities (`calendar` / `documents` / `mcp_tools` / …), niet uit een gestolen static_id: `dropbox_mcp` heeft `static_id=dropbox-mcp` zodat die niet botst met core Dropbox (Documents → Apps). Lege remote-MCP placeholders op een host die al een native offer heeft, worden weggelaten. Filter herberekent kind per offer; zoeken matcht geen kind-labels meer.
 `ApplicationHubDialog` krijgt `modules` en toont via `ModuleUsageNote` welke modules op die login kunnen draaien plus hun `capability_summary`, met deeplink naar `/connections/{slug}`. Matchen gebeurt in `lib/module-applications.ts` (`moduleUsesApplication`: catalog-tag `application.module` of overlap met `module.provider_slugs`). Dode componenten verwijderd: `IntegrationHubDialog` (type `HubBanner` verhuisd naar `ApplicationHubDialog`), `IntegrationManageSheet`, `ModulePowerSwitch`, `ProviderCard`, `ConnectionsCatalogList`, `InstalledModuleCard`. Gereserveerde bladeren `connected` en `tools` zijn geen moduleslugs: `/connections/connected` en `/connections/tools` redirecten naar de hub (zelfde als `legacyModulesPath`). Module-home-copy wijst naar **Settings > Connections** / **Instellingen > Koppelingen**, niet naar Modules. Het dashboard wrapt `GET /orchestration/tasks` niet meer als product-taken-API; `AgentTask` blijft intern (playbooks, delegatie, werkledger).
- **Integratie-modules (2026-08)**: een **module** is een first-class product-hub (schema, verbs, skill, setup, sources), geen Settings-only power-switch. Een **connector** is het pakket eronder (KING, Moneybird). Inbox/GitHub blijven kanalen/kinds, geen modules. Enablement staat in `Tenant.settings_json.modules` (`{ "accounting": { "enabled": true, "default_connection_id": "…", "default_company_by_connection": {…} } }`). Lifecycle: `not_installed` → `setup` → `installed`. **Minstens één AI-agent** moet op de module-roster (`module_agents`, migratie `021`) staan vóór **Setup afronden**; `is_default` is de setup-chat agent. Alleen roster-agents krijgen module-tools (`filter_tools_for_agent_modules` in `AgentLoop`). API: `GET/POST /integrations/modules/{slug}/agents`, `PATCH/DELETE .../agents/{agent_id}`. Catalog-rows bevatten `assigned_agent_count` + `default_agent_id`. Een pakket koppelen zet de parent-module in **setup** (niet meer auto-`installed`) en seedt platformbronnen. Registry is `ModuleSpec` in `apps/api/app/modules/catalog.py` (accounting `available`; banking, investing, documents `coming_soon`). Hub: `/modules`; module-home: `/modules/:slug` met tabs Overview / Connections / Sources / Setup. Legacy `/settings/modules*` redirect naar `/modules*`.
- **Modulebronnen (indexering):** `ModuleSource` rijen per tenant. Accounting-platformpacks in `ACCOUNTING_PLATFORM_SEEDS` (`belastingdienst`, `nba_hra`, `rjnet`, `kvk_jaarrekening`, `bw2_titel9`) hebben `start_urls`. Indexer (`module_sources.crawl_source_pages`) doet BFS same-origin vanaf die URL's, `robots.txt`, geen inlogpaden, PDF via pypdf, caps 20 pagina's (platform) / 12 (tenant), 80k tekens/pagina, 400k/doc. `ensure_platform_seeds` enqueue't `pending` (status `indexing`). Wekelijkse cron pakt ook stale `indexing`. Content landt als één `WorkspaceDoc` met `##` per pagina → `doc_chunks`. Agents: `search_module_sources` alleen `ready`. UI Bronnen polt tijdens indexing.
- **Agent ziet modules alleen als ze aan staan én hij op de roster staat**: tenant-snapshot `Modules:` toont `off` / `on, no package` / `connected`. `filter_tools_for_modules` verbergt prefixes tot de module `installed` is; `filter_tools_for_agent_modules` houdt tools alleen voor agents op `module_agents`. Skill-playbook alleen bij on + geen pakket; volle skill bij on + connected. `accounting_*` geeft eerst `module_off`, daarna `no_connection`. Tools `list_modules` en `recommend_module` blijven ungated.
- **Accounting-module**: één contract over KING Accountancy, Björn Lundén en Moneybird (Exact Online en SnelStart schema-ready, coming_soon). Canoniek schema `apps/api/app/modules/accounting/schema.py` (Company, Party, Document, Account, LedgerLine, Outstanding, BankMutation, TaxRate); capability-matrix `capabilities.py`; router `router.py` (connectie-discovery, verb-dispatch, capability-checks, dev-mocks, `summarize`); adapters onder `adapters/` (king, bjorn_lunden, moneybird). Agents gebruiken builtin `accounting_*` read-tools en `accounting_propose_*` write-tools (writes altijd via DecisionRequest). Moneybird is een OAuth-provider (`oauth_providers.py`; prod vereist `MONEYBIRD_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` + `MONEYBIRD_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`; redirect `{PUBLIC_API_URL}/api/integrations/oauth/callback`; ops: `scripts/ops/vps-set-moneybird-oauth.py`). Native client `services/moneybird.py`. Frontend-endpoint: `GET /integrations/modules/accounting/companies`.
- **Accounting write-laag (2026-08)**: uniforme propose→approve→apply-keten voor alle boekhoudconnectors. Canonieke write-payloads in `schema.py` (`PartyUpsert`, `JournalEntry`+`JournalLine`, `SalesInvoiceDraft`, `PaymentRegistration`; map `WRITE_PAYLOADS`); capability-keys `parties.write` / `journal.write` / `documents.sales.write` / `payments.write` (alleen KING heeft nu parties+journal True). Apply-verbs `apply_party` / `apply_booking` (later document/payment) valideren de payload vóór dispatch. `build_proposal` wijst de approve-optie naar de mutating tools `accounting_apply_party` / `accounting_apply_booking` (`mutating=True, gated=True`), zodat `resolve_decision` → `execute_tool(approved=True)` → adapter → AuditEvent, zelfde patroon als calendar. **Kill switch**: platform-env `ACCOUNTING_WRITES_ENABLED` (default false, `config.py`) én tenant-pref `modules.accounting.writes_enabled` (default false, alleen owner/admin via `PATCH /integrations/modules/accounting/prefs`) moeten beide aan; anders nette `writes_disabled`-fout als decisionresultaat. `list_module_connections` levert `prefs.writes_enabled` + `writes_active` voor de UI-banner in `AccountingModuleOverview` (toggle voor owner/admin). KING writes: `CreateJournaalpost` en `Create/UpdateStamTabelRecord` (DEB/CRED) in `king_finance.py` (`_soap_write`, `_stamtabel_dataset`, `_journaalpost_dataset`; Primarykey wordt teruggelezen).
- **Accounting proposal-weergave (2026-08)**: beide decision-oppervlakken tonen een gestructureerd veldenblok bij accounting-voorstellen (administratie, relatie, type, omschrijving, regels/totaal): inbox `DecisionRequestMessage` én agent-chat `ChatDecisionCard` delen `lib/accounting-proposal.ts` + `AccountingProposalBlock`. De chat-serializer (`serialize_decision` in `assistant_threads.py`) geeft per optie het volledige `payload` mee. `_propose_handler` verrijkt het voorstel met `company_label` (opgelost via live `list_companies`) zodat de kaart een leesbare administratienaam toont i.p.v. een UUID; de apply-laag filtert onbekende keys weg vóór payload-validatie.
- **Accounting granulaire toegang (2026-08)**: per agent op de roster: `ModuleAgent.company_ids_json` (JSON-lijst administratie-ids, leeg = alle) en `ModuleAgent.can_write` (default false), migratie `022_module_agent_scope`. Afgedwongen in `call_accounting_verb` (agent buiten roster → `agent_forbidden`; buiten company-scope → `company_forbidden`; apply zonder can_write → `write_forbidden`; `list_companies` gefilterd op scope), in `filter_tools_for_agent_modules` (propose/apply-tools weggefilterd zonder can_write) en in `tenant_introspection` (agent-snapshot toont alleen zijn administraties + write-vlag). UI: toegangspaneel in `ModuleAgentsSection` (Switch Schrijftoegang + administratie-multiselect); badges Write/Read-only/scope ook op de workspace-roster. Per user: module-pref `user_access` (`{"mode": "all_members"|"selected", "user_ids": [...]}`); `user_can_access_module` in `catalog.py` (owners/admins altijd toegang), afgedwongen via `_ensure_module_access` op module-endpoints (agents, connections, sources, accounting companies → 403) en `GET /integrations/providers` levert `user_accessible` per module zodat sidebar en workspace de module verbergen voor niet-toegelaten members.
- **Mailbox-gated compose (2026-08, Milan walk):** Write email / New email / Email a customer only appear when a mailbox can send. The built-in Bokito address counts (`isSendableMailbox`); Gmail/Outlook need `connected`/`active`. Without a sendable mailbox, New chat and the command palette offer **Connect a mailbox**, compose shows a connect empty state, and Contacts / Cockpit / the thread contact panel hide the write-email trap. Marketplace lists the full catalog (no connected/available status filter). Page-guide banners are a single title + Learn more row. Times use a 24-hour clock in both EN and NL.
- **Communication reply fallback (2026-08):** Email threads without `channel_account_id` still send when the workspace has an enabled mailbox. `deliver_outbound` binds the first sendable account (Bokito, then Gmail/Outlook/smtp_imap/mock). The composer blocks only when **no** sendable mailbox exists — not when the thread itself has a null `emailConnectionId`. Website chat (`widget` / `webchat` / `livechat`) stays on the chat surface even if the visitor left an email. Closed/spam composers keep notes and offer **Reopen**. **Quote** works on chat and email. **R** on a closed thread reopens then focuses the composer. Widget visitors with a placeholder address get an ask-for-email hint. Contacts search honors `?q=`. Empty inbox no longer requires a mailbox before New chat / widget setup.
- **Communication tags (2026-10):** tags are a join table `signal_tag_links (signal_id, tag_id)` on the `SignalTag` registry (see section 47). `Signal.tags_json` is gone; the conversation payload's `tags` comes from the links.
- **Thread list turn arrows (2026-08-28):** Customer rows no longer show the “Needs reply” / “Jij aan zet” text chip. An accent **left** arrow (tooltip: Needs reply / Jij aan zet) means the last real line is inbound or unread; a muted **right** arrow (Waiting on them / Wacht op antwoord) means open + last line outbound. The arrow sits at the far right of the preview row. Tags (and the needs-decision chip) sit under the contact title so the preview stays readable.
- **Tag registry (2026-10):** `signal_tags` is the workspace tag vocabulary again, managed in Settings → Channels → **Tags** and via `/api/signals/tags`. There is no `billing` regex heuristic; tag suggestions come from the registry and AI triage only.

- **Cases hub (retired 2026-09):** the queue, rail tab, and `CasesPage` are gone. `/cases` and `/cases/*` redirect to `/settings/action-tags`, where `SignalTypesSettings` owns the type catalog and policy. A typed Signal is worked from **This conversation**; workstream/project bindings stay on their destination settings.
- **Category outcome (2026-10):** `case_types.follow_up_mode` is dropped. The workstream binding decides the outcome: a category without one is a label (the case is `done` at once), a category with one makes the case a ticket that moves through the workstream's stages (section 47). Filing never creates a human `AgentTask`; exceptions use an inline Decision or a playbook step.
- **Agenda schedules + conversation tasks (2026-10):** **Agenda** merges planned triggers, scheduled `AgentTask` rows, calendar occurrences and activity. Conversation reminders are human `AgentTask`s (`kind=task`), not `Signal.follow_up_at`. `case_types.follow_up_task` remains retired: filing a typed Signal never opens an AgentTask.
- **What next on a conversation (2026-10):** Operators use one **What next / Wat nu** action (always on the thread meta row for open external conversations). A free reminder creates a human `AgentTask` on the conversation (`scheduled_for`). Snooze-as-park is retired (mark unread or plan a task; the thread stays in Open). Typed work opens or attaches a Case (operator Signal). Planned tasks live on the conversation chrome / WhatsNext / Agenda — not on the contact side panel. Signals on the conversation sit in **This conversation** below identity; there is no operator control to link a chat to a Project (Project is not a folder for conversations). Noreply / donotreply senders default the composer to Intern. Task is not an eighth operator noun; `AgentTask` stays an internal execution ledger for playbooks and workbench jobs. Agenda operator copy is “what is scheduled”, not “triggers”.
- **Communication empty channels / reply-without-mailbox (2026-09):** Channels section omits empty-state preachy copy and shows only an **Add a channel** CTA. When a customer reply is blocked (no mailbox), the composer stays on **Reply** and shows the connect-mailbox banner there (with **Write an internal note instead**); it does not silently dump the operator on Intern. The in-app personal assistant FAB tints with the workspace `brand_color` while keeping the Bokito mark. Typed Signals are reviewed under **This conversation**; Projects related links include Workstreams. The retained `GET /api/cases` API supports conversation and internal lookup flows, but there is no Cases hub. Signal lifecycle is independent of the thread: closing a Signal never closes the conversation.
- **Mental-model cleanup (2026-09):** Who panel no longer hosts project/look-at/Signals. Dedicated `/communication/decisions` is the Decisions hub leaf (open DecisionRequests). Govern stays under Settings (not a main-rail item). Projects drop per-project autonomous mode (Govern only); Lead → project agent; queue kinds are Improvement/Problem/Request/Idea/Risk. Inbox AI / Messages AI → AI replies. Free-form tag chips removed from the thread list; Signal chips use a radio icon. Slash help is `/assign`, `/signal`, `/approve` only. Activity is demoted on Overview.
- **Portal motion + skeletons (2026-09):** Shared load/enter tokens live in `index.css` + `tailwind.config.ts` (`page-enter`, `content-enter`, `list-row-enter`, richer `skeleton-shimmer` + breathe, longer `skel-stagger`). `skeleton.tsx` adds `CockpitSnapshotSkeleton`, `CockpitPanelsSkeleton`, `ProjectCanvasSkeleton`, `NavSectionSkeleton`, and denser inbox/chat placeholders. Surfaces: Overview shows panel skeletons while loading; Communication hub nav uses row skeletons; thread list staggers first rows; project canvas board uses a widget grid skeleton; AppShell soft-enters full-bleed hubs without replaying on every thread leaf. All decorative motion respects `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **Operator UX polish (2026-09):** `AiAvatar` supports `decorative` so list/select rows do not double the agent name in the accessible name. Agent Direct threads prefer last-message preview over repeating the agent name as secondary. Unrestricted tool catalogs on an agent chat side panel show a capped count such as **2 modules | 3 connections | 999+ tools**; expanding lists modules and connections on one muted row (logo then name, no section headings). **Open agent** / **Agent openen** goes to the agent page (no separate Configure button). Manual Case attach CTA is **Signaal toevoegen / Add a signal**. Mock heartbeat wake bodies surface as **Check-in**. Agenda prefers resolved agent display names over role slugs like `orchestrator`. Related footers use Set C **Communicatie** (not Berichten/Messages) and localized `aria-label` Gerelateerd/Related. Channel filters label aliases (`customer_widget` → Websitechat) and the API matches widget aliases together. Contact list titles derive from email local-parts when no name is set. Agent library audiences are **Klanten / Intern / Partners**. Draaiboeken links to Signaaltypes use NL copy (`manageInCases` + `openTypes`). Hub settings link is **Kanalen**.

---

### 2.6b Project Hub (`/projects`, `/project/:id/*`)
Workforce-projecten hebben een dedicated hub met sidebar-navigatie.

- **Admin rail**: Home, **Projecten** (`/projects`), Inbox, Assistent, Integraties, Data, **Agenten** (`/admin/runs`).
- **Routes**: `/projects` (lijst), `/projects/new` + `/projects/new/:projectId/connect` (create wizard stap 2: GitHub OAuth of "Set it up for me" coming soon), `/project/:id/overview|doc|doc/:pageSlug|request|messages|settings`. Legacy `/project/:id/pkb` redirect naar `/project/:id/doc`.
- **Home redirect**: bij precies één project gaat `/` naar `/project/{id}/overview`.
- **Repo status**: projectkaarten en header tonen `repo_index_status` (none, pending, queued, indexing, ready, error).
- **GitHub OAuth flow**: tenant-scoped `integration_connections` (+ legacy `github_connections` dual-write); meerdere GitHub-accounts per tenant; project wizard kiest account + repo/branch → `PATCH /projects/{id}/repo` met `connection_id` → `POST /projects/{id}/repo/reindex`.
- **Integrations API**: zie §2.6; project repo worker via `POST /integrations/worker/credentials` en `POST /github/worker/index-status`.
- **Runtime**: `POST /repo/reindex` op worker clone/pull via OAuth token, enqueuet per bestand naar index queue; clones onder `REPO_CLONE_DIR` (default OS temp `bokito-repos`).
- **Index search**: `POST /index/search` sorteert chunks op pgvector cosine (`<=>`) i.p.v. `updated_at`.
- **Shell + UI**: alle `/project/:id/*`-pagina's wrappen content in `components/project/ProjectShell.tsx`. De shell rendert de slanke `ProjectContextBar` (projectnaam + repo-status badge + acties Details/Request a change/Connect code; de volledige `autonomous_scope` staat alleen op `/project/:id/settings`). De legacy `ProjectHeader` is verwijderd. `Layout.tsx` levert al `px-5 pb-5 pt-2.5`, dus pagina's voegen geen eigen `p-6` meer toe. Alle vlakken gebruiken `Card` (`border-border/80 bg-bg-surface/95 rounded-2xl`) i.p.v. de oude `border-border-subtle bg-surface-raised`-utilities.
- **Sidebar nav**: `SectionSidebar` voegt op `/project/:id/doc[/...]` een tweede groep `Pages` (NL: `Pagina's`) toe met de doc-tree (`PageTree variant="sidebar"`). De Documentation-link blijft actief op alle `doc/:slug`-routes via een custom `isLinkActive`. De doc-tree wordt 1x per project geladen door `ProjectDocNavProvider` (in `ProjectLayout`); zowel de sidebar als de doc-pagina lezen daaruit.
- **AppHeader breadcrumb**: voor project-routes toont de header `{project.name} / {section}`, en op doc-routes wordt de actieve pagina-titel toegevoegd (`{project} / Documentation / {page}`). Project- en doc-context worden via `useOptionalProjectContext()` + `useOptionalProjectDocNav()` gelezen zodat de header geen eigen fetches doet.
- **Doc-canvas**: `ProjectDoc.tsx` is een single-column canvas zonder eigen sidebar/right rail (tree zit nu in de `SectionSidebar`). Top toolbar: `kind`-eyebrow + page title + acties **History** (opent een Radix `Dialog` met `RevisionPanel variant="embedded"`) en **Request a change**. Lege/foutstaten: empty scaffold-CTA, friendly retry-card bij API-404 (geen ruwe HTTP-paths in UI), locked-page hint via i18n. Page-strings staan in `locales/{en,nl}/nav.json` onder `project.{contextBar,overview,messages,settings,request,list,doc}`.

### 2.6c Project Documentation System (block-based, replaces `pkb_sections`)
Elk project heeft één centrale documentatie als Notion-stijl block-based hub. Vervangt het oude `pkb_sections` model. Docs zijn de single source of truth voor wat een project is en waar het heen gaat; agents lezen ze op elke run en schrijven er direct in met volledige audit trail.

- **Datamodel** (zie `docs/archived/v1-platform-tables.md`): `project_docs` (1 per project) → `doc_pages` (boom van pagina's; `kind` ∈ overview/vision/features/brand/tech/marketing/operations/roadmap/log/notes/custom; `is_locked` blokkeert agent-writes) → `doc_blocks` (Notion-blocks: heading_1/2/3, paragraph, bullet/numbered/to_do, quote, callout, divider, code, image, embed, link_to_page, toggle, table; `text` is array van inline runs `{text, bold, italic, underline, strike, code, color, link}`; `props` is type-specific). Audit: `doc_block_revisions` (op=create/update/delete/move; before/after JSON; actor_type=user|agent; actor_label; required `change_note` voor agents). Inbox: `doc_change_requests` (vervangt `pkb_sections.layer=change_queue`; status pending/in_progress/implemented/blocked/rejected).
- **PRD scaffold seed**: `POST //api/workforce/projects` (project create) zaait nu `project_docs` + 8 standaardpagina's (Overview, Vision and audience, Features and scope, Brand and voice, Tech stack, Marketing, Operations, Roadmap), elk met heading_1 + callout + paragraph startblokken. Niets is gelocked; gebruiker kan vrij hernoemen, toevoegen of verwijderen. Pagina-`kind` matcht de scaffold zodat agents kunnen anchoren.
- **Workforce API** (user-auth, alle onder `//api/workforce`): `GET /projects/{id}/doc` (root + page tree, geen blocks), `POST /projects/{id}/doc/pages`, `PATCH /projects/{id}/doc/pages/{page_id}`, `DELETE /projects/{id}/doc/pages/{page_id}` (soft delete via `archived_at`), `GET /projects/{id}/doc/pages/{page_id}/blocks`, `POST /projects/{id}/doc/pages/{page_id}/blocks` (batch op-array `[{op:create|update|delete|move, ...}]`; schrijft blocks + revisions atomisch en triggert `WORKER_BASE_URL/doc/reindex-page` voor embedding), `GET /projects/{id}/doc/pages/{page_id}/revisions?block_id=…`, `POST /projects/{id}/doc/change-requests` (vervangt legacy PKB change_queue POST; zelfde PO heartbeat dispatch), `GET /projects/{id}/doc/change-requests`.
- **Integrations API** (worker-auth via `WORKER_INBOUND_SECRET` body-token): `POST //api/integrations/doc/worker/blocks` (agent batch ops, `actor_type=agent`, `change_note` verplicht), `POST //api/integrations/doc/worker/reindex-page` (worker fetch van blocks voor embedding), `POST //api/integrations/doc/worker/tree` (project page tree voor doc-map opbouw).
- **Frontend**: `/project/:id/doc/:pageSlug` rendered via `apps/dashboard/src/pages/ProjectDoc.tsx` met `components/doc/PageTree.tsx` (sidebar; lucide icoon per page kind), `components/doc/BlockEditor.tsx` (contentEditable per block; debounced auto-save via diff → batch op API; `@dnd-kit` reorder; slash-style block-type dropdown; agent-edited blocks krijgen `ActorBadge` (kleine accent-stip links)), `components/doc/RevisionPanel.tsx` (audit list met one-click revert per revision). Transport: `apps/dashboard/src/lib/doc-api.ts` + `lib/doc-blocks.ts` (`buildBlockTree`, `diffBlockLists`, `newBlockId`). `ChangeRequest.tsx` schrijft nu naar `doc_change_requests` en accepteert optionele `target_page_id` via `location.state` zodat de "Request a change"-knop op een doc-pagina automatisch de juiste pagina koppelt.
- **Runtime + agent tools**: `apps/runtime/src/docs/{client.ts,reindex.ts,block-utils.ts,doc-map.ts}` doen reindexing (per block één `index_chunks` row met `source_type=doc_block`, `source_ref=<page_slug>#<block_id>`; één samenvatting per pagina met `source_type=doc_page_summary`) en doc-map opbouw. `dispatcher.ts` accepteert `POST /doc/reindex-page` (Bearer secret). `runner.ts` stopt een compacte plain-text doc-map (pagina-titels + headings) in elke `RUN_CONFIG_JSON.platform.doc_map`; agent-loop voegt die toe aan het system prompt. Agent tools (`packages/docker/agent-run/agent-loop.js`): `write_doc` (page_id + change_note + ops; logt revisions met agent als actor) en `read_doc_page` (huidige block list ophalen). De oude `read_pkb` / `update_pkb_section` tools zijn verwijderd; het PO heartbeat-pad blijft identiek (een nieuwe change_request triggert dezelfde `/agent/po/run`).
- **Workspace documentatie (project hub):** parallel datamodel `workspace_docs` / `workspace_doc_pages` / `workspace_doc_blocks` / `workspace_doc_block_revisions` (tenant-scoped, geen `project_id`). UI: `/projects/docs[/:slug]` via `WorkspaceDocNavContext`, `ProjectHubDocs`, `docScope=workspace`. API: `/workspace/doc/*` (workforce) en `/workspace/doc/worker/*` (integrations). Zie `docs/archived/v1/PROJECT-HUB-BACKEND.md`.
- **Migratie**: `docs/archived/v1/MIGRATE-pkb-to-docs.md` beschrijft het one-shot pad: bootstrap missing scaffolds, map `pkb_sections.layer = current_state|intended_state` rows als blocks onder de juiste `kind`-pagina (default Overview), map `change_queue` rows naar `doc_change_requests`. Oude pkb-rijen blijven leesbaar voor één release; daarna wordt de tabel gedropt. Deprecated XS files (`workforce-pkb-create.xs`, `-list.xs`, `-worker-list.xs`, `-worker-update.xs`) en frontend `lib/pkb-api.ts` + `pages/Pkb.tsx` zijn al verwijderd.

### 2.6d Conversation-driven project work (V1: queue + smart docs + resources)

Core principle: **the queue is the motor, the doc is the truth, the conversation is the source.** Projects in the V1 FastAPI track carry an implementation queue, project-scoped smart documentation, and generic resource slots.

- **Data model** (`apps/api/app/models/project_work.py`): queue items on `AgentTask` (kind feature/bug/task/idea/risk; priority; status machine `proposed → queued → analyzing → planned → running → verifying → completed` plus `rejected`; provenance `origin_type`/`signal_id`/`message_id`; `impact_summary`), `ProjectDocSection` (optional status layer over `##` headings), `TaskDocLink` (queue item ↔ knowledge doc via `doc_id`; optional legacy `section_id`; relation implements/modifies/touches/documents), `ProjectResource` (generic slot). `WorkspaceDoc` is the single knowledge store: organization (`project_id` and `agent_id` null), project (`project_id` set, usually `kind=project_doc`), or agent (`agent_id` set). Alembic `029_knowledge_hub_scopes`.
- **Knowledge hub**: `/knowledge` shows entity chips — workspace name (default), then each project and agent by name. Project **Documentation** tab is a contextual view of the same rows. Editors default to WYSIWYG (**Write**) with a **Markdown** toggle; storage is always markdown `content`. Doc payloads include `linked_requests[]` (active queue statuses only); status never lives on the doc.
- **Repo fields migrated**: the GitHub fields formerly on `Project` live in a `ProjectResource(resource_type="repo", provider="github")`; `serialize_project` derives the legacy repo fields from that resource (alembic `016_project_work`).
- **Service** (`apps/api/app/services/project_work.py`): validated status transitions with AuditEvents and SignalEvents; section sync on project-doc save; `link_item_to_doc` for document-level links; analysis instructions prefer impact analysis → `link_queue_item_to_doc(doc_id)` → `write_doc` → verify before complete. Project Home shows one left-to-right kanban of typed Signals on the project (`GET /api/cases?project_id=`): Proposed → Open → Waiting → Done. The implementation queue (`AgentTask`) is not a second board on Home.
- **Agent flows**: accepting an item creates an `AgentTask` (`trigger_type="queue_item"`) for the project agent with doc context; the agent links docs, updates via `write_doc` (Govern-gated), and finishes with an impact summary. Verification checks docs against reality before complete. Orchestrator `system_prompt` reinforces knowledge architecture/maintenance. Queue items auto-accept only when the **workspace** autonomy posture is `autonomous` (Govern). There is no per-project autonomy flag. The Projects list badge is **open signals** (Cases on the project), not open queue items.
- **Agent tools** (category `projects` / `workspace`): `list_docs` honors `project_id` / `agent_id` / `scope=all`; `write_doc` accepts `agent_id`; `link_queue_item_to_doc` prefers `doc_id` (section_id legacy); plus queue/resource tools as before.
- **API**: workspace docs accept `project_id` / `agent_id` / `scope` query filters and return `linked_requests`. Under `/workforce/projects/{id}`: `GET/POST /docs`, `POST /docs/{doc_id}/links` (document-level), legacy section link route kept, queue + resources unchanged.
- **UI**: Knowledge hub scopes + shared TipTap editor; Project Documentation shows linked-request chips and softens section chrome; Queue items can **Link document** (project docs preferred, org docs allowed).
- **Workbench abstraction**: Bokito dispatches coding work through one normalized adapter contract (`JobSpec`, `JobHandle`, capabilities, status/follow-up/cancel, normalized webhook events). Built-in provider slots are GitHub Copilot, Cursor Cloud Agents, Anthropic Managed Agents, OpenAI Agents API, and generic MCP-attached workbenches. Connections use `kind=workbench`, projects may select a default `workbench_connection_id`, and `WorkJob` records provider IDs, state, artifacts, cost, and links back to the originating conversation, Signal, and Project. Bokito remains the governed orchestration layer; code execution stays in the connected workbench.

---

### 2.7 Bronnen (`/integrations/sources`)
Onder **Integraties** als **Bronnen** (legacy redirect van `/datasources`); geen koppelingenbeheer op deze pagina.

**Bovenste sectie: geindexeerde documentatie (primair)**
- Haalt tenant-scoped docs op via `GET /docs` (auth API); lege staat wanneer nog geen bronnen geindexeerd zijn.
- Cards tonen docnaam, bron-URL en status **Actief voor agents** (read-only weergave).

**Onderste sectie: verbonden integraties (alleen-lezen)**
- Component `ConnectedIntegrationsPreview`: compacte chips voor verbonden GitHub-accounts, e-mail-tellingen, geinstalleerde MCP.
- Geen connect/disconnect op deze pagina; CTA **Beheer in Integraties** → `/integrations/marketplace`.

**Tenant-scoped dataflow**
- Frontend haalt docs op via auth API-contract `GET /docs` (zelfde auth base als dashboard)
- Tenantcontext wordt door backend/JWT bepaald en centraal opgehaald via `GET /auth/me`
- Datasources gebruikt `user.tenant` uit `AuthContext` als single source of truth voor tenant labels en fallbacklogica
- De paginatitel toont `AI Bronnen van {tenantnaam}` met `user.tenant.name` (of slug/`je organisatie` als fallback); links staat het tenantlogo (`user.tenant.logo`) met fallback naar `/bokito-logo.png`
- Empty/error/loading states zijn aanwezig op de pagina

**Fallbackbeleid**
- De datasources pagina gebruikt geen lokale tenant-fallback voor docsweergave; de UI toont uitsluitend resultaten uit de tenant-scoped API.

**Definitieve docs API (tenant-isolatie + RBAC)**
- De Authentication API group bevat tenant-scoped docs endpoints met `auth = "user"`:
  - `GET /docs`
  - `GET /docs/{doc_id}`
  - `POST /docs`
  - `PATCH /docs/{doc_id}`
  - `DELETE /docs/{doc_id}` (soft delete via `status = archived`)
  - `GET /docs/{doc_id}/pages`
  - `GET /docs/{doc_id}/sections`
- Tenant filtering gebeurt in backend op basis van ingelogde user (`user.account_id`) + account→organisation mapping
- Write-endpoints zijn RBAC-gated: alleen `admin` mag create/update/archive, read blijft voor geauthenticeerde users binnen dezelfde tenant
- Frontend gebruikt geen lokale docs-fallback meer voor weergave van tenantdocs

**Seeddata tenant `bourgondienadvies` (organisatie Bourgondiënadvies)**
- Vier doc-bronnen in `doc` met pages/sections in `doc_page`, `doc_section`: [RJNet](https://www.rjnet.nl/), [NBA HRA](https://www.nba.nl/wet--en-regelgeving/hra/), [Belastingdienst](https://www.belastingdienst.nl/), [Bourgondiënadvies diensten](https://bourgondienadvies.nl/diensten/)
- `doc_section.embedding` blijft null voor seed records (klaar voor latere embedding-pipeline)

---

### 2.8 Communicatie (`/communication`)
Slack-achtige teaminterne chat.

- **ChannelSidebar** (links): Favorieten, Kanalen (Inbox/Klantvragen/Jaarrekening/Loonadministratie), Direct berichten met klantnamen, ongelezen-badges, zoekknop
- **ChannelSidebar** (links): mailbox-achtige secties **Mappen**, **Labels** en **Klanten** met bijpassende iconen en mailbox-zoekveld
- **MessageArea** (midden): mailbox-achtige 2-koloms opzet met links een inbox-lijst (afzender, onderwerp, preview, unread-dot, labels) en rechts een geselecteerde mail-preview met body en quick actions (reply/reply-all/archive)
- Mail-preview ondersteunt een inline **AI suggesties**-blok boven de mailinhoud met hiërarchische tekstwolkjes (`info`, `proposal`, `task`)
- Suggestiewolkjes kunnen optionele metadata tonen (bijv. boekingsreferentie of taakcontext) en in de `proposal`-variant actieknoppen tonen zoals **Genereren** en **Reageren** (UI-only)
- Voor spoedmails ondersteunt de `task`-variant een visuele prioriteitsindicatie in dezelfde previewkolom
- Middenheader bevat mailbox-filters (**Alle**, **Ongelezen**, **Urgent**) voor snelle triage
- **InfoPanel** (rechts): tabloos overzicht gekoppeld aan het geselecteerde bericht met drie vaste blokken: **Contact info afzender**, **Notities** en **Eerdere berichten** van dezelfde afzender

**Email sample datamodel in frontend (`Message`):**
- Ondersteunt extra velden voor mailboxweergave: `subject`, `preview`, `body`, `fromEmail`, `accountName`, `labels[]`, `unread`
- Ondersteunt optioneel `aiSuggestions[]` met `level`, `text`, `meta` en optionele `actions[]` voor contextuele AI-voorstellen per mail
- Bestaande `content` blijft beschikbaar als fallback

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, less-techy pass)
- **Cockpit tiles** spell out what a number means (`awaitingDecisionHint`, `autonomyHint`). Partial-load slice labels are human words (`how much agents can do`, `pending reviews`). Empty CSAT goes to `/settings/widget`. When the platform check-in is off, the banner title is **Hourly inbox scan is off**.
- **Govern Undo** opens a confirm dialog that shows the change summary. `applied_yolo` reads **Applied without review**. Hardcoded Cancel is i18n `history.cancel`.
- **Messages sidebar:** queue rows have tooltips (All / Mine / Open / Unassigned / More). **Team chat** is the internal channel. An empty mailbox list shows **Connect email**. Settings leaf is **AI reply settings**. **Agent runs** has a hint.
- **Composer:** **Templates** is a visible label. Note vs Reply reminders (**The customer sees this** / **Only your team sees this**) sit on the tab tooltips. **Send and close** and **Send and snooze** live under the chevron next to Send. New conversation has **Back to Messages** and a To-field hint.
- **Contacts:** **Group contacts by company** is the backfill action. Status chips have hints. A thread without a person offers **Add to contacts** via `newContactPath(address)`. Dirty contact/company edits warn on tab close. Create form seeds `?address=`.
- **Activity:** Headlines vs Full log have titles. **Jump to newest** / **Clear list (does not delete data)**. Empty primary CTA is Open Messages.
- **Onboarding:** demo-thread failure toasts. Watching step primary CTA is Setup; secondary is ask the assistant.
- **Help center (public):** Retry on load error. Empty catalog stays on the public page (settings stay in Help centers for operators).
- **Agents library:** Lead badge also shows **Handles mail when nothing else is assigned**. `/agents` and `/agents/:id` show a now-centered **Activity** swimlane (`AgentActivityTimeline`, `GET /api/workforce/activity-timeline`, ±7 dagen): even-sized points (icon per action type: chat, wake, heartbeat, webhook, workstream, queue, coding, generic session). Nearby marks cluster into one numbered point; hover lists them stacked. Sessions ≤ 2 minutes show a single clock time. Click opens the run, conversation, or Agenda item. Combined library view is one lane per agent. This stays a custom swimlane (not catalog `Chart` bar/line/area).
- **Members:** invite copy explains they will see Messages; selected role shows a one-line hint.
- **AI reply settings:** Page title matches the Messages sidebar label. Layout is **Workspace defaults** (How AI responds per channel type, Language, Sending and triage) then a visible **Mailbox exceptions** list (effective mode/language, **Custom** badge, expand to edit with Workspace default) then **Who answers** (channel routing). Workspace + mailbox overrides autosave; **Last modified** sits in the page header.
- **Decisions hub leaf (2026-09):** Open DecisionRequests are a first-class Communication sidebar folder (**Waiting on you** / **Wacht op jou**, formerly Decisions) at `/communication/decisions` (`view: awaiting_decision`, no folder scope so customer + internal cards share one list). `decisionsPath()` builds that URL (plus `/t/{id}` and merged extras such as `message` or `agent`). Legacy `?filter=needsDecision` and `/communication/runs/awaiting-decision` redirect here; the sticky inbox Needs-decision chip is removed so folder switches stay independent. Empty state + auto-select first match (orphan detail is replaced). Overview Needs attention, bell notifications, command palette, Agents card counts, and keyboard **5** deep-link to this leaf. Decision-card chrome: NL source label **projectqueue**, decision ID under **Technisch**, internal open-decision composer defaults to a neutral note (not Message Platform PO). Agent-runs / **AI check-ins** chips stay All / Updates / Results.
- **Communication one chat (2026-09):** One `ThreadDetail` timeline for customer, assistant, and AI check-in threads. Composer modes are `reply` | `ask` | `note` (chip + placeholders); assistant/internal expose only ask+note. `AiStatusChip` in the header shows effective channel AI mode + takeover (`ai_paused`); takeover banner and header icon are gone. Reply-type decision requests load as a composer draft (purple glow for ~2s; send with the normal composer control; discard rejects) with a compact timeline line; other decisions keep a card titled Waiting for your OK. `AgentChatView` is deleted. Thread payloads include `ai_mode` from `channel_ai.resolve_ai_mode`. Agent auto-reply after customer send runs as a background task (`_schedule_agent_reply`). Hot indexes (Alembic 053): `signals(tenant_id, status, last_message_at)`, `(tenant_id, has_unread, status)`, `(tenant_id, assigned_user_id, status)`, `signal_messages(signal_id, created_at)`. List previews use `row_number` (newest user + newest non-placeholder). Open-decision badges use `count(distinct)`. Frontend: quiet `fetchThreads`, `useAiChatStream` 64ms SSE flush, no post-send detail refetch, memo `ThreadListItem`, Virtuoso `ThreadTimeline`.
- **ChannelStatus single truth (2026-09):** `GET /api/channels/status` returns per-channel `summary` (`ready` | `setup` | `action` | `paused` | `broken`) plus `can_send` / `can_receive`, and workspace flags `email_ready` / `send_ready`. Setup checklist, Connections mailbox counts, Kanalen, and composer banners all derive from the channel registry (not mere OAuth/calendar existence). Calendar-only connections show “Agenda gekoppeld — mail nog niet verzendklaar”.
- **Autonoom gate (2026-09):** Govern posture `autonomous` is blocked until a live chat model is ready and at least one channel can send. API `PUT /govern/posture` returns 400 otherwise; the UI disables the Autonoom card with the same reasons. Workspace banner warns when AI runs in mock (placeholders are never labeled as sent to the customer).
- **Front desk defaults (2026-09):** New tenants get a **Front desk** customer agent; the website widget `default_agent_id` binds to it. After human takeover (`ai_paused`), the composer stays on **Reply**. Chat widget Install warns when the embed snippet uses a local development URL (`localhost` / `127.0.0.1`).
- **NL/MKB signal types (2026-09):** Platform case-type seeds are NL (`Factuur/betaling`, `Klacht`, `Storing`, `Functieverzoek`, `Spam of misbruik`). `ensure_platform_case_types` renames prior EN seed labels in place when the tenant still carries a known platform name. Dashboard `signalTypeLabel` shares catalog labels across ThreadCasesList, WhatsNextDialog and Govern type dials.
- **Module status honesty (2026-09):** Marketplace/Connections badges: **Not installed** → **Needs setup** / **Nog inrichten** → **Installation incomplete** / **Installatie incompleet** → **Connected** / **Gekoppeld**. Only Connected is green; installed without packages uses accent, not success. Legacy redirects: `/marketplace` → `/connections/marketplace`, `/settings/privacy` → `/settings/trust`, `/settings/providers` → `/settings/models`, `/settings/signal-types` → `/settings/action-tags`.
- **Govern in the rail (2026-09):** Govern is a rail tab in the **Control** group (`communication`, `agenda`, `govern`) pointing at `/settings/govern`; `tabFromPath` resolves `/settings/govern` and `/govern` to `govern` before the generic `/settings` match. Autonomy is governed there only — the per-project **Autonomous mode** toggle on `/projects/{id}` is gone (one dial in Govern). A module workspace page now shows **Connections** in the topbar instead of Modules.
- **Projects framing (2026-09):** Projects are longer-running work with a goal, documentation and a queue, not a way to group conversations. Project cards lead with the project agent; **Open threads** is a quiet secondary shortcut into Communication. Operator wording is **project agent** (not Lead) and queue kinds read Improvement / Problem / Request / Idea / Risk instead of Feature / Bug / Task.
- **Channel rename + reply From (2026-09):** Operators rename a channel via **Channels** row menu (**Rename** → `PATCH /api/channels/accounts/{id}` `label`). The Communication reply tab shows the mailbox display name + provider icon; with multiple sendable email mailboxes a picker switches From. `POST /api/signals/{id}/reply` accepts optional `channel_account_id` and rebinds the thread to that email mailbox before delivery (visibility + send checks). Cross-medium hops are out of scope.
- **Workspace hub portal (2026-09):** `WorkspaceHubLayout` mounts `PersonalAssistantWidget` (same in-app Bokito FAB as inside a tenant). Page context for `/workspaces` and `/account` is explicit so the helper knows the hub screen. Sidebar **Workspaces** links to `/workspaces` (not `/`, which still resumes the last tenant). **Settings** nav item opens `/account`. Footer user row shows `UserAvatar` (photo when set) without a redundant profile icon.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, SMB-owner pass)
- **Leftover suggestion cards** collapse to one line (`Earlier draft — dismissed` / `Eerder concept — terzijde gelegd`) once resolved or deferred. Feedback thumbs stay off those leftover cards. Approving one Suggested reply defers sibling Suggested reply cards (`human_approved_sibling`). `_defer_open_reply_suggestions` only matches `title == "Suggested reply"` so other awaiting decisions stay open.
- **Draft cleaning:** `split_suggestion` strips echoed invoke/draft instructions (`A teammate asked you…`) and `> Note for the reviewer` lines. `POST /signals/{id}/draft` and invoke-agent `reply_suggestion` run the same cleaner. The dashboard `stripAiScaffolding` / `translateMockAgentBody` hides leftover leaked prompts in already-stored cards.
- **Needs reply must not swap the inbox for setup.** `isInboxEmpty` is first-run only: no search, quick filter All, and no open thread. Search empty states offer **Clear search**. Mailbox load errors and missing-org screens have Retry / Sign out. Server `needs_reply=1` ignores mock/placeholder `agent_message` bodies (`I received your message about:` / `[mock]`), matching list previews — a stub reply does not count as answered.
- **Decision buttons** for less-technical operators: **Not now** / **I'll handle it myself** (NL **Niet nu** / **Ik doe het zelf**) on the canonical single escalate option (`id=escalate`). Agent-authored multi-choice cards keep each option's own label even when several share `action_type=escalate` — the UI must not collapse them all to "I'll handle it myself". Prefer `send_reply` + `payload.body_text` for choices that should mail the customer (e.g. ask for clarification). Policy gate options `always_auto` translate to **Always allow** / **Altijd toestaan**. In production, `call_mcp_tool` against `mock://` MCP servers returns unavailable to the agent instead of creating a dead-end approval card. Customer-facing email rewrites relative `/docs` / `/learn` markdown links to absolute `public_app_url` links (`format_customer_email_body`) and expands mistaken `/docs/{slug}` (missing section) via `get_article`. `search_product_help` returns `path`, `docs_path`, and `public_url` so agents cite `/docs/{section}/{slug}` exactly. When suggest-mode ends with bare `Done.` (no decision card) or a meta draft about Govern/concepts, the worker raises a **Needs your attention** card instead of leaving the thread empty. Defer toast is **Saved for later**.
- **Empty states:** Contacts filtered-empty has **Clear filters** and always shows the Pending chip hint. Activity filter-no-matches is distinct from “no activity yet”. Agent library empty search clears the query. Agent-runs Decisions chip empty CTA includes **Back to Open**. Members without invite permission see **Ask an admin**. Assistant missing config is a primary button to `/settings/assistant`. Delete confirm uses i18n.
- **Cockpit:** at most one promo banner besides onboarding (guide and hourly-scan hide while setup is incomplete). Autonomy % always shows “Share of replies sent without you clicking Send.” Tokens tile is **AI usage this month**. Review CTA is **workspace changes to approve**. Open-decisions empty copy points at customer threads.
- **New conversation** treats **Email a customer** and **Chat with assistant** as equal chips. Reply composer CC/BCC placeholders are i18n. The thread-list shortcut footer was later removed (shortcuts stay behind **?**). Agent-runs chip row has a muted subtitle. Govern Agent access tab has a one-line hint. Agent library model chips keep the pretty label and put the raw id in the tooltip.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, power-user pass)
- **Keyboard is shared:** `useInboxListShortcuts` drives Communication and Assistant/agent chats. J/K move and scroll the active row into view. Esc leaves the thread (back to the list). H snoozes 1 hour (undo reopens). X toggles bulk select. R refuses closed/spam. Shift+A opens the assignee picker. N starts a new chat. L copies the canonical link. `#` copies the thread id. 1–5 switch All / Needs reply / Unread / Pinned / Needs decision. `/` works even if compose is open, unless focus is inside the dialog. New conversation Esc returns to the last inbox queue.
- **AND list flags:** `GET /api/signals` accepts `unread=1`, `needs_reply=1`, `needs_decision=1`, `pinned=1` on top of the current `view`/`folder`. `needs_reply` is open + (unread or last inbound `user_message` newer than last outbound reply). `needs_decision` is threads with an open `DecisionRequest`. Serialized threads include `has_open_decision` for the list badge. Human outbound replies are `user_message`/`outbound`, so they must count as answered; since 2026-10 an outbound `system_event` (handled outside Bokito) counts too and drives `last_message_direction`. The dashboard sends the flags from the Unread / Needs reply / Needs decision / Pinned chips so they compose with Open/Mine/etc. Live gateway upserts honor the same flags in `threadMatchesFilters`.

#### Conversation currency: stale drafts, outside contact, channel switches (2026-10)
- **Reply proposals carry an anchor.** A suggestion `DecisionRequest` (titles in `REPLY_SUGGESTION_TITLES`) stores `based_on_message_id` = the inbound message it answers; `serialize_message` exposes it under `payload.decision.based_on_message_id` next to `resolution_reason` and `superseded_by_signal_id`. `process_inbound_signal` re-checks that anchor before persisting: a run that finishes after a newer inbound message landed is dropped (stale run) instead of posting an outdated proposal.
- **Deferral reasons** (`signal_threads.py`, stored in `DecisionRequest.chosen_option_id` with status `deferred`): `human_replied` (a person answered), `superseded_by_inbound` (the customer wrote again; the new run posts a fresh card), `superseded_by_external_reply` (a Sent-items copy shows the team already answered from their mailbox), `sibling_thread` (the same contact got a newer proposal on another channel), `handled_externally` (operator or agent marked the conversation as handled by phone/WhatsApp/mail elsewhere). Deferred cards stay on the timeline as parked; the composer draft that was taken from them is treated as stale.
- **NO_REPLY_NEEDED after reasoning:** `extract_no_reply_summary` also recognises the sentinel when the model puts it after a short analysis; the meta-draft guard catches drafts that only describe the mail ("this email is a notification about...") so they never reach the composer. Prompt rule tightened accordingly.
- **Composer drafts (frontend, `lib/inbox-ops.ts`):** `localStorage` key `inbox.draft.{threadId}` holds `{body, cc, bcc, decisionMessageId?, basedOnMessageId?}`. A draft is only written after the operator touches it (an untouched AI prefill is not persisted). On restore, `isStoredDraftStale` flags it when its proposal card is no longer open or its anchor is not the newest inbound message; the composer shows **Outdated draft / Verouderd concept** with **Propose again** (asks the agent for a fresh proposal on the current thread) and **Discard**. An untouched restored draft keeps its original anchors on write-back so a reload cannot make a stale draft look current. Drafts are cleared on close, spam and handled-externally. A conversation that ended with a "No reply needed" card opens the composer on **Note**.
- **Sent items are read along (Gmail, Microsoft 365).** `InboundMessage.direction` distinguishes inbound mail from Sent copies; `parse_address_list` fills `recipient_addresses` from To/Cc (header strings or Graph recipient lists). `_ingest_outbound_copy` (`channels/base.py`) logs a Sent copy as a team reply: external recipients only (the mailbox itself and workspace members are stripped), dedupe on provider id, RFC `Message-ID` and the stored outbound row Bokito sent itself, author = mailbox owner via `mailbox_owner_user_id`, message metadata `origin=external_mailbox`. It only lands on an existing conversation or starts one for a **known (approved) contact**; mail to unknown recipients raises `UnknownRecipientError` and stays out of Bokito. The copy never marks the thread unread or reopens it, clears `has_unread` when it is the newest message, parks an open proposal (`superseded_by_external_reply`, plus a `suggestion_deferred` event) and writes a `reply_logged_externally` event. The timeline shows it as an outbound bubble by the matching member (falls back to the mail's own sender name) with an Outlook/Gmail/mailbox label; a forwarded mail carries `forwarded_from` so the bubble shows who the original mail came from. Docs: `/docs/inbox/channels` "Read Sent items along".
- **Related conversations (per channel, shown to each other).** `services/related_conversations.py` finds sibling threads of the same canonical Contact (email/phone fallback), excluding `INTERNAL_CHANNELS`; thread payloads carry `related_conversations[]` (`id, channel, subject, status, last_message_at, last_message_direction, last_message_preview, has_open_proposal`). The dashboard shows one `RelatedConversationBanner` above the composer when a sibling had activity within 7 days ("{name} also wrote {when} via {channel}" / "Your team replied ...", with **Open**), and the contact panel lists **Other conversations / Andere gesprekken** (open/pending first, newest next, open-proposal mark). Conversations stay separate per channel; there is no merged timeline. The inbound agent receives a `recent_contact_context` prompt block with the sibling activity, and `_defer_sibling_thread_suggestions` parks older open proposals on sibling threads whose latest inbound message is older than the new one, so a contact gets at most one live proposal.
- **Already handled outside Bokito.** `POST /api/signals/{id}/handled-externally` (`HandledExternallyBody`: `channel` phone|whatsapp|email|other, `note`, `close`, `language`) → `signal_threads.mark_handled_externally`: writes an outbound `system_event` (`metadata.handled_externally` with channel, by_name, note; body localized server-side from the request language, client renders `timeline.handledExternally.{channel}` from the structured payload), clears unread, defers open proposals (`handled_externally`), picks the thread up for the actor, optionally closes it (normal close path + `signal.closed` webhook), writes a `handled_externally` SignalEvent and audit `signal:handled_externally`. UI: **What next → Handled elsewhere / Buitenom afgehandeld** tab and the composer send-menu item **Already handled outside Bokito / Al afgehandeld buiten Bokito** (`HandledExternallyForm`: channel chips, optional note, "Also close" default on); the send menu stays reachable with an empty body for this action. Agent tool `mark_handled_externally` (category `messaging`, same gate as `close_thread`) does the same; without a user in context the actor falls back to the thread assignee, then the agent's `owner_user_id`. Docs: `/docs/inbox/communication` "Already handled outside Bokito".

#### Modules in the rail (2026-08-28)
- **Connections** (`modules` tab) lives in the rail **Organization** group with **Workforce** and **Settings** (`TAB_GROUPS`), path `/connections` (not under Settings layout), Lucide `Plug` icon. Active highlighting uses `tabFromPath` matching `/connections`. Legacy `/settings/modules*` redirects to `/connections*`. Settings sidebar no longer lists Modules; Connected / Marketplace / MCP stay under Integrations.
- Installed modules in the sidebar **Modules** group use per-slug Lucide icons via `moduleNavIcon` (`accounting` Calculator, `banking` Landmark, `investing` TrendingUp, `documents` FileText; fallback BookOpen) — not the catalog `Boxes` icon.
- A temporary **New** / **Nieuw** text badge (`isNewTab`, `tabs.modules.newBadge`) sits next to the label (dot when the rail is collapsed). Count badges for inbox/agents are unchanged.
- Module home: attached registrations (`ModuleConnectionsPanel`) sit at the top of `/connections/:slug` (not a tab). Remaining tabs: Overview (writes, customer tools, roster, verbs), Sources (`ModuleSource`), Setup (company-assistant deep-link). Legacy `?tab=connections` lands on Overview. Marketplace module cards: not installed = **Install** only; installed = grey **Manage** then red **Uninstall**. Coming-soon modules (Investing, Documents) render at 50% opacity; planned partners (Tink, Yapily, Knab, Twelve Data, Bitvavo, TradingView, Google Drive, OneDrive/SharePoint, Dropbox) are coming_soon marketplace cards and logos on the module card.

#### Communication chat chrome unification (2026-09)
- Shared recipe in `lib/chat-chrome.ts`: thread header bar, action cluster, transcript column (`max-w-[820px]`), composer dock, AI composer left strip, list selected/AI row tints.
- Agent chats (`AgentChatView`) use the same `ChatMessageBubble` variants as customer timelines (`self` / `agent`), matching header height/padding and action cluster; always-on AI disclosure sub-bar removed (subtitle carries agent + disclosure).
- List rows: denser default (`compact` unless user chose comfortable); AI-decision rows use CSS `[data-ai-managed]` strip color instead of inset glow; selection is fill-only.
- Email inbound cards use the same radius language as chat bubbles (`rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm`).

#### Project canvas (snapshot nodes) (2026-10)
- **What it is:** a canvas is a child node of a **Project** or of the **Tenant** (Overview). Many canvases per parent (`owner_kind` + `owner_id` + `slug`). Snapshot document (`source` + `tree_json`, `schema_version` 2) compiled from `bokito/canvas`. Operators add/delete and set metadata (title, brief in `notes`, managing agent, refresh cadence). Agents write source under Govern (`resource_type=project_canvas`). Not an eighth rail noun; not the `/os` graph (`canvas_node`).
- **Refresh:** `refresh_cadence` is `manual` | `hourly` | `daily` (default on create) | `weekly` | `monthly`. Daily/weekly/monthly are Agenda `cron` wakes at 07:00 UTC (weekly Monday, monthly day 1); hourly is `interval` 60. Manual uses a one-shot `once` trigger for the first write only. Create stores the operator brief in `notes` and sets `next_run_at=now` so the managing agent starts immediately. Default manager: project `po_agent_id` else lead agent; tenant canvases use the lead agent.
- **API:** create/patch accept `refresh_cadence` and `notes` (legacy `refresh_minutes` still maps). `GET/POST /api/workforce/canvases?owner_kind=&owner_id=`, `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/workforce/canvases/{id}` (PATCH = metadata only). Nested `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/workforce/projects/{id}/canvases[/{slug}]`. Source writes only via Govern apply / `put_canvas_source`.
- **Agents:** `get_project_canvas`, `update_project_canvas`, `create_canvas`, `delete_canvas`.
- **UI:** `CanvasHost` is a left-to-right tab strip; **Add canvas** sits to the right of existing canvas names (modal: title, brief, cadence). Extra names wrap instead of scrolling. Empty canvases show that the managing agent is writing. Workspace canvases live on Overview **Canvas** (`/cockpit/canvas`). Canvas `BarChart` / `LineChart`, the Overview token sparkline, and the Usage **Token use** card (axes, same series endpoint, selected period) all render catalog `Chart` (Recharts, Bokito tokens).

#### Uniform folder system (channels and agents) (2026-08-28)
- **One repeatable folder model**: every channel, personal assistant, and company agent in the Communication sidebar is a `SidebarFolder` with the same sub-queues — **Open, Mine, Unassigned, Closed** (`SUB_QUEUES`). Clicking a folder expands it (and opens the default sub-view); clicking again collapses it. Sub-items stay hidden until then.
- **All communication** (2026-08): the fixed top inbox is the same folder pattern — one `SidebarFolder` labelled **All communication** / **Alle communicatie** (`support.inbox.allCommunication`) instead of flat All / Mine / Open / Unassigned / More. Scope key `inbox`; `HubLeaf` inbox queue is optional. Expand opens the configured default (Open or Mine from `inbox_folders`), with **Snoozed** and **Spam** as extra rows under the standard four. Closed lives in the standard sub-list (no separate More for it).
- **Accordion**: only one folder's sub-list is open at a time (`expandedLeaves` holds a single scope key) to keep the rail scannable.
- **Team group chat (2026-10):** pinned custom teams expose **Group chat** / **Groepschat**. `POST /api/teams/{id}/room` get-or-creates one internal `Signal` (`channel=internal`, `source=team`, owner = that team). Empty rooms have no turn, so they stay out of **For you** until someone posts; hub Open still includes them via `assignee_kind=team`. For you also ignores `source=team` ownership so an empty Groepschat does not sit under **Jij bent nodig**. Composer defaults to a note. The header names the team, not the handling agent. Thread-list fetches key on `team_id` so a team folder does not reuse the All-communication list. Agent-card **need a decision** keeps `agent` and `needs_decision=1` on thread URLs. System teams have no room. Handoff bell copy is translated client-side. Agenda week view shows overdue look-ats outside the visible week. Empty **For you** no longer swaps in the setup checklist.
- **Agent paths** carry the same queues: `/communication/agent/{id}/open|mine|unassigned|closed` (+ `/t/:threadId`). Company-agent leaves use the shared `Communication` shell (`configForLeaf` → `folder=assistant` + `agentId`, mode `customer`) so close / assign / snooze / bulk match Alle communicatie. Legacy `/communication/assistant*` and `/communication/direct/*` redirect away; `DirectCommunication.tsx` is removed.
- **Alle communicatie includes agent chats (2026-09):** backend `folder=inbox` lists customer channels plus assistant chats the operator may see (`owner_user_id` is self or null); internal agent-run threads stay under Agent-runs. Open queue client filter (`customersOnly`) drops only `channel/folder=internal` runs, not `channel=assistant`. Badge counts use the same hub scope. Thread payloads carry `folder` as `external` (customer channels), `assistant` (agent chats) or `internal` (agent runs); `isAgentRunThread()` ignores `channel=assistant` regardless of folder (fixed 2026-09: assistant chats were stamped `folder=internal` and vanished from Open). `AgentChatView` (via `ThreadDetail`) exposes assign + close/reopen like customer chrome.
- **Communication memory window (2026-09):** `GET /api/signals/{id}` returns the newest 80 messages by default (`limit`, `before` cursor) with `has_older` / `oldest_message_id`. Timeline events in that window are capped (~100 newest). Chat/assistant/WhatsApp windows omit `body_html` and `agent_trace` (flags `has_html` / `has_agent_trace`); email keeps HTML inline. `GET /api/signals/{id}/messages/{message_id}` loads the full payload when the operator expands activity. Virtuoso auto-loads older pages on `startReached` (button remains). Opening a thread lands at the bottom of the timeline; a new inbound email lands at the **start** of that mail. The bottom pin is interruptible: a wheel/touch upward cancels the short (~280ms) height-settle snap, `followOutput` only runs while actually at the bottom, and `atBottomThreshold` is 72px (was 200px plus a 1.8s forced follow that fought the operator). New messages while scrolled up show the **New messages** chip instead of yanking the viewport. Gateway `thread` events patch the open row; other non-message events debounce a quiet refetch (~400ms) instead of hitting the newest 80 on every triage burst. HTML email iframes and attachment thumbs mount lazily near the viewport. The thread list uses `react-virtuoso`. Stream text RAF stops when caught up; gateway deltas batch ~64ms. Unread dots pulse only on the selected row. List poll skips when the tab is hidden.
- **Nav column is resizable** (`MessagesHub` SplitPane, max width 380px). Folder default sub-view still roams via `/me/preferences` `inbox_folders`.

#### Uniform mappensysteem per kanaal (2026-08-28)
- **One repeatable sub-view set** (Front-model): every channel folder in the Communication sidebar nests the same queues — **Open, Mine (Van mij), Unassigned (Niet toegewezen), Closed (Gesloten)** (`SUB_QUEUES` in `messages-paths.ts`). URLs include `/communication/channel/email/12/mine` and `/communication/channel/webchat/open` (+ `/t/:threadId`). `HubLeaf` channel leaves carry `queue?`; `configForLeaf` maps the sub-queue to server views (`all_open`/`mine`/`unassigned`/`closed`); a folder without queue keeps view `all`.
- **Sidebar folders** (`SidebarFolder`/`QueueSublist` in `QueueSublist.tsx`): clicking a folder opens its default sub-view and expands; the chevron expands/collapses without navigating.
