Expansion state persists in `communication-sidebar-prefs` (`expandedLeaves`, keyed by `folderScopeKey`). A folder auto-expands once when it becomes active.
- **Default sub-view is configurable and roams**: `/me/preferences` stores `inbox_folders` (`default_queue` + `channel_defaults` keyed by folder scope key like `channel:email:12`). Global default is Open. UI: Settings → Email & messages → **Folders** (`FoldersAndTagsManager`).
- **Folders and tags (2026-10):** the sidebar **Folders** group holds saved filters and project folders; tags come from the registry. Both are managed in Settings → Channels (`FoldersAndTagsManager`: default sub-view, `SavedFoldersSection`, `TagRegistrySection`). See section 47.
- **Channel leaf filter integrity (2026-08):** Inbox-only UI `channelFilter` must not overwrite leaf-scoped `channel`. The old merge `channel: channelFilter ?? undefined` wiped Websitechat (`webchat` → API `widget`), WhatsApp, and Slack so those folders listed all open threads (mostly email). `mergeHubThreadFilters` in `lib/hub-list-filters.ts` applies the chip only on inbox leaves; channel leaves keep `channel=widget|whatsapp|slack`. Email mailbox leaves also send `channel=email` with `connectionId`. Wrong-scope deep links on channel leaves redirect via `threadFitsChannelLeaf`. Live client filter set aligns with API `EXTERNAL_CHANNELS` (includes `whatsapp`, `api`).
- **Cohesion pass (2026-08):** Approvals stay on their source conversation; the Communication sidebar **Decisions** leaf lists every open card. Agent library cards add a **Runs** quick link (`?agent=`). Modules cards (when on) link to Agents and Govern policy. Cockpit/notifications copy uses **Communication** (not Messages) so it matches the rail.

#### Person avatars (2026-08-28)
- **One avatar convention for external people** (`PersonAvatar` in `components/ui/PersonAvatar.tsx`): a known name renders colored initials (first + last word, color seeded by address), an email-only contact renders initials from the local part (`john.doe@…` → JD, via `getEmailInitials` in `lib/avatar.ts`), and an unknown person (website visitor, placeholder address like `visitor@web` / `cust_…`) renders a muted circle with a question mark — never a domain favicon or globe. Used for people on the contact panel, Contacts page and Cockpit recent contacts.
- **Domain favicons are for companies only** (`DomainFavicon`, `host` prop on company cards; Google S2). Google S2 serves a generic 16px globe with HTTP 200 for unknown domains; `DomainFavicon` detects that via `naturalWidth <= 16` on load and falls back to initials.
- **Thread list counterparty** (`ContactAvatar`, 2026-10): org senders on a business domain (not free mail, name not a multi-word personal name) use `DomainFavicon` again — restoring logos for Google / Moneybird / Resend after an earlier pass that forced `PersonAvatar` on every external row. People and free-mail stay on `PersonAvatar`. Choice: `lib/company-avatar.ts` (`preferCompanyFavicon`, aligned with `FREE_EMAIL_DOMAINS` on the API).
- **Channel glyphs are SVG icons** (`ChannelGlyph`): email renders the lucide Mail icon and widget/webchat renders MessageCircleMore. Only true third-party brands (Slack, WhatsApp) show a logo image via `CHANNEL_BRAND`; the smtp and bokito brand logos are no longer used as channel icons.

#### Contact context panel + data router (2026-08-28)
- **Contact context panel is open by default** in Communication and agent chats; closing it (X or the header PanelRight toggle) lasts only for the current browser session (`sessionStorage` key `inbox.contactPanel.open`), so it returns on the next visit.
- **SplitRow only accepts direct `SplitPane` children** — anything else (including a wrapping fragment) is silently dropped. The Communication context pane must be a direct conditional child; the mobile slide-over lives outside the `SplitRow`.
- **The dashboard runs on a data router**: `main.tsx` uses `createBrowserRouter` with a single `path: '*'` route wrapping the provider tree + `App` (which keeps its descendant `<Routes>`). This is required for `useBlocker` in `useUnsavedChangesGuard` (contact notes, workspace/messenger/project settings forms); a plain `BrowserRouter` makes those pages throw.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08-28, filter-and-daily polish)
- **Chip race:** clicking All must not snap back to Needs decision. URL sync only reads `?filter=` (it no longer writes the last chip back when state updates first). A stale list fetch is ignored; the list shows a skeleton until the matching page is ready.
- **Esc vs menus:** Escape closes an open menu first. It does not leave the conversation while a menu or dialog is open.
- **Saved searches** prompt for a short name (`suggestSavedSearchName` strips `from:` / `to:` and shortens).
- **New email** has the same **Templates** library as the thread composer.
- **Mailbox last sync** sits next to Channel settings on the list (`Mail 8m ago`).
- **Contact notes** use the shared unsaved-changes guard on in-app navigation, not only `beforeunload`.
- **Cockpit Needs attention** can snooze a row until tomorrow 9:00 without opening the thread.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08-28, daily-workflow pass)
- **Needs decision / Decisions leaf:** Rows still show a **Needs decision** badge. Cockpit empty “awaiting decision” and legacy `?filter=needsDecision` open `/communication/decisions`, not Agent-runs.
- **Defer parks the thread:** Decision Later / Defer resolves the card and snoozes the conversation until tomorrow 9:00 so it leaves Open. Bulk **Snooze until tomorrow 9:00** and the list indicator menu do the same (`POST /api/signals/bulk` action `snooze`).
- **Agenda clicks open customer threads:** Agenda search no longer forces `folder=internal`. A matching customer thread opens in Communication; internal matches still go to Agent-runs.
- **Auto-open does not mark read:** Opening `/communication/inbox/open` still selects the first thread for display, but skip-mark-read until the operator clicks it.
- **Contact panel:** Identity only — no Who chrome; collapse control on the name row. Kind labels: **Unknown chatter** (grey) for unnamed visitors, **Contact** (grey) for a saved person, **AI agent** (`text-ai-ink`) for agents, **Blocked** when blocked. **Last seen** sits on the same grey line under the kind for contacts, unknowns, teammates and agents. Named contact title and avatar link to `/contacts/{id}` (same pattern as agent name → agent page). No Unlink or Block in the panel; block lives under the thread menu (⋯) as **Block {name}** and on the contact page. **+ Contact** appears only until the conversation is linked to a saved person. Notes with unsaved warning (`beforeunload`), previous conversations, profile. On an agent thread, tools/scopes sit under the agent (not under **This conversation**) plus **Configure** to `/agents/{id}`. Signals for the open conversation sit under **This conversation** below identity; look-at stays on thread chrome / WhatsNext.
- **Create task** is always in the thread ⋮ menu on customer conversations. Closed/spam composer **Reopen** is a filled button. Email Send shows **Ctrl+Enter**.
- **Search debounce:** the search box stays instant; list fetches use `listSearch` (300ms). Clearing search fetches immediately.
- **Remembered inbox:** `bokito.inbox.lastQueue` and `bokito.inbox.quickFilter` persist. `/communication` and `/` resume the last queue. Compact density is `bokito.inbox.density`.
- **Copy / undo:** conversation link is canonical (`threadHubPath` + origin). More menu also copies the thread id. Close and spam toasts can undo (reopen). Activity **Clear list** keeps a snapshot for undo. Audit activity links use `threadHubPath` (Open, not All).
- **Bulk:** Assign to me, Pin, Unpin, and Snooze until tomorrow sit on the selection bar. Command palette adds Closed, Spam, Agent runs, Assistant, Needs reply, Needs decision, and searches numeric/UUID ids from the first character.
- **Govern / Setup / Agents:** Govern "Open in Communication" uses Open (the hub redirects internal threads to Agent runs). Setup Communication pillar has **AI reply settings**. Agent history empty-state Communication CTA opens Agent runs.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, inspectability pass)
- **Range select and unread nav:** Shift-click a list checkbox selects the inclusive range from the last anchor (`toggleOrRangeSelect`). Cmd/Ctrl+A selects loaded rows when the list is focused (not the composer). **]** / **[** and Shift+J/K jump only unread threads. Shift+U marks the current thread read. **Mark loaded as read** bulk-reads the current list page, not the whole server queue.
- **Shareable chips:** `?filter=needsReply|needsDecision|unread|pinned` (aliases `needs_reply`, `needs_decision`, `awaiting_decision`) syncs with the quick-filter chips. Search and label filters persist when switching queues. List scroll position is restored per leaf in `sessionStorage`. The timeline no longer snaps to the bottom on every new message; scrolled-up readers see an **N new** jump pill.
- **Inspect IDs:** message bubbles copy message ID and plain text via hover-only icon buttons; live decision cards copy the decision ID via an icon (resolved one-liners dropped copy-ID); the thread menu copies `graphConversationId` when set. Activity rows copy run / thread / agent IDs and show an absolute timestamp tooltip. Event pills expose payload JSON on hover. Govern audit rows expand to JSON + copy. Agent traces have **Copy trace**.
- **Composer drafts** persist as JSON `{body,cc,bcc}` (legacy plain text still restores as the body). A **Draft restored** chip can discard. Scheduled outbound bubbles have persistent **Cancel send**. Failed sends offer **Copy text**.
- **Extra filters:** assignee and channel go to `GET /api/signals`; priority is client-side on the loaded page. Partial chip counts show a “loaded only” tooltip. Infinite scroll loads the next page via an IntersectionObserver sentinel.
- **Palette:** thread search returns 12 hits plus **View all matches in Communication**. A UUID/numeric query offers **Open conversation by ID**; two IDs open an agent run. Saved searches live in `bokito.inbox.savedSearches`.
- **Chrome:** ConnectionStatus is i18n. Cockpit attention rows show a channel glyph and the thread id in the title. Contact previous-conversation rows use the channel glyph.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, workforce/setup pass)
- **Agents:** Members can read an agent page (no redirect to inbox). Library search is always on; pills All / Working / Paused / Lead filter the grid. New agent defaults to Communication, shows role hints, templates, optional project link, and a model retry. Detail keeps Chat / Agenda / threads / Pause / Lead primary; Knowledge, Inbox AI, Setup, Duplicate and Archive sit in **More actions**. Archive is an in-page confirm that offers Pause first. Autonomy has a one-line consequence plus a Govern posture link. Tools use humanized labels. Model cost shows Low / Medium / High (cents stay in the tooltip).
- **Projects:** Members can browse projects read-only. Create is name-first (slug under Advanced). Cards hide the slug. After create, the operator lands on the project. Repo without GitHub points to Integrations. Lead can be changed. Remaining budget shows token units and a cap tooltip. Empty flows offer **Add the first step on Agenda**.
- **Knowledge:** New docs take a title and generate `docs/{slug}.md`. Search has a button plus 300ms debounce. Empty state is a PDF/Word drop zone. The sidebar lists only kinds that have documents (no fixed filter chips for Voice / Daily notes / etc.). Scope chips show **Platform**, **Project**, or **Agent** under each name. AI-maintained docs show a banner. Publish asks before going public.
- **Contacts:** Create has a channel picker. Pending lists have Approve all / Block all. Row actions stay visible. Dirty contact/company forms use a router blocker. Empty companies auto-run company backfill once per session.
- **Agenda / Setup / Govern:** Empty week days offer Schedule. Automations empty state has Create automation. `kind` persists in the URL. Setup has a sixth Projects pillar and an Intelligence action **Organize work by project**. Govern remembers `bokito.govern.lastTab` and defaults to Policy when there is no last tab and no pending drafts. Posture cards list what changes. Members show a role matrix. Inbox AI save sticks when dirty. Widget copy offers Check installation (preview). Cockpit empty metrics link to Setup.

#### Auth/Knowledge operator walk (2026-08, less-techy pass)
- **Sign-in / verify / reset / invite:** Login remembers the last email on this device and can switch EN/NL. Verify-email pending shows a spinner, prefills `?email=`, and can copy the local dev link. The signed-in verify banner allows another resend after 60 seconds. A reset link without a token shows an error card instead of bouncing to login; after a successful reset the page counts down and **Sign in now** skips the wait. Accept-invite keeps a full-width **Back to sign in** and submits the welcome name on Enter.
- **New conversation / direct chat:** The last recipient is remembered. `?agent=` preselects an agent and `?to=` opens compose. Load failures offer **Try again**. When more than eight people match, **Open Contacts** is shown. Direct chat no longer swallows target-load errors; empty and error states explain the next step.
- **Knowledge / Projects / Branding:** Knowledge warns on unsaved edits, saves with Ctrl/Cmd+S, copies the document path, shows last refresh, and filters by kind. Duplicate agent names on Knowledge show role/slug so two Front desk agents are distinguishable. Projects search can be cleared, create submits on Enter, and `?new=1` (command palette **New project**) opens the dialog. Project detail tabs sync to `?tab=` (`canvas`/`docs`/`settings`; legacy `queue` lands on Home). Linked knowledge queue chips open `?item=` on Home and expand that row. The Project tab shows one project-agent row (status and last active under the name); admins hover to assign any active company agent as the project default (`PATCH /po-agent` is no longer limited to orchestrator/po roles). Budget caps stay on `Project.token_budget_daily` / `token_budget_hourly`; the card shows usage, and **Edit** in the header opens the cap modal. Branding Save stays disabled until something changed; the workspace URL can be copied and public help previewed.
- **Help / widget:** Help centers create a collection on Enter, confirm collection delete, search documents, and show last refresh. Public help can clear search, copy an article URL, and explains an empty index. Widget appearance Save also tracks Voice fields and availability settings; Ctrl/Cmd+S saves.

#### Inbox density cleanup (2026-08)
- **Composer hints live on hover:** the Enter / Ctrl+Enter send hints sit in the Send button tooltip; Reply/Note tab reminders sit on the tab tooltips; the signature note is a tooltip on the recipient row. No always-on hint sentences under the composer (ReplyComposer, DirectChatPanel, NewConversationPage).
- **Split Send button:** the customer-reply Send button has a chevron with **Send and close** and **Send and snooze** (snooze presets in a submenu). Notes keep a single button. The NEXT label before the **Create task** chip is gone; the chip stands alone.
- **Quiet thread list:** the shortcut footer is removed and **All N conversations loaded** is a tooltip on the list. Compact density (`bokito.inbox.density`) hides assignee, ticket chip and free hashtags on a collapsible bottom row until hover, keyboard focus or selection; Comfortable list keeps that row visible. The ticket chip no longer sits on the preview line.
- **Quiet timeline:** copy message ID / copy text are icon buttons that appear on message hover (`group/msg`); failed sends keep a visible **Copy text**. `decision_created` / `suggestion_created` events are filtered out of the timeline because the cards already represent them. Resolved decision one-liners have no copy-ID; active cards use an icon-only copy-ID. **Correct this** on decision cards is an icon with a tooltip, matching the message feedback controls.

#### Email thread readability (2026-09)
- **Quote collapse:** rich email HTML in `EmailHtmlFrame` collapses Gmail/Outlook quote roots (`.gmail_quote`, `#divRplyFwdMsg`, `On … wrote` / `Op … schreef`) behind **Show quoted text** / **Toon geciteerde tekst**. Very tall bodies cap at ~480px with **Show full message**.
- **Scroll:** opening a thread scrolls to the **top of the latest message**, not the bottom of a tall iframe (so operators land on new content, not the signature footer).
- **Inline images:** inbound sync captures Content-ID / `isInline` parts (including Gmail parts without a filename; SMTP/IMAP too), stores them, and rewrites `cid:…` in `body_html` to hosted `/api/uploads/…` URLs (quoted and bare). The timeline iframe also rewrites leftover `cid:` refs from attachment metadata and sets `referrerpolicy=no-referrer` so remote ESP/Google CDN images load. Inline parts are omitted from the attachment chips under the body.
- **HTML fidelity (2026-10):** LazyEmailHtmlFrame off-screen placeholders use cleaned plain text (never raw MSO/CSS). Mislabelled HTML in `text/plain` is promoted to `body_html` on ingest (`promote_html_body`). Large Gmail parts that only expose `attachmentId` are fetched before ingest. When the timeline omits `body_html` but `has_html` is true, the bubble fetches the full message once. **Plain-path bug:** `usePlainBody` used to trigger whenever `translateMockAgentBody` trimmed whitespace (`displayBody !== plainBody`), so real HTML mail (e.g. Google) stayed on ChatText with leaked `<!--[if !mso]>` markers; HTML now wins unless a mock body was actually rewritten.
- **List previews:** `clean_message_preview` strips zero-width noise and cuts at quote markers (`From:` / `Van:` / `On … wrote` / Original Message) so the list shows only the new head.
- **AI violet cue:** thread-list purple glow is only for open decisions (`hasOpenDecision`), not every `!aiPaused` customer thread. The “AI is handling this conversation” banner shows only when the thread is human-taken-over, has an open decision, or an active agent session; the header takeover toggle remains always available.

#### Messaging sync (inline agent, 2026-09)
- Customer-thread meta sessions reuse the DirectChat pattern: optimistic user bubble, live SSE deltas in `AgentSessionCard`, composer **Stop**, Enter/Send locked while streaming (`useAgentSessionChat` + `ReplyComposer.agentStreaming`).
- API: `POST /signals/conversations/{id}/stream` rejects a second in-flight chat run with **409 `agent_busy`** (`trigger_id` = conversation id). `POST .../cancel` sets cooperative cancel (memory flag + `AgentRun.status=cancelled`); `AgentLoop` and `execute_tool` skip further tools (including `suggest_thread_reply`) so late drafts cannot land after Stop. A dropped SSE (proxy idle / client disconnect) used to leave `AgentRun=running` with no reply bubble — UI looked idle, next Send toasted busy. Fix: stream `finally` closes orphan runs; runs older than 90s are reclaimed before 409; client cancels when the SSE ends without a `done` frame; empty completed turns get a short fallback bubble.
- **Plan-only stop (2026-10):** CHAT_STYLE tells the model to announce then act in the **same turn**. If it still ends after a work announce with no `tool_use`, `AgentLoop` nudges once ("Continue now…") so tools run. Tool blocks always execute even when the provider labels the turn `end_turn`. Assistant sends use quiet thread refresh + optimistic outbound bubbles (no mid-stream hard reload that blanked the timeline).
- **Decision card placement (2026-10):** `place_turn_decisions` only moves cards whose id appears in **this** turn's tool activity. An older fallback moved every prior card with `received_at <= first bubble`, so an already-approved Bin restore showed as **Afgehandeld** inside a later turn. Heal snaps drifted stamps back to `created_at`. Consequential tools (e.g. `restore_trash_item`) must be called to raise Approve/Reject — agents must not only ask in prose.
- **Inline agent proposals (2026-10):** On internal and assistant threads the newest decision raised in a turn attaches to the **last bubble of that turn**: the bubble gets `metadata.proposal = {decision_id, question, selection}`, the card message gets `metadata.attached_to_message_id`. Showcase items live on `metadata.items` (also mirrored on the proposal). The thread API serializes `payload.proposal` (live status, options, `selection` single|multiple, card `message_id`, `chosen_option_id` / `chosen_option_ids`, `resolved_at`, resolver name) and `payload.items`. Timeline: item previews inside the bubble, buttons under it, attached card hidden. After an answer the operator gets a **user bubble** with the chosen label(s) plus chosen item snapshots (`decision_response`); buttons fold into a short *Answered* line and the `decision_*` event pill is hidden when that reply exists. Those operator decision replies do **not** reopen a closed customer thread (`append_signal_chat_message` skips reopen when `metadata.decision_response` is set) — approving Close thread stays closed. Customer channels never attach inline. DecisionRequest stays the single object.
- **Two presentation layers:** (A) **Inline chips** in ChatText — `#name` / `#[[name]](tag:id|action_tag:id)` (actietag = accent, free tag = muted), docs/app links as icon pills, optional status pills, mention chips with initials. (B) **Showcase blocks** — EntityRow cards from `attach_items` or proposal `items` (navigate when no open proposal; choose option when a proposal is open). Tags are primarily chips, not showcase cards, unless the agent attaches a tag showcase on purpose.
- **Proposal / showcase items:** `services/proposal_items.py` resolves refs into snapshots (MAX 10). Types: conversation, trash_entry, trigger, file, image (remote https URL), user, agent, tag, flow, project, contact, integration (alias connection), marketplace, module, help_doc (aliases docs/article), message (alias quote). `attach_items` stashes items for the turn; `turn_persist` writes them onto the last bubble without raising a decision. `_create_policy_decision` still derives refs from gated tool input.
- **Web search (2026-10):** Builtin tool `web_search` calls Brave Search (`BRAVE_SEARCH_API_KEY`). `kind=web|images`, ungated, category workspace; on personal-assistant allowlist and suggest-mode tools. Activity stores a slim result (host/favicon/title/url ×3) so the 480-char compact does not drop icons. UI: animated Globe while running, then site favicon + hostname (`chargecars.nl`, no scheme); host also inferred from `site:` / bare domain in the query before results land. Agents showcase image hits via `attach_items` type `image` + url.
- **Ask paste / vision (2026-10):** Composer clipboard paste of images uploads like the paperclip; Ask/stream passes `attachments` through `bokitoStreamMessage` into `AgentLoop` multimodal messages (current chat models — no separate vision model).
- **`list_tags`:** returns free tags and action tags (name, id, kind, description, project/flow) so agents stop inventing a single actietag; prefer `#name` in chat after listing. The Tenant snapshot also lists tags (with kind) and tells agents not to infer tags from `search_index` / thread text — that mistake produced “only #klacht exists” while the Communication rail already showed `#storing`.
- **`propose_action` / `create_decision_request`:** `question`, `action` + `payload`, `items`, `selection: single|multiple` (default single), optional labels/options. Multi-select: UI toggles picks then **Confirm** sends `option_ids`; stored as comma-joined `chosen_option_id` plus `chosen_option_ids` in payloads. Options may set `item_ref` or match item id so showcase click chooses that option. Soft choices work with `options` and no `action` — but destructive work must set `action` to a real tool so Approve executes. When `action` (or an executable option `action_type`) is set, the approve option carries `learn` for Next time. CHAT_STYLE: chips + `attach_items` to show; `propose_action` only when asking; never dump UUIDs as prose. Live turn shows *Preparing proposal...* when activity carries `proposes` and does not also show the idle Thinking brain.
- **`delete_tag` (2026-10):** Gated messaging tool deletes a workspace tag by `id` or `name` via `signal_tags.delete_tag`. Prefer `propose_action(action=delete_tag, payload={name})`. On personal-assistant allowlist.
- **Continue after decision (2026-10):** On assistant / internal / agent_session / personal chats, resolving Ja/Nee posts the operator bubble. When Approve ran a real tool (`delete_tag`, …), the thread gets a short deterministic confirm (e.g. `#name is verwijderd.`) — no LLM wake. Soft Yes (no tool) wakes the agent only when it is the **sole** open proposal; if other cards are still awaiting, Soft Yes gets the same short confirm (`Gedaan: …` / `Oké.`) so the agent does not stack new asks. Reject gets a short “Oké” confirm (with `#tag blijft staan` when a delete was declined). Rapid soft wakes coalesce (~1.25s). Soft Ask-box “Ja”/“Nee” (also “Ja graag”, “Nee dank”, or the exact button label) resolves when **exactly one** open card is a classic binary Ja/Nee — multi-select and text-input cards may stay open without blocking. Several binary Ja/Nee cards at once stay button-only.
- **Composer with open decision (2026-10):** Assistant/internal agent chats keep default mode **Ask** when a decision is open (soft “Ja” in the box); only threads owned by a team (`owner.kind=team`) default to Note. `turn.kind=team` (human turn / open question) must not flip the composer to Note. Recipient chip stays hidden while a decision is open.
- **Next time UI:** ProposalActions shows **Next time:** allow / always ask under the buttons when `learn` is present (same `teach()` path as the more menu). **You may do this yourself** while the card is still open also approves (runs the tool) then proposes the always-allow rule — it does not skip Ja.
- **Unknown `action_type` on approve:** Invented names (e.g. `remove_tag`) return 422 and reopen the card; only registered tools execute. Soft options omit `action_type`. Successful tool runs defer sibling open asks with the same action+payload on that thread.
- **Chat proposal chrome:** Agent asks with `question` / executable action render without heavy **Wacht op jouw OK** / Technisch chrome. Choice-list prose (`Kies hieronder` + bullets) is stripped from the bubble when buttons attach.
- Suggestion **Edit** / draft fill is suppressed while a session stream is active so the Agent tab is not yanked mid-chat.
- **Weak-spot residuals:** shared ARQ pool used from orchestration/coding enqueue; mobile Decisions tab redirects to Messages `awaiting_decision`; mailbox Routing button scrolls to Automations (InboxRule). Full `/email`→`/channels` collapse still outstanding.

#### Cockpit/profile operator walk (2026-08, less-techy pass)
- **Overview in Control rail (2026-09):** The daily scan (`/cockpit`, aliases `/overview` `/home`) is a primary **Control** rail item labeled **Overview**, above Messages — not under Settings. Settings no longer lists Reports/Rapportages. Per-user `default_landing` on `GET/PATCH /api/me/preferences` is `communication` (default) or `overview`; Profile **Start page** persists it (localStorage cache `bokito-default-landing`). After sign-in, `/` routes to Messages or Overview accordingly.
- **Cockpit Overview:** Subtitle greets by first name and shows today's date. A display name that starts with the product or organisation name (`Bokito Admin`) is skipped, so the greeting is just **Good morning** / **Goedemorgen**. Refresh shows last successful load time. **Agent freedom** shows **You decide** / **Ask first** / **Act first**. Empty AI-usage hides `US$ 0.00`. Placeholder website-visitor rows stay off Recent contacts. Custom **Your numbers** / **Jouw cijfers** section removed. Numbers use the in-app locale. Empty time-saved and handled-without-you cards explain when values appear. Customer-rating empty state opens website widget install. Partial-load failures name agenda / contacts / inbox in plain language. **Needs attention** offers **Open first**. A dismissed setup card can be restored from the subtitle.
- **Activity:** Source, detail, search, and jump-to-newest persist in the URL. The log groups **Today** / **Yesterday** / **Earlier**. Refresh shows last load time.
- **Notifications:** Pause all in-app alerts, restore recommended defaults, and preview a sample toast. Slack column header links to connect Slack. Fetch uses `APP_API_BASE`. The bell menu groups Today / Earlier, can mark one group read, and opens Messages when items exist.
- **Profile / My assistant:** Edit pencils stay visible. Avatar shows file-type hint. Password Enter submits; errors are i18n. Language notes it stays on this device. Assistant has templates, character count, unsaved guard, sticky save, Ctrl/Cmd+S, and a Talk CTA.
- **Members / palette / docs:** Invite validates email and submits on Enter. Promoting to owner confirms. Command palette adds invite, profile, notifications, talk-to-assistant, and My assistant. Learn and public Docs remember the last article. Public help search stays in `?q=`. Setup hide can be undone. Live connection says **Live updates paused** with Reload.

#### Settings operator walk (2026-08, more-techy pass)
- **Providers & models:** Models page is Bokito-first: managed card, gated custom wizard when entitled. Agent model picker follows `selectable_chat`.
- **Developers:** New tokens warn when scopes are empty (full access), offer presets (Read workspace / Ops + messaging / Full governed) plus All API / All tools, Copy Cursor MCP config / Claude Desktop / MCP curl / REST curl, and mount setup cards after create. Revoked tokens are hidden unless shown. Last-used is relative. Webhook URLs must be https (localhost http allowed). Copying a signing secret toasts.
- **Channels / Help center:** Channel routes can target one account, set priority, and pause without delete. Help center collections persist in `?collection=`, can be deleted, validate file URLs, and expose **Copy public link** (`/help/{slug}`).
- **Integrations / Usage / forms:** Marketplace `q` and `status` stay in the URL. Connected remembers last kind and can search. MCP table filters, copies endpoints, and confirms disconnect. Usage breakdowns accept `?days=7|30|90` and export CSV; clearing both caps confirms. Workspace, Branding, widget Look, and Inbox AI use the unsaved-changes guard.

#### Inbox operator walk (2026-08, techier pass)
- **List preview fidelity:** `GET /api/signals` already returned `last_message_preview` + `last_message_direction`. The Communication list goes through `listSignalThreads` → `normalizeSignalThread` in `signals-api.ts` (not `inbox-api` mapping). That normalizer must keep both fields or rows fall back to the subject. `_latest_message_previews` returns `(snippet, direction, by_agent)` and skips placeholder / `[mock]` agent lines. `last_message_by_agent` (an `author_agent_id` on that line) makes the row read **AI:** instead of **You:**/**Jij:**. The normalizers strip chat markers (`*bold*`, links) from the preview via `plainChatText`.
- **Resolved reply drafts** collapse to one line labelled by outcome: approved = **Draft approved and sent / Concept goedgekeurd en verstuurd**, rejected = **Draft rejected / Concept afgewezen**, otherwise **Earlier draft — dismissed / Eerder concept — terzijde gelegd**. Non-reply decisions show **Decision / Beslissing**. An edited composer draft is split into bubbles again on blank lines at approval.
- **Activity notes:** in mail mode earlier speech becomes an `other` item with `label: "note"`. List and stream payloads keep a note's `text` (max 280 chars, `slim_activity_item`) so a lone note renders as one static line; tool input/result and thinking text stay detail-only. Sub-second groups read **Thought briefly / Kort nagedacht** and **N actions / N acties** (no duration). A collapsed work group shows a provider logo only when every action used the same provider.
- **Tooltips and accessibility:** `NativeTitleTooltipBridge` moves `title` into `data-bokito-tip`; for unnamed icon-only controls it copies the title into `aria-label` (marked `data-bokito-tip-label`, kept in sync) so screen readers keep a name.
- **Needs reply:** client filter `threadNeedsReply` = open + (unread or last inbound). Chip **Needs reply** / **Jij aan zet** sits next to Unread/Pinned. Outbound previews prefix **You:** / **Jij:**.
- **Keyboard (Communication inbox):** `/` search, `J`/`K` next/prev, `E` close, `U` unread, `A` assign to me, `P` pin, `R` focus `#inbox-reply-composer`, `C` compose, `?` shortcut overlay, `Esc` clears/blurs search. Shortcuts are skipped while the compose modal is open or while typing in an input.
- **Assign to me:** `/auth/me` top-level `id` is already `user_numeric_id`. Nested `user` in the payload used to be a UUID string; it now also includes `numeric_id`. Dashboard `normalizeAuthUser` prefers `numeric_id` so Mine / assign / “your message” compare against the same numeric id as `GET /api/signals/members`. `AssigneeSelector` also matches the current user by email as a fallback.
- **Parked threads drop leftover drafts:** `PATCH` to `pending`/`closed`/`spam` defers open reply-suggestion decisions (`human_snoozed` / `human_closed` / `human_spam`), same as a human reply (`human_replied`).
- **Contacts:** profile header **Open conversation** jumps to the latest thread (primary CTA for widget visitors who cannot write email).
- **Browser tab:** AppShell sets `document.title` to the page name and prefixes `(N)` with `inboxUnread`.
- **New email compose:** From is always visible; Ctrl/Cmd+Enter sends; focus lands on the body when To is prefilled.

#### Inbox operator-tooling (2026-08, iteratie 1 completeness sprint)
- **Labels & prioriteit**: onder de thread-header staat een compacte meta-rij (`ThreadMetaRow` in `ThreadDetail.tsx`) met prioriteit-chip (Normal/High/Urgent), case/signaal-pills voor cases op dit gesprek (link naar `/cases`), optionele AI-triage, en rechts uitgelijnd de kanaal-agent in AI-paars (link naar agent). De agent-pil staat niet meer in de title-header of in het contactpaneel — de agent behandelt het kanaal/de thread, niet het contact. Alleen zichtbaar op externe (klant)threads, niet op interne agent-threads. Cases blijven ook in `ThreadCasesList` in het zijpaneel beheersbaar.
- **Labelfilter**: klikken op een labelchip in de threadlijst zet een server-side filter (`GET /api/signals?tag=`). Boven de lijst verschijnt een filter-pill met x om te wissen; het filter reset bij het wisselen van hub-leaf.
- **Notities bewerken/verwijderen**: interne notities (`kind="internal_note"`) tonen potlood/prullenbak-iconen in de header van de note-bubble. Bewerken gebeurt inline (textarea + Save/Cancel) via `PATCH /api/signals/{id}/notes/{message_id}`; verwijderen via DELETE met een confirm. Backend `update_note` synchroniseert ook `body_html` (notities worden met een `<p>`-mirror opgeslagen; zonder die sync bleef de timeline de oude HTML tonen).
- **Mark as unread vanuit thread detail**: envelop-icoon in de headertoolbar zet de open thread terug op ongelezen (`useThreadDetail.markUnread`), met toast en badge-refresh.
- **Bulkacties contextueel (2026-10):** de bulkbar toont alleen acties die bij de map passen (`lib/bulk-actions.ts`). In Open / Voor jou / Niet toegewezen / Alle: **Sluiten** (geen Heropenen). In Gesloten: **Heropenen** i.p.v. Sluiten. In Spam: **Geen spam** i.p.v. Markeer als spam. In Uitgesteld: Sluiten + Heropenen (wekt naar Open). Filter Vastgezet: **Losmaken** i.p.v. Vastzetten; filter Ongelezen: geen Markeer ongelezen. Sneltoets E sluit open threads en heropent gesloten/spam (zelfde als de thread-knop). Overige acties (Gelezen, Toewijzen, Bin) blijven; Bin vraagt twee keer bevestiging.
- **Sluiten blijft in de map (2026-10):** na Close / Send-and-close / spam in Open, Voor jou, Alle, … blijft de operator in die map; het gesprek verdwijnt uit de lijst en de volgende (of de lege map) opent. Communication springt niet meer naar de Gesloten-/Spam-map (`leaveResolvedThread` i.p.v. `inboxPath(dedicated)`).
- **Sync status-paneel** (historisch, verwijderd aug 2026): `SyncStatusPanel` toonde per mailbox status, laatste sync, fouten en per-map berichtaantallen. Vervangen door de checks op de kanaalrij (zie kanaalregister).
- **Mailbox-tabel (`/settings/channels`, 2026-08)** (historisch): Connected inboxes was een eigen tabel met kolommen Inbox/Sync/History/Status/Actions. Sinds het kanaalregister is dit één kanalenlijst met alle kanaaltypen als gelijke rijen.
- **Opgeruimd**: dode componenten `AutonomousProposalCard`, `RunStatusIndicator`, `AgentActivityLog` en `lib/messages-api.ts` zijn verwijderd (nergens geïmporteerd; het canonieke decision-pad is `DecisionRequestMessage` + `POST /signals/.../resolve`).

#### UI-polish en feedback (2026-08, iteratie 2 completeness sprint)
- **Humanized labels**: `lib/labels.ts` levert `humanizeLabel()` (snake_case → sentence case met overrides zoals `needs_auth` → "Needs sign-in") en `memberRoleLabel()`. Toegepast op alle plekken die rauwe API-enums toonden: SyncStatusPanel, WorkLogsTable, ContactsPage-statusbadges, AgendaPage-occurrencestatussen, Cockpit/Activity event kinds, MemberManagement/AcceptInvite/NewConversationPage-rollen, ProjectsSettings (`repo_index_status`, `autonomous_scope`), HelpCentersSettings (`index_status`), AgentContextPanel (agentstatus, threadstatussen).
- **AgentContextPanel**: "Open in Activity" heet nu "Open run" (de link ging altijd al naar runs); recente-conversatielinks gebruiken `inboxPath`/`agentRunsPath` i.p.v. legacy `/communication/agents|customers`-paden; en als een van de parallelle context-fetches faalt verschijnt een warning-banner met Retry i.p.v. stilzwijgend lege secties.
- **Onboarding company-stap**: de CTA linkt naar `/knowledge` (voltooiing wordt gemeten aan `company.md`-inhoud, niet aan branding); copy heet nu "Open company doc" / "Open bedrijfsdocument".
- **Personal agents**: `AgentChatAccessCard` wordt verborgen op personal-agentdetail (de chat-access API is company-only en gaf 404). `PATCH /workforce/agents/{id}/model` staat nu ook toe dat een gebruiker het model van de **eigen personal assistant** wijzigt (My Assistant-pagina); company agents blijven owner/admin-only.
- **Notificatie-instellingen**: de tabel toont naast In-app ook een **Email**-kolom (de backend ondersteunde e-mailnotificaties per categorie al via `notification_mail.py`); de ontbrekende "decisions"-rij is toegevoegd (werd voorheen door het frontend-filter weggegooid) en een korte "Saved."-bevestiging verschijnt na opslaan.
- **Members**: de Date-kolom toont de join-datum (backend `list_members` levert nu `joined_at` uit `Membership.created_at`); invite versturen geeft een success-toast.
- **Stille fouten opgelost**: profielveld-edits tonen een error-toast en blijven in editmodus bij een mislukte save; avatar-uploadfouten tonen een toast; taalvoorkeur-save meldt als de PATCH faalt; Knowledge-doc laden en zoeken tonen een toast bij fouten; contact aanmaken toont een success-toast.

#### Realtime inbox append (2026-08, coverage 2026-10)
- Gateway `message`/`thread` events dragen de canonieke REST-payloads (`serialize_thread` / `serialize_message` uit `signal_threads.py`), gesplitst per topic: `threads` krijgt de volledige thread-row + een lichte message-preview (geen bodies), `signal:{id}` krijgt het volledige bericht (html, attachments, decision-payload). `publish_signal_message` accepteert optioneel `decision=` zodat decision-cards zonder refetch renderen.
- **Publish contract:** na commit van iets dat operators zien, de matching helper in `app/gateway/publish.py` aanroepen. Matrix in `docs/architecture.md`. `email.send` en `set_read` publiceren expliciet; nieuwe `SignalMessage`-rijen krijgen een post-commit safety net in `entity_events.py` (dubbele publish is harmloos door id-upsert). Agent-beurten gaan via `TurnRecorder` → `publish_turn_event` zodra `AgentLoop` een `signal_id` heeft (inbound, chat, orchestration, workstream, trigger, stage checkup).
- **Redis:** productie weigert te starten zonder bereikbare niet-localhost `REDIS_URL` (gateway fanout + ARQ). Dev/tests blijven in-process.
- Elke envelope heeft `data.audience: "operator" | "all"`; `GatewayConnection.matches` laat operator-frames nooit bij widget-verbindingen aankomen. Interne notities, system events, `threads`-feed en thread-triage-updates zijn operator-only; visitor-zichtbare berichten blijven `all` (de widget leest `data.message.body_text` ongewijzigd).
- Dashboard: `useThreads` upsert de meegeleverde row direct in de lijst (`lib/thread-live.ts`). `useThreadDetail` appendt berichten met id-dedupe, quiet-refetch bij reconnect/tab-focus, en slaat turn-stream events over (die gaan naar `useSignalStream`). `NavBadgeContext` luistert op `threads`/`decisions`/`notifications`; `NotificationContext` refreshed op reconnect. ConnectionStatus blijft zichtbaar bij disconnect (reload-hint).
- Widget: `gatewayFrameToWidgetEvent` mapt `message`, `message.delta` → `streaming_delta`, en `agent.activity` → legacy `tool_started`/`tool_completed`/`tool_error` (geen aparte legacy tool-frames meer vanaf de API).
- Guardrail: `tests/test_publish_guardrail.py` faalt als een app-module `SignalMessage(` bouwt zonder publish-helper. Payload/audience-tests in `tests/test_gateway.py`.

#### Zelflerende inbox-regels (2026-08)
- **Concept**: het platform leert per afzender wat er met automatische mail moet gebeuren uit herhaalde operatorkeuzes op de "No reply needed"-kaart. Model: `InboxRule` (`models/learning.py`, tabel `inbox_rules`) met `match_type` (`sender` | `domain` | `list_id`), genormaliseerde `match_value`, `action` (`auto_close` | `auto_task` | `mute_ai`), `status` (`suggested` | `active` | `paused`), `source` (`learned` | `manual`), plus tellers `observations` (leerteller) en `hit_count`/`last_hit_at` (toepassingen). Service: `services/inbox_rules.py`.
- **Leren**: bij het approven van optie `close` of `create_task` op een action-suggestion-decision registreert `resolve_message_decision` (`signal_threads.py`) een `Feedback`-rij (`comment="no_reply_action:<optie>"`) en telt `inbox_rules.record_outcome` de keuze op een `suggested` regel voor de afzender. Drempel: 3 consistente keuzes (`PROMOTION_THRESHOLD`). Een afwijkende keuze reset de teller naar de nieuwe actie; `keep_open` leert niets; gepauzeerde regels worden niet opnieuw gesuggereerd. De resolve-response bevat `rule_suggestion` (serialized rule + `ready_to_activate` + `auto_promoted`).
- **Bevestigen**: bij `ready_to_activate` toont `DecisionRequestMessage` inline een "Always do this"-prompt (i18n `decisionCard.rulePrompt.*`); bevestigen doet `PATCH /api/signals/rules/{id} {status:"active"}`. Onder autonomy posture `autonomous` promoveert de regel zichzelf bij de drempel (toast meldt dit); `manual`/`assisted` vragen altijd bevestiging.
- **Toepassen**: `process_inbound_signal` (workers/tasks.py) checkt vóór de automated-mail-classificatie en vóór elke agent-run `inbox_rules.find_matching_rule` (prioriteit: sender > list_id > domain, alleen `active`). Match → `apply_rule_to_signal`: `auto_close` sluit de thread (unread weg, gateway thread-update), `auto_task` maakt een `AgentTask` (`trigger_type="inbox_rule"`, auto_start uit, thread blijft open — pariteit met de handmatige keuze), `mute_ai` slaat AI over en laat de thread ongelezen voor het team. Elke toepassing schrijft een `rule_applied` SignalEvent (timeline-pill "Auto-closed by rule (afzender)" e.d., AI-accentstijl) + `AuditEvent` (`inbox_rule:apply:<actie>`) en verhoogt `hit_count`.
- **Beheer**: CRUD op `/api/signals/rules` (list/create/patch/delete, routes in `app.routes.ts`); Inbox Settings heeft een "Automation rules"-kaart (`AutomationRulesManager.tsx`) met status (Active / Paused / Learning n/3), hit-teller, pauzeren/activeren, verwijderen en handmatig aanmaken (sender/domein/list-id). Handmatige regels zijn direct actief.
- **Hygiëne (2026-08)**: mailbox-sync stempelt `ai_live_since` in `settings_json` bij de eerste sync (`ensure_ai_live_since`); backfill-mail ouder dan die cutoff (`is_backfill_message`, 5 min grace) wordt wel opgeslagen maar triggert géén agent-run. Geautomatiseerde afzenders (no-reply/nieuwsbrief/bounce via `classify_automated_email`) krijgen geen CRM-`Contact`-rij meer bij ingest (`channels/base.py`); bestaande contacten blijven matchen zodat blokkeren blijft werken.
- Tests: `apps/api/tests/test_inbox_rules.py` (matching, leren/promotie, CRUD, pipeline-acties, backfill, contact-onderdrukking) en `apps/dashboard/src/lib/inbox-rules.test.ts` (normalizers).

#### Two-way agent chat in interne threads (2026-06)
- Een reply in een **interne agent-thread** (`channel="internal"`) via `POST /api/signals/{id}/reply` triggert nu de thread-agent: de backend draait `AgentLoop.run_chat` en voegt het antwoord toe als `agent_message`. Gated op `channel="internal"`, `direction="outbound"` (geen interne notities) en `not signal.ai_paused`. Externe kanalen (email/whatsapp/widget) en assistant-threads (afgehandeld via `/api/chat`) zijn uitgesloten. De agent-run is best-effort: een LLM-fout verliest nooit het bericht van de gebruiker.
- De geopende thread ververst live via het gateway-topic `signal:{id}` (`useThreadDetail`), zodat het agent-antwoord zonder handmatige refresh verschijnt.
- Mock-modus geeft een canned reply terug; live-modus vereist een tenant-BYOK of platform-sleutel.

---

### 2.9 Organisatieconfiguratie (`/settings/company-config`)
Scrollbaar instellingenformulier met sticky opslaan-balk.

**Secties:**
1. **Bedrijfscontext**: Naam, sector, website, beschrijving, doelgroep, tone of voice (6 opties), merkwaarden (tag-input), extra agent-instructies
2. **Huisstijl & Branding**:
   - Logo upload (drag & drop, SVG/PNG/WebP)
   - Primaire kleur + achtergrondkleur pickers (live hex)
   - Font selectors (display + body, 9 opties elk) met live preview
   - **StyleScanner**: URL invullen → scan detecteert kleuren en fonts van de website → "Huisstijl overnemen"
3. **Contactinformatie**: Telefoon, support e-mail, openingstijden
4. **Agent persona**: Agentnaam, begroetingsbericht, persoonlijkheidsbeschrijving

---

### 2.10 Sidebar Navigatiestructuur

#### Communication-rail met aanpasbare secties (juni 2026; vereenvoudigd juli 2026)
De binnenrail van de Communication-hub gebruikt een Intercom-achtig sectiemodel (`MessagesHubNav` + `SidebarPrefsContext` + `SidebarCustomizeDialog`):

- **Vaste top:** `+ New chat` in de rail-header, links van customize (geen dubbele Communicatie-titel; page-guide zit naast de topbar-titel). Daaronder de **Inbox**-sectie (**Alle communicatie**) met queues All / Mine / Open / Unassigned / Closed (`/communication/inbox/:queue`). Inbox toont klantkanalen plus assistant/agent-chats die de operator mag zien (backend folder `inbox`; view `all` sluit closed/spam uit). Interne agent-runs blijven onder Activiteit (`folder=internal`).
- **Aanpasbare secties** (`ALL_SECTIONS` = `agents | channels | settings`; volgorde/verbergen/inklappen in localStorage `communication-sidebar-prefs`; legacy `assistant` in opgeslagen prefs wordt genegeerd bij normalize):
  - **Agents** — company agents (`/communication/agent/:agentId` met dezelfde sub-queues), plus één **Activity**-leaf (`/communication/runs/all`). Updates / Results zijn quick-filter chips; open beslissingen zitten in de map **Beslissingen**. Geen persoonlijke-assistent-pin meer.
  - **Channels** — e-mail per mailbox (`/communication/channel/email/:connectionId`), Webchat→`widget`, Internal chat→`internal`; WhatsApp/Slack alleen bij bestaand ChannelAccount. Het voormalige "Agent messages"-leaf is verwijderd (duplicaat van Activity).
  - **Settings** — vast geanchord onderaan de rail (niet meesleepbaar in customize; show/collapse wel); link-outs naar `/settings/channels` en `/settings/assistant`.
- **Dode surface (roadmap):** view/label preset-leaves in `messages-paths` (`/communication/view/:key`, `/communication/label/:key`) zijn niet meer in de rail; routes kunnen nog bestaan maar zijn geen primaire nav.
- **Customize-dialog:** tandwiel in de rail-header opent een dialog met drag-to-reorder (dnd-kit), Show- en Collapse-toggles per sectie en een reset-knop. Sectieheaders in de rail zijn zelf ook inklapbaar (chevron, gepersist).
- **Leaf-dispatch:** `leafFromPath` (in `messages-paths.ts`) bepaalt de actieve leaf; `Communication.tsx` rendert één shell voor inbox / decisions / runs / channel / agent (assistant-threads via `ThreadDetail` → `AgentChatView`).
- **Backend:** `list_threads` ondersteunt `channel`-filter, folder `inbox` (klant + zichtbare assistant), view `all` en tag-filter (JSON-LIKE op `tags_json`). Owners/admins zien álle actieve company agents in `GET /api/chat/targets` ongeacht `chat_access` (`allowed_company_agents(..., is_admin=True)`); members alleen `everyone`/`selected`-agents.
- **Routes/redirects:** default landing is Messages (`/communication/inbox/…` via last-inbox path) unless the user set Profile **Start page** to Overview (`/cockpit`). Preference: `default_landing` = `communication` | `overview`. Legacy redirects: `/communication/direct/my` → `/communication/assistant`, `/communication/direct/agent/:id` → `/communication/agent/:id`, `/communication/customers/:queue` → `/communication/inbox/:queue` (my→mine, all→open; awaiting-decision → runs), `/communication/customers/ch/:id/:queue` → `/communication/channel/email/:id`, `/communication/agents/:queue` → `/communication/runs/:queue`.

#### Communication-hub + company agent chat (juni 2026; personal assistants removed aug 2026)
Product noun is **Messages** again (rail title, palette group, settings “Messages AI” / “Email & messages”); URL paths remain `/communication/*` and the API entity stays `Signal`. Entry module: `pages/MessagesHub.tsx` re-exports the Communication controller (agent chats included). **Personal assistants (`kind=personal`) are removed:** startup deactivates legacy personal rows; no provisioning on signup/invite; no `/settings/assistant` or `GET/PATCH /api/me/assistant`. `GET /api/chat/targets` lists permitted company agents only (`default_agent_id` may be null). `POST /api/chat/conversations` requires `agent_id` (400 "Choose which agent to talk to"); 409 "No agents available for user" when the member has no chat-permitted company agents. New chat UI shows the same empty state. Bring-in-agent candidates are channel → project → company only.

- **Agent kinds (current):** chat targets are company agents the user may message. Legacy `kind=personal` rows may still appear in history display filters; workforce nav continues to hide them.
- **Company agent chat access:** `Agent.chat_access` = `everyone` | `selected` | `nobody`; allowlist in `agent_chat_users`. Manage on agent detail → `GET/PATCH /api/workforce/agents/:id/chat-access`.
- **Chat targeting:** `GET /api/chat/targets` returns permitted **company** agents only; `default_agent_id` may be null. `POST /api/chat/conversations` requires choosing a company agent; with none available the API returns 409 "No agents available for user". Optional user default: `/api/me/chat-default`.
- **New conversation:** `/communication/new` is composer-first with a To picker (company agents + people/email). User must choose an agent; `default_agent_id` may preselect when present in the list. Empty targets show **No agents available** / **Geen agents beschikbaar**. Send navigates to `/communication/agent/:agentId/t/:id`.
- **Hub nav:** Agents section lists company agents only (no personal pin). Legacy `/communication/assistant*` and `/settings/assistant` redirect to `/communication/new`.
- **Thread-context (rechterpaneel):** `AgentThreadPanel` toont per gesprekspartner de juiste kaart — Contact-tab voor externe threads, "Agent"-tab met de target-agent (via `Signal.agent_id`) voor interne/agent-threads; Orchestration blijft als tweede tab.
- **AI-chat-feel:** thread-timeline en reply-composer zijn gecentreerd op max-breedte (zoals de assistant-chat), met een rounded composer-card, Enter-to-send en een inline send-knop; notitie-modus blijft geel gemarkeerd.
- **Channel-aware composer (Intercom-model):** `resolveComposerSurface()` in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/message-composer.ts` kiest het reply-kanaal op basis van `Signal.channel` + ontvanger — **E-mail** (met handtekening + "Aan"-rij) voor `email` + contactadres, **Bericht/Chat** voor `internal`/`assistant`/`widget`/`chat` (geen handtekening), **WhatsApp** / **Slack** wanneer het kanaal dat is. Interne notities blijven altijd beschikbaar als aparte tab. Handtekening wordt alleen bij `format: email` toegevoegd in `useThreadDetail`.
- **Agent-thread labeling:** interne threads tonen in de lijst de **agent-naam** (`agent_name` of project-orchestrator) i.p.v. generiek "Agent"; `get_thread` en `list_threads` resolven de agent via `signal.agent_id`, bericht-`author_agent_id`, of `project.po_agent_id`. Rechterpaneel Agent-tab toont rol, status, activiteit en projectlink.

#### Complete messaging product (juni 2026)
Canonical model blijft `Signal` / `SignalMessage` (`/api/signals`) plus assistant via `/api/chat` — geen parallelle inbox-stacks.

- **Streaming:** `AgentLoop.stream_chat` roept echte `llm.stream_chat` aan (Anthropic + OpenAI). Gateway op topic `signal:{id}` publiceert `agent.turn` (`{stream_id, phase}`), `agent.activity` (`{item}` — think/work/other, upsert op `id`), `agent.thinking` (reasoning deltas), `message.delta` (token-tekst met `segment_id`; nieuw segment = nieuwe bubbel) en `message` (final, per bubbel). Dashboard (`useSignalStream` → `LiveTurn`) en mobiel (`apps/mobile/src/hooks/useSignalStream.ts`) gebruiken hetzelfde contract. Assistant/internal chat redeneert natively (Anthropic extended thinking; Mistral Medium 3.5 / Small 4 via `extra_body={"reasoning_effort":"high"}`, stream-`content` als lijst met `thinking`-chunks; Large 4 heeft geen reasoning). Per beurt beslist `services/agent/reasoning_policy.turn_thinking_budget`: een eigen `agent.thinking_budget` redeneert altijd; de globale `chat_thinking_budget` (alleen met `enable_chat_thinking`) alleen als het bericht erom vraagt (bijlage, 160+ tekens / 24+ woorden, 2+ vragen of regels, code/link, EN/NL taakwoorden zoals waarom/maak/leg uit); op slug `bokito-kong` geldt altijd minimaal 4096 (alleen effectief als de backing reasoning ondersteunt; Large 4 niet). Reasoning wordt een `think`-item in `metadata.activity` en toont als **Nagedacht voor Xs** alleen als er tekst is: lege/whitespace-deltas maken geen item, `thinking_payload()` geeft `None` zonder tekst, slim list-items dragen `has_text`, en `isVisibleActivity` (dashboard) verbergt think-items zonder tekst. Live is de regel **Nadenken** zelf al uitklapbaar en streamt de redenering mee (`LiveThinkLine` in `ActivityTrail.tsx`). Zie "Agent-beurt: bubbels + activiteitsgroepen" in 2.12.
- **Assistant reliability:** `POST /api/chat/conversations/{id}/messages` en `/stream` vangen LLM-fouten af; bij failure wordt een zichtbaar agent-foutbericht opgeslagen zodat user-berichten nooit verweesd raken. Responses bevatten `llm_configured`, `llm_mode`, `llm_key_source`; `ai_paused` wordt expliciet afgehandeld.
- **Attachments / file hosting (aug 2026):** `app/services/storage.py` has `local` and `s3` (Cloudflare R2-compatible). Canonical attachment URLs always stay on auth-gated `GET /api/uploads/files/{tenant_id}/{filename}` — the bucket is private; API and worker `fetch()` from the same backend. Serve accepts Bearer access tokens, widget `session_token` (query or Bearer), or the refresh cookie. Widget image tags append `?session_token=` for cross-origin loads. Caps: 10MB (`POST /api/uploads`, livechat images-only, knowledge originals). Email inbound/sync also uses this store. Avatars stay as base64 on `User.avatar_url`. `boto3` is a main API dependency. Deploy upserts `STORAGE_S3_*` from GitHub secrets into `.env.staging`/`.env.prod`. Local fallback: Docker volume `bokito_uploads` mounted on both api and worker at `/app/data/uploads` so deploys no longer wipe files and the worker can see API writes. R2 (EU jurisdiction bucket `bokito-uploads`) is the production target; the existing Cloudflare DNS token cannot create R2 resources — enable with `STORAGE_BACKEND=s3` plus `STORAGE_S3_BUCKET/ENDPOINT/ACCESS_KEY/SECRET_KEY`.
- **Email:** outbound ondersteunt HTML body, CC/BCC, handtekening uit account settings, en `In-Reply-To`/`References` threading via laatste inbound `external_id`. Inbound sync bewaart `body_html` en attachment-metadata (Gmail MIME + Graph). **Signed body in timeline (aug 2026):** `deliver_outbound` / `send_via_provider` return the final HTML after signature append (`OutboundDelivery.body_html`); all store paths (`reply_to_thread`, compose `/email/send`, `send_reply` tool, auto-reply, scheduled flush) persist that HTML on `SignalMessage.body_html` so the thread bubble matches what was sent. Older messages stored only the unsigned draft remain without signature until resent.
- **From display name follows send_as (2026-08):** `send_as` was signature-only; the mailbox From stayed on the wire. Now `resolve_from_display_name` (signatures.py) sets the visible From name to the user or agent while the address stays `ChannelAccount.address`. Wired through `deliver_outbound` → `format_outbound` / `send_via_provider` for Gmail (`Name <addr>`), Bokito/Resend, and Outlook Graph best-effort `from.emailAddress.name`. Stored on outbound message metadata as `from_display_name`. Tests: `tests/test_reply_identity.py`.
- - **Composer dictation (2026-09):** Web Speech dictation (`useSpeechDictation` + `DictationMicButton`) on Messages Reply/Internal/agent tabs, AgentChatView, and the Write-assist **What should this say?** field. Live interim text is shown so auto-grow follows speech; listening UI matches the chat widget (bordered idle control, green glow + shared SVG wave bars, check on hover). Commit/dedupe (`appendSpeechChunk`, `consumeSpeechResults`) and wave geometry live in `@bokito/shared` and are used by both the dashboard and `apps/chat-widget`. Finals commit once per result index; stop/confirm ignores trailing finals. Click toggles start; hold-to-talk confirms on release (≥280ms). Chat max height 360px. Widget prefers Web Speech → textarea when SpeechRecognition exists; MediaRecorder+transcribe remains fallback. Composer max-height 200px in the widget.
**Reply identity + clean drafts (aug 2026):** voorgestelde antwoorden zijn puur klantgericht. `services/suggestion_format.split_suggestion()` splitst rauwe LLM-output in draft-body en interne notitie … **Send-as + handtekening:** in dezelfde agent-bubbel (gedeelde ChatBubble-chrome), direct onder de body; selector Jij/Agent + preview. **Per agent (2026-08):** `settings_json.reply_send_as` (`user` = impersoneer goedkeurende collega, `agent` = mail als zichzelf; default `agent`) en `email_signature_text` (platte tekst → HTML bij verzenden; legacy `email_signature_html` wordt nog gelezen). Bij `send_as=agent` voegt `signatures.with_agent_disclaimer` altijd een subtiele regel toe: “Beantwoord door een AI-agent · Powered by Bokito AI” met link naar https://bokito.ai (EN/NL). Zonder custom tekst: `compose_default_signature_html` + disclaimer. Precedence op de decision card: operator localStorage → agent `reply_send_as` → tenant `reply_send_as` → `user`. Tenant-default blijft via AI modes (`/settings/communication`). UI: `AgentSignatureCard` op agent-detail.
- **Decisions in threads:** `serialize_message` verrijkt decision-berichten met `payload.decision.options` uit `DecisionRequest`. `GET /api/notifications/decisions` bevat `signal_id` voor thread-linking. Notes: `GET/PATCH/DELETE /api/signals/{id}/notes[/{message_id}]`.
- **Mobile parity:** Expo app gebruikt SSE streaming voor assistant, react-query + gateway invalidation, volledige inbox-filters/paginatie/badge counts, thread composer (reply/note, send_and_close/pending, attachments), thread ops (pin/close/takeover/mark unread/delete), email HTML via WebView, conversation list + agent selection.

#### OpenClaw Control UI shell + hybride Messages-hub (juni 2026; paden hieronder zijn sindsdien hernoemd naar `/communication/*`)
De dashboard-shell is volledig herbouwd naar OpenClaw's Control UI-model (`apps/dashboard/src/components/shell/`, navigatiemodel in `src/lib/navigation.ts`). Chat en Inbox zijn daarna samengevoegd tot één hybride Messages-hub op `/inbox`:

- **Chat-first:** `/` redirect naar `/inbox/chat` (assistant-chat met SSE-streaming, inline decision cards); conversaties op `/inbox/chat/:conversationId`. Workspace-hub blijft op `/` voor multi-workspace accounts zonder tenant-subdomein.
- **Messages-hub (`MessagesHub` + `MessagesHubNav`):** full-bleed hub met drie spaces op "met wie praat je": **Assistant** (`/inbox/chat`, New chat + recente sessies), **Customers** (`/inbox/customers/:queue` — Awaiting decision, Open, Mine, Unassigned, Pinned, Pending, Closed + per-kanaal views op `/inbox/customers/ch/:channelId/:queue`; folder = external) en **Agents** (`/inbox/agents/:queue` — All, Updates, Results, Decisions (`awaiting-decision`); folder = internal). Aparte Chat- en Sessions-navitems bestaan niet meer.
- **Gedeelde thread-view:** één `ThreadDetail` met channel-aware composer (zie hierboven); interne agent-threads krijgen een **Bericht**-tab (geen e-mailhandtekening) plus **Notitie** en optioneel **Ask assistant** (prefill naar `/communication/new`).
- **Decision-threads zijn internal-channel signals**: agent-decisions verschijnen in de Agents-space (`/inbox/agents/awaiting-decision`), niet in de Customers-space; decisions op externe klantthreads verschijnen wel in `/inbox/customers/awaiting-decision`.
- **CRM:** `Contact`-model heeft `company`, `title`, `phone`, `notes`. Contactpaneel in thread-context als tab naast Orchestration (`AgentThreadPanel`): contactkaart, bewerkbare notities, approve/block, eerdere conversaties — geen projectkoppeling of snooze-presets (die blijven in thread-toolbar / agent-context). Contacts-pagina op `/contacts` (zoeken, lijst met kanaal/bedrijf/laatst gezien/#threads) en contactdetail op `/contacts/:contactId`. De lijst vult `last_seen` en thread counts vanuit Communication wanneer de CRM-velden leeg zijn (`enrichContactsFromThreads`: e-mail/id, unieke echte namen — geen generieke websitebezoekers). Avatars gebruiken het domein-favicon (`DomainFavicon` via Google S2, initials als fallback) op de contactenlijst, company-lijst, contactdetail, ContactPanel en threadlijst; de kanaalkolom toont een Lucide-icoon (`ChannelLabel`: e-mail, widget, Slack, WhatsApp, intern). Endpoints: `GET/PATCH /api/channels/contacts/:id`, `GET /api/channels/contacts/:id/threads`; signal thread-payload bevat `contact_id`.
- **Agenda = planning + conversation look-ats (2026-09):** `GET /api/agenda` shows planned trigger expansions, completed one-shots (`once`/`event` via `last_run_at`), run history for real triggers (`AgentRun.trigger_id` must point at a `Trigger`), external calendar blocks, and conversation next look-ats (`Signal.follow_up_at`, `source: "follow_up"`). It never merges `AgentTask` ledger rows. On-demand runs (email suggestions, chat, widget) belong on the activity timeline. `AgentRun` records are finalized everywhere; startup repair `_close_stale_agent_runs` closes runs stuck `running` for more than 6 hours. Cockpit Activity (`/activity`, formerly `/cockpit/activity`) keeps the horizontal timeline strip.
- **Agenda:** `/agenda` is the schedule surface. Timeline and week views of planned agent wakes, conversation look-ats, and calendar events (`GET /api/agenda?from=&to=&agent_id=`). Filters: agent, source (`all|wakes|calendar`), and kind. `once` means a one-off agent wake (not a human follow-up). Schedule opens the trigger dialog for `once`, `event`, `cron`, `interval`, `heartbeat`, `webhook`.
- **Agenda + externe kalenders (2026-08):** Google Calendar and Outlook Calendar are aparte marketplace-providers (`google_calendar` / `outlook_calendar`, capability `calendar`) met eigen OAuth-scopes (geen stille mail-scope-uitbreiding). Events landen in `calendar_events` (niet als `Trigger`). `GET /api/agenda` merged kalenderitems (`kind=calendar`) wanneer niet op één agent gefilterd. API: `GET/POST /api/calendars/connections`, sync, create/`PATCH`/delete events. Worker sync elke 15 min. Agent tools: `calendar_list_events` (stabiele event-ids), `calendar_propose_event` (decision `calendar_create_event`), `calendar_propose_update` (decision `calendar_update_event`). Agenda UI: connect-strip, Sync, Calendar block, detail met Edit/Delete, filter Wakes/Calendar. `IntegrationConnection.provider` may store the catalog slug or the catalog UUID; `canonical_provider_slug` maps both so Google Calendar lists under Agenda (not Apps with a UUID heading) and still syncs.
- **Calendar connect path (2026-10):** Operators connect Google Calendar / Outlook Calendar only under **Connections** (marketplace `kind=calendar`). Agenda rail empty state: overlapping Google + Outlook `BrandMark`s, title **Koppel je eigen agenda's** / **Connect your calendars**, info tip with the longer explanation, link **Open Koppelingen**; sync via `POST /api/calendars/sync`, CRUD via `/api/calendars/events`. Agenda no longer starts OAuth itself; Integrations OAuth callback still triggers calendar sync for `google_calendar` / `outlook_calendar`.
- **Sidebar-groepen:** Control (Overview `/overview`, Inbox `/inbox/chat`, Contacts `/contacts`, Activity `/activity`, Agenda `/agenda`, Usage `/usage`), Agent (Agents `/agents`, Skills `/skills`, Memory `/workspace`), Settings (`/settings/:section`). Footer: theme toggle, buildversie, gateway-verbindingsstatus.
- **Topbar:** breadcrumb `Bokito / <workspace> / <pagina>` met workspace-switcher, command palette (Ctrl+K), user-menu.
- **Overview-integratie:** naast stats en "Needs attention" toont Overview "Today on the agenda" (komende 24u) en "Recent contacts"; attention-items linken naar de juiste hub-space op basis van thread-folder. Agent-detail (`/agents/:agentId`) toont een "Upcoming on the agenda"-kaart plus knoppen naar `/agenda?agent=` en `/inbox/agents/all`.
- **Settings-secties:** Personal (profile, notifications, access-security), Workspace (general, branding, members), Channels (channels = email, assistant widget = `/ai/assistent/*`, communication agent, help-centers), Integrations (connected, marketplace, mcp), AI (`/settings/models` = Models, owner/admin), Autonomy (`/settings/autonomy` = voormalige GovernPage).
- **Models settings Bokito-first (2026-09, UX 2026-10 tiers):** `/settings/models` toont **Managed models** met drie kaarten (Maki / Bokito / Kong: icoon, EU-badge, lighter / standard / heavier copy, contextvenster + tools/vision uit catalog, vaste lijstprijs/Mtok uit env, ready-status; geen underliggende provider-/modelnamen) plus een **Your models**-lijst (add/remove, ook met model-icoon). Agent- en create-pickers tonen altijd `ModelOptionLabel` (icoon + naam). Vaste Bokito-prijzen via `BOKITO_*_COST_PER_MTOK_CENTS`; `TOKEN_MARKUP_MULTIPLIER` alleen voor niet-Bokito platform usage. Platform keys: env / `platform_secrets` (ops).
- **Per-tenant LLM providers (BYOK, gated):** when custom models are allowed and opted in, owners/admins connect `provider_connections` (`anthropic|openai|openai_compatible`, encrypted key, optional `base_url`) and `tenant_models`. Keys encrypted (Fernet); client sees `last4`. Test: `POST /api/settings/providers/{id}/test`. Provider/model CRUD returns 403 when not entitled. Legacy `/settings/llm-keys` removed; `scripts/migrate_tenant_models.py` remains for older rows.
- **Model resolution:** `resolve_model_call` uses tenant models only when `custom_models_active` (env + entitlement + opt-in). Otherwise platform catalog / Bokito backing. Platform-fallback key chain: platform key → env → mock (billable on platform key). Tenant BYOK is not billable through Bokito. `GET /api/settings/models` returns `managed`, `custom_models`, `selectable_chat`. Staff Ops lists `custom_models` per tenant.
- **Govern opgelost:** geen `/govern`-pagina meer; posture/allowances/audit onder Settings > Autonomy & approvals; per-agent tools & permissions als panel op agent-detail (`/agents/:agentId`); decisions uitsluitend inline in chat/inbox-threads + "Needs attention" op Overview.
- **Legacy redirects:** `/home`→`/overview`, `/govern`→`/settings/autonomy`, `/chat`+`/sessions`→`/inbox/chat`, `/c/:id`→`/inbox/chat/:id`, `/messages/*`→`/inbox/customers/*` (updates/results→`/inbox/agents/*`), `/triggers`+`/automations`+`/orchestra`→`/agenda`, `/os`→`/agents`, `/os/docs`→`/workspace`, `/communication`→`/inbox/customers/my`, `/integrations/*`→`/settings/*`, `/ai/communicatie`→`/settings/communication`.
- **Nav-badges:** alleen op Inbox (ongelezen) en Agents (attention) in de sidebar; queue-tellers in de hub-kolom.
- Run-detail leeft op `/agents/:agentId/runs/:workLogId`; project-specifieke run-URLs (`/project/:id/workforce/...`) bestaan niet meer.

#### Legacy rail-shell (tot juni 2026, vervangen)
De portal gebruikte een Featurebase-achtige 2-laagse shell. Zie ook `apps/dashboard/docs/NAVIGATION.md`. Onderstaande beschrijving is historisch:

- **Primary rail (admin, icon-only):** Home (`/home`) → **AI OS** (`/os`, Network-icoon; unified Projects + Workforce) → Inbox → Integraties → Data; **Instellingen** onderaan (`/settings/profile`). Bokito rail keeps Orchestra under the AI OS sidebar.
- **AI OS sidebar:** Canvas (`/os`), Agent library (`/os/agents`), Decisions (`/os/communication`), Workspace (`/workspace`, markdown docs), plus platform agents. Workstreams sidebar block on `/project/:id/*` only. Canvas uses React Flow (`AiOsCanvas.tsx`); add nodes via toolbar palette; connect by drawing between handles. V1 is config-first (edges stored; runtime enforcement is a follow-up). Legacy `/projects`, `/workforce`, `/ai/agents` redirect to `/os` paths.
- **AI OS canvas interactions:** Click a node for `NodeDetailPanel` (connections list, drill-in links, remove from canvas). Orchestrator panel includes workspace-wide `DecisionsInline`. Draw connections: workstream→orchestrator (`routed_by`), workstream→repo (`uses_repo`), workstream→tool (`uses_tool`).
- **Agent avatarstijl (dashboard):** Agents tonen `AiAvatar` (foto, Lucide-icoon of getinte initialen met rand+glow) in bibliotheek, detail en contextpanelen. Beheerders zetten naam + kind/icoon via **Bewerken** op agentdetail (`AgentIdentityDialog`); velden komen uit `settings_json` / `RuntimeAgent.avatar_*`.
- **Inbox uitgaande handtekening-afbeelding:** Thread-replies sturen alleen body-HTML; de API plakt de handtekening server-side (`resolve_signature_html` → mailbox fallback). Een optionele `user.signatureUrl` mag nog als afbeelding in de body, maar tenant-logo en `/bokito-logo.svg` worden nooit meer als fallback meegestuurd (dat zette ongevraagd het Bokito-merk onder elke mail).
- **Dynamische e-mailhandtekeningen (2026-10):** Custom HTML mag `{{name}}`, `{{company}}`, `{{function}}`/`{{job_title}}`, `{{email}}`, `{{phone}}`, `{{website}}`, `{{address}}`, `{{closing}}`, `{{avatar}}` bevatten; `render_signature_template` vult die bij verzenden vanuit de afzender (lege contactregels zoals `T:` verdwijnen). **Standaard is tekst-only** (afsluiting + naam/rol/contact met linker rand); SVG-/data-URI-avatars breken in veel mailclients. Foto is optioneel via template **Met profielfoto** / `{{avatar}}` (alleen publieke https-profielfoto). **Per mailbox:** `signature_source` = `mailbox` of `sender` + `signature_html`; UI onder **Kanalen**. Resolve user-send: mailbox-template → persoonlijk → tekstdefault. Agent: eigen handtekening + disclaimer. MailComposer: preview + link naar kanaal (`?edit=signature`). `SignatureEditor` Preview: **Voorbeeld als** / **Preview as** (jij, teamleden, agents, sample); default = ingelogde user, niet Jane Doe. Dashboard: `default-signature.ts`.
- **Mail-native reply UX (2026-10):** Elke ontvangen mailwolk in een e-mailthread draagt actieknoppen **Beantwoorden** (Reply), **Allen beantwoorden** (ReplyAll — alleen als `canReplyAll` meer ontvangers dan de eigen mailbox ziet) en **Doorsturen** (Forward). Klikken opent `MailComposer` onderin: groeit animerend naar ~2/3 van de thread (mini-threadweergave erboven), met To/CC/BCC-velden, bewerkbaar onderwerp (`Re:`/`Fwd:` via `replySubject`/`forwardSubject`), From-mailboxkeuze. Handtekening en citaat zitten in dezelfde brief-scroll als de tekst: de handtekening is niet afgekapt (`previewSignatureHtml`), de 3-puntjes klappen de geciteerde geschiedenis uit in die scroll (sandboxed iframe op inhoudshoogte, `buildQuotedHtml`, Outlook-stijl Van/Verzonden/Aan/Onderwerp, NL/EN). Verzenden gaat via `POST /signals/{id}/reply` met `to`, `mode` (reply|reply_all|forward), `source_message_id`, `subject`, `quoted_html`; de server plakt quote onder de handtekening en forwards onderdrukken provider-threading. **E-mailthreads openen op Reply/mail** (`resolveComposerSurface` defaultMode `reply`, niet Ask); een actieve meta-session mag dat niet overschrijven. De modusknop **E-mail** start Reply op de huidige/laatst-gebruikte mailbox; het chevron-menu kiest From en rebindt de thread (`PATCH … channel_account_id`, last-used in `last-mailbox.ts`). Recipient-logica in `apps/dashboard/src/lib/mail-reply.ts`. Outbound rows slaan `from_address`, `to_addresses` en `external_id` (provider message id; Outlook Graph geeft er geen bij verzenden — Sent-sync vult aan) op. Inline CID-afbeeldingen in de e-mailweergave krijgen het access token als query-parameter op eigen-host upload-URLs (`authorizeUploadUrls`), zodat de sandboxed iframe ze kan laden. `send_status` kent nu ook `scheduled` en `failed:{reason}` in de dashboard-normalisatie, zodat annuleer-knop en faalreden in de bubbel werken.
- **Mailconcepten (2026-10):** `MailComposer` autosaved zijn concept per conversatie in localStorage (`mail-draft-store.ts`, key `inbox.mailDraft.{threadId}`): body, To/CC/BCC, onderwerp, bijlagen en gekozen From-mailbox (debounced 400ms + flush bij unmount). Sluiten (Esc/X), threadwissel of herladen verliest niets. De chip **Mailconcept** verschijnt alleen op de Reply/mail-modus (nooit achter Ask); bij openen van de thread op Reply wordt een bestaand concept eenmalig hervat in MailComposer. **Verder schrijven** herbouwt de intent uit het bronbericht via `buildMailDraftIntent`. Verzenden wist het concept (bij een mislukte send blijft het staan); sluiten/spam van de conversatie ruimt het mee op via `clearStoredComposerDraft`. De geciteerde mailgeschiedenis wordt bewust niet opgeslagen (klein houden van localStorage, altijd verse quote).
- **Mailen altijd via de mailcomposer (2026-10):** Elke uitgaande e-mail loopt door dezelfde `MailComposer`. (1) Nieuw gesprek → **E-mail** embedt de composer als paginavariant (`variant='page'`, mode `new`, draftKey `new`): Aan-veld met contact- en teamlid-typeahead, CC/BCC, onderwerp, From-keuze in de kop, handtekening-preview, bijlagen, dicteren; versturen gaat via `POST /email/send` (From-adres wordt teruggemapt naar het numerieke connection-id en als voorkeur bewaard). Contact en Teamlid zijn geen aparte starttypes meer; `?intent=contact` en `?intent=teammate` openen nog dezelfde mail. (2) Op e-mailthreads start de ondercomposer op Reply/mail; Ask is een bewuste switch. AI-voorstellen landen in Reply (niet Ask). Focus in de kleine reply-tekst opent `MailComposer` met dezelfde body (`preferSeedBody` wint van een ouder mailconcept) plus de reply-intent van de nieuwste inkomende mail.
- **Nieuw gesprek is een kanaal, geen persoonstype (2026-10):** De kiezer op `/communication/new` toont **E-mail**, **Ticket** en **Agent**. **WhatsApp** verschijnt alleen als een ingeschakeld WhatsApp-kanaal kan versturen. WhatsApp (`POST /signals/start` kind `whatsapp`) stuurt het eerste bericht; één thread per nummer (`external_id` = wa_id), een bestaand gesprek krijgt het bericht erbij. **Ticket** (kind `ticket`) maakt een intern `Signal` (channel `internal`, eigenaar = de operator) en legt meteen `file_ticket` vast: actietag in een flow, project of Geen project wanneer de flow projecten heeft, optionele notitie en intakevelden. Deep links `?intent=agent` blijven de agentchat.
- **Gedeactiveerd lid ≠ teammate voor inbound (2026-10):** `find_member_by_email` / `member_emails_for_tenant` eisen `Membership.is_active` én `User.is_active`. Een gedeactiveerd lid (Team → Gedeactiveerd) telt niet meer als workspace-member: hun mail triggert weer interpretatie + conceptantwoord. Voorheen checkte de helper alleen `User.is_active`, waardoor `lorenzo_boers@outlook.com` (membership gedeactiveerd 2026-10-06, user nog actief) inbound AI bleef blokkeren met `workspace_member` terwijl de Team-UI hem niet meer onder Mensen toonde.
- **Mailcomposer-afwerking (2026-10):** `RecipientField` (in `MailComposer.tsx`) geeft To, CC en BCC dezelfde contact-typeahead: pijltjestoetsen + Enter/Tab kiezen, Escape sluit eerst de dropdown en pas daarna de composer; de composer haalt contacten zelf op via `listContacts` wanneer er geen `recipientSuggestions`-prop is (threads, forwards). Ongeldige invoer (geen e-mailadres) toont een rode regel onder de velden en blokkeert versturen; de entry die nog getypt wordt in het gefocuste veld telt niet mee voor de melding. Een kale thread-intent zonder bronbericht heet **Nieuwe e-mail** (niet Beantwoorden). De Nieuw-gesprek-kiezer toont een onverzonden `new`-concept als chip **Mailconcept** met **Verder schrijven**/weggooien. **Ongedaan maken** op de verstuur-toast (15s undo-venster) zet de mail terug als concept in `mail-draft-store`. MailComposer en decision-card laden de workspace-handtekening via `GET /api/settings/email-signature` voor de preview (zelfde template als bij send). Statuslabels op de groene zelf-bubble gebruiken `text-accent-fg` (Verstuurd) of een chip op `bg-bg-surface` (Niet bezorgd) voor contrast. De globale title-tooltipbridge verbergt zijn tooltip bij pointerdown en wanneer het gehoverde element uit de DOM verdwijnt (geen zwevende tooltips na verzenden). De persoonlijke assistent-FAB (`bokito-chat`) is verborgen op open threads (`/t/`) en `/communication/new`, zodat hij nooit verzendknoppen afdekt.
- **Landing tenant:** admins gaan naar `/home` (overzicht: recente projecten, inbox-links, agent runs); end-users behouden project-redirect (0/1/many). Op `/home` is er geen context-sidebar (alleen rail + hoofdinhoud).
- **Project hub (`/projects`):** context sidebar: **Overzicht**, **Communicatie** en **Workspace** (link naar `/workspace`); **Workstreams** (`ProjectHubBackgroundWorkersNav`) met compacte projectrijen: gekleurde dot + teller, status als gekleurde tekst onder de titel. Per-project canvas: `WorkerStatusStrip` boven `ProjectTabNav`; status afgeleid via `deriveWorkerStatus` uit berichten (`decision_request` = geblokkeerd, overige `awaiting_human` = aandacht), budget, orchestration, work logs en repo-index.
- **Centrale workspace-docs (Phase 4):** de oude block-based Blueprint UI (`WorkspaceDocNavContext`, `PageTree`, `BlockEditor`, `RevisionPanel`, scaffold/handbook seeding) en het FastAPI `workspace_doc_*` schema zijn verwijderd. Tenant-brede markdown-docs leven nu op `/workspace` (`WorkspaceDocs.tsx`) tegen `/api/workspace/*`.
