An accepted ticket enters the first open stage and starts the flow when it is enabled. Every stage move writes a `ticket_stage_changed` `SignalEvent` with `to_stage`, `to_key`, `to_kind`. The dashboard shows default stage names (key equals kind and the stored name is the English default) in the operator's language via `stageLabel` in `lib/tickets-api.ts`; the stage editor seeds those localized names, so a save stores them. Services: `services/tickets.py`; API: `/api/categories` (list with `project_choices` and `open|waiting|proposed|filed_7d|filed_prev_7d`, proposals excluded from filed counts; create, patch, policy, backlog), `GET/PUT/PATCH/DELETE /api/signals/{id}/ticket`, `GET /api/projects/{id}/board` (one board per playbook on the project). Agent tools (category `tickets`): `list_categories`, `get_ticket`, `file_ticket`, `update_ticket`. Composer verb `/ticket #name`.
- **Split on a new intent.** `services/conversation_split.py` `split_conversation` moves the messages from a chosen message onward into a new conversation with the same contact, channel, owner and hold (`_COPIED_FIELDS`). The new conversation keeps `parent_signal_id`; the old one points forward with `superseded_by_id`, and inbound matching follows that chain so replies on the same email thread or chat land in the newest conversation. Outbound keeps the original provider thread id. Triage on a later inbound message that reads a different category asks the conversation's AI handling: autonomous splits at once, assisted raises an inline Decision ("Split into new conversation"), manual does nothing. Operators always have **Split from here** on a message. Internal, assistant and team threads never split. Migration `072_conversation_split`.
- **Hashtags.** `signal_tag_links (signal_id, tag_id)` on the `SignalTag` registry (migration `073_signal_tag_links`). Names are normalized lower case, max 40 characters (`services/signal_tags.py` `normalize_tag`) and always shown with `#` (`HashtagMark`: accent `#` for a category, muted for a free hashtag). Operators can create one inline; it joins the registry. Agents (`set_thread_tags`) and inbox rules may only use registered hashtags. Inbox rule action `tag` ("Add tags") adds hashtags and lets the normal flow continue. `SignalTag.pinned` gives a free hashtag a Communication rail row; `show_in_nav` does the same for a category. `POST /api/signals/tags/{id}/promote` attaches a new or existing playbook ("Make category"). Module packages ship hashtag templates (`/api/integrations/modules/{slug}/tag-templates`). Managed on Settings → **# Tags** (`TagRegistrySection`).
- **Communication rail: Tags and categories, Projects.** `InboxFolder` and saved folders are gone (migration 075). `GET /api/signals/nav` (`services/communication_nav.py`) returns teams, `ticket_tags` (categories with `show_in_nav`), `tags` (pinned free hashtags) and projects, each with an open count inside the hub scope and the caller's channel visibility. Rail rows route to `/communication/tag/:tag/{queue}` and `/communication/project/:id/{queue}` with the shared sub-views For you, Open, Unassigned, Closed; `hub-list-filters.ts` maps them to `tag` / `project_id` list filters. Settings → Channels → **Tags and Communication** (`CommunicationTagsCard`) sets the default sub-view per row. Sidebar prefs key `communication-sidebar-prefs-v5`.
- **SQLite dev schema.** The dev DB (`apps/api/dev.db`) is not on Alembic. `db/sqlite_converge.py` `converge_sqlite_schema` mirrors the Alembic revisions that move data or drop columns (073 tags to links, 074 drop `show_as_folder`, 075 workstream projects and cases to conversation tickets); add a step there whenever a revision does more than add columns.
- Product help: `ai/categories.md` (Categories and tickets; screenshots `categories/catalog`, `categories/ticket-panel`), `ai/workstreams.md` (stages, hashtags and projects; `workstreams/links`), `ai/projects.md` (playbook boards; `projects/boards`), `inbox/communication.md` (hashtags, rail rows; `communication/hashtags`), `inbox/channels.md` (Tags and Communication; `channels/communication-tags`), `getting-started/cockpit.md`, `developers/mcp-endpoint.md` (tickets tools). App endpoints (`/signals/{id}/ticket`, `/signals/nav`, `/categories`) are not public API; they are documented through docstrings and `response_model`.

## 48. Lessons from the first production tenant (October 2026)

Derived from a production workspace that ran for weeks with one owner, an unanswered check-in and a mailbox in error. Each item removes a way the platform could waste a team's attention; the tenant configuration itself is applied per workspace, not in code.

- **Decision hygiene.** `services/signal_decisions.py` `find_open_duplicate_decision(session, tenant_id, title=...)` dedupes signal-less decisions tenant-wide (same title, still `awaiting_human`), not only per thread; `recently_declined_decision(..., within_days)` backs the proposal cooldown. `tools/builtin.py` `PROPOSAL_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 7`: `recommend_module` / `suggest_integration` / signal-less `create_decision_request` return a note instead of a card when the same title was declined or dismissed in the last 7 days. `create_decision_request` without a `signal_id` must carry an executable option (`RESOLVER_ACTIONS`, `approve`, or a free-text option) or it returns `{"ok": False, "code": "no_executable_option"}`; the agent writes the question in its channel instead. New `thread_subject` argument: `resolve_signal_by_subject` finds the newest open/pending/snoozed conversation whose subject matches (`ilike`), so a question about a conversation lands on that thread.
- **Bulk dismiss.** `GET /api/notifications/decisions/groups` groups open decisions by title (`count`, `without_thread`, `latest_at`; platform-change decisions excluded). `POST /api/notifications/decisions/dismiss` `{title?, without_thread_only?, older_than_days?}` (422 without a filter) sets `status=deferred`, `chosen_option_id="dismissed"`, marks notifications read and publishes each decision; nothing executes and a dismissal counts as a decline for cooldowns. Dashboard: `DecisionGroupsBanner` above the Decisions list (groups with two or more cards, **Dismiss all**), `lib/decision-groups-api.ts`, `ThreadList` `banner` prop.
- **Check-in hygiene.** `PLATFORM_CHECKIN_INTERVAL_MINUTES = 24 * 60` (was hourly). `services/triggers.py` passes the previous heartbeat report into the prompt (`previous_heartbeat_report`), suppresses a report whose normalized similarity to the previous one is at or above `HEARTBEAT_SIMILARITY_SUPPRESS = 0.85` (`is_repeat_heartbeat_report`), and tells the agent to pass `thread_subject` when a question concerns a conversation. The module line is softer: suggest at most one module and only with a concrete reason from the conversations.
- **Module MCP routing.** `tools/modules.py` `route_module_mcp_call(tool_input)` maps `call_mcp_tool` aimed at an installed module's server onto the module's own `{slug}_{verb}` tool, so the agent reads module data through the registry (category `integrations`, non-mutating) instead of a raw MCP call that fails. `workers/tasks.py` `assisted_tool_allowed(name)` lets Assisted runs use `SUGGEST_MODE_TOOLS` plus ungated non-mutating module tools; the assisted prompt lists them as `module_hint`.
- **Mailbox auto-pause.** `services/email_sync.py` `SYNC_AUTO_PAUSE_AFTER = 50`: after 50 consecutive sync errors the account is disabled with `settings.auto_paused_at` / `auto_paused_reason`, one ops alert is raised (`paused=True`) and the channel row reads **Paused** with reason `sync_errors` (`channel_registry.py`). A successful sync or **Resume** calls `clear_sync_pause` and resets `sync_error_count`. Alerts also fire on `auth_expired` and from the third consecutive error.
- **Workspace LLM block (circuit breaker).** `services/run_errors.py`: `Tenant.settings_json["llm_block"] = {kind, error, since, until, deferred_signal_ids}` (`LLM_BLOCK_SETTINGS_KEY`), `LLM_BLOCK_BACKOFF_MINUTES = 60` (rate_limit uses 5), `LLM_BLOCK_MAX_DEFERRED = 500`. `open_workspace_block` is called when a run fails with a workspace-wide provider error (`workspace_block(exc)`: credits, invalid key, rate limit / 429 code 1300); `active_workspace_block` is checked before `process_inbound_signal` and `fire_trigger` run. Blocked inbound signals get a `SignalEvent("agent_deferred")` and are appended to `deferred_signal_ids`; blocked triggers set `last_status = "blocked:{kind}"` and reschedule. The first successful `run_chat` calls `workers/tasks.py` `release_workspace_block`, which clears the block and re-enqueues every deferred signal. `record_run_error` always writes `error` and `error_code` into `AgentRun.result_json`, so a failed run is never empty.
- **LLM rate-limit retry (2026-10):** `services/agent/llm.py` `with_rate_limit_retry` retries OpenAI-compatible and Anthropic chat (and OpenAI stream open) up to 4 times on 429 / `rate_limited` / platform code 1300, honouring `Retry-After` when present. Interactive chat that still fails shows a clear rate-limit line instead of the generic assistant error (`signal_chat._agent_error_message`). Seen in prod when Support agent (`bokito-ai-3-1`) hit the platform gateway under load.
- **Provider failover (2026-10):** `services/provider_health.py` parks a (api key, model) pair for 15 min on a zero request quota (`x-ratelimit-limit-req-minute: 0`) or a plan refusal (403 "not available in your subscription tier"), 2 min after a soft 429 that survived one retry (`with_rate_limit_retry` attempts=2). `AgentLoop._llm_chat` / `_llm_stream` then re-resolve and continue the same turn on the next chain step (up to 3 steps; streams only before the first event). Background callers (interpretation, assistant threads) pick the next step on their next resolution. Limits are per model: one Mistral key can serve ministral while mistral-medium is blocked. Bokito backing resolve batches catalog rows and memoizes platform keys per provider; inbound triage loads only the newest message (`get_triage_context`) and builds category hint lines from the already-fetched category list.
- **Hot-path batching (2026-10):** Agent category map loads playbook projects once via `project_choices_by_workstream`. Channel routing batches agent team memberships (`agent_principals` / `agents_that_can_handle`) and skips the query when channel access is the default All agents. Inbound worker shares `_inbound_preflight` between interpret and reply (one message load + classify + member check). Communication `needs_decision` / `awaiting_decision` use correlated `_exists_open_decision` instead of materializing every open-decision signal id. Token markup falls back to a process-local cache after the first DB read.
- **Hot-path batching 2 (2026-10):** Inbox `find_matching_rule` / `find_tag_rules` load all key matches in one query (specificity still ordered in Python). Workspace approved-examples load messages with one `IN` query. AgentLoop memoizes Tenant + team reachability and reuses `_session_user_role` in operator context. Triage loads `registry_rows` once for categories, free-tag hints, and the auto-tag allow-set. Tenant Host middleware caches slug→id for 60s (`clear_tenant_slug_cache` for tests/rename).
- **Mistral account (prod):** organization "Bokito", Default Workspace, pay-as-you-go with auto recharge (€20 when the balance drops below €10). The platform key (`...kmHJ`, stored in `platform_secrets` and `MISTRAL_API_KEY`) belongs to it. Right after the first credit purchase (2026-10-07) the key still had free-tier limits (0 req/min on small/medium, 403 on large) for a few hours; paid limits then applied by themselves. Paid limits per minute: medium 1000, large-4 1000, small 100, large-latest 15, ministral-3b 3000. A 429 with `x-ratelimit-limit-req-minute: 0` therefore means the account/plan, not load.
- **Claude 5+ thinking:** Anthropic models from major version 5 reject `thinking.type.enabled` + `budget_tokens`; `AnthropicLLMProvider._create_kwargs` sends `thinking={"type":"adaptive"}` + `output_config.effort` (low ≤2048, medium ≤8192, else high). Older ids that answer with that 400 are learned at runtime and retried once.
- **Live thinking row:** `AgentTurnLive` takes the working agent's avatar (28px, same as the bubbles) instead of the generic AI mark, and the "Thinking" line is 28px high so it centers on it. `.thinking-shimmer-text` must sit on its own element: sharing one with `.activity-live-label` let the later `animation` rule cancel the shimmer.
- **Inbox rule cards.** `services/inbox_rules.py` `normalize_match_value(match_type, raw)` (sender → address, domain → bare host, list_id → raw) is used by `suggest_rule` / `create_rule`; the `suggest_inbox_rule` tool validates `match_type`, `action` and the value and returns hints on bad input. `raise_rule_decision(session, tenant_id, rule_payload, signal_id=...)` turns a draft rule into an inline decision titled `"{Action label} mail from {label}?"` with options **Activate** (`action_type="activate_inbox_rule"`, payload `rule_id`, resolved in `services/notifications.py` via `update_rule(status="active")`) and **Later** (defer); it respects the 7-day decline cooldown, the tenant-wide duplicate check and supersedes an older card on the same thread. `maybe_suggest_bulk_sender_rule(..., threshold=BULK_SENDER_THRESHOLD = 3)` runs after `acknowledge_automated_mail` and proposes an `auto_close` sender rule once three distinct email conversations from the same sender carry a `no_reply_noted` event.
- **Archive automated mail.** Per-mailbox `ChannelAccount.settings_json["archive_automated_mail"]` (`ARCHIVE_AUTOMATED_MAIL_KEY`; `ChannelRow.archive_automated_mail`, `PATCH /api/channels/accounts/{id}` `archive_automated_mail`). When on, `services/inbound_agent.py` `archive_automated_mail_if_enabled` closes the thread on arrival, runs `on_status_change` and adds the `#automated` tag (`AUTOMATED_MAIL_TAG`); the `no_reply_noted` event carries `archived: true` and `acknowledge_automated_mail` returns `delivery: "archived"`. Off keeps the no-reply card. Dashboard: `MailboxSettings` switch **Archive automated mail** (`nav:channelsPage.archiveAutomated`).
- **Onboarding brief and lead chat access.** `ONBOARDING_SYSTEM_PROMPT` step 3 **Brief**: the front desk agent calls `update_agent` on itself with a description and system prompt derived from the first conversation (lands in Govern as a draft), then asks one decision and plans the check-in. The seeded front desk ships with a default `description`. `ensure_front_desk` / `ensure_front_desks` set `chat_access="everyone"` for lead company agents still on `"nobody"`; `services/projects.py` `create_po_agent` creates project leads with `chat_access="everyone"` and a description.
- **Flows always have stages.** `POST /api/workstreams` without `stages` stores `services/tickets.py` `default_stages_json()` (Open / Waiting / Done) instead of `[]`; `PATCH` with an empty list or no done stage returns 400 (`validate_stages`). Startup `ensure_flow_stages(session)` (called from `db/session.py` `init_db`) backfills existing non-deleted flows that have no valid stage of their own (`has_explicit_stages`). The flow editor disables **Save** while the draft is invalid.
- **Recommended tenant configuration (apply per workspace, not in code):** invite the whole team as members and set lead agents to chat access Everyone; keep one front desk plus one specialist agent per domain with a one-line description each; connect the primary mailbox with **Archive automated mail** on and activate `auto_close` rules for recurring bulk senders; give every active action tag a flow with real stages and an owner per stage; keep the check-in daily and dismiss stale module proposals in bulk; load the company brief, services and pricing into Knowledge before turning on Assisted replies.
- Product help: `inbox/channels.md` (auto-pause, archive automated mail), `inbox/inbox-ai.md` (archive, bulk-sender rule card), `ai/decisions.md` (bulk dismiss, executable options, `thread_subject`), `ai/agents.md` (front desk brief, lead chat access), `ai/workstreams.md` (default stages), `govern/models.md` (provider refusal block), `getting-started/setup-guide.md` and `quickstart.md` (daily check-in).
### Tenant apply: bourgondienadvies (2026-10-06)

Applied plan section 2 on prod tenant `bourgondienadvies` after go-ahead. Lead renamed to **Kantoor AI** with accountancy brief; **Harry AI** widget brief + Assisted; Outlook Assisted + archive automated mail + default agent Kantoor AI; invites for `sandra@` and `inge.steenbeek@`; Harold promoted to admin; Casper deactivated; company/persona/memory/heartbeat docs rewritten; flows `#btw-aangifte` `#ib-aangifte` `#vpb-aangifte` `#jaarrekening` `#inkoopfactuur` `#belastingdienst` `#klantvraag`; projects Aangiften 2025 + Kantoor; bulk auto-close/mute rules; 179 noisy decisions dismissed; 34 Banking threads closed; posture Assisted; monthly budget EUR 50; check-in daily and paused. Outlook still needs a manual reconnect for the stale sync error. Moneybird was already absent.
